To address the high variance and instability common in discrete sequence optimization, we adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a stable on-policy algorithm that smooths reward signals and improves training robustness. This framework enables LLMol to effectively handle a range of molecular design tasks, including single-property targeting (e.g., penalized logP, QED) and structure-constrained optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMol consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving higher success rates and improved efficiency across diverse molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [Probabilistic Physics-Aware Machine Learning Predictions of Electric Truck Energy Consumption with Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19054)
*Hannes Nilsson,Rafael Basso,Balázs Kulcsár,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将第一性原理物理模型融入数据驱动方法，构建了考虑车辆运行能量损失的物理感知模型。基于该模型的贝叶斯线性回归相比标准线性回归提高了能耗预测可靠性；更复杂的机器学习模型（神经网络、梯度提升回归树）结合相同物理模型后显著提升了预测精度。此外，还开发了预测标准差形式的不确定性估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法缺乏物理先验知识，导致能耗预测的可靠性和精度不足。作者希望将第一性原理物理模型融入数据驱动方法，以提升预测的物理一致性和准确性。

Method: 构建了考虑车辆运行中不同能量损耗源的物理感知模型；分别基于该物理模型采用贝叶斯线性回归、神经网络和梯度提升回归树进行能耗预测；并开发了预测标准差形式的不确定性估计框架。

Result: 物理感知的贝叶斯线性回归相比标准线性回归提升了能耗预测可靠性；物理感知的神经网络和梯度提升回归树相比标准版本显著提升了预测精度；所有模型均能合理估计预测不确定性。

Conclusion: 将物理先验知识融入数据驱动方法能够同时提升能耗点预测的可靠性和精度，且复杂模型受益更明显；所提出的不确定性估计框架可有效量化预测置信度。

Abstract: In this work, we incorporate first principle physics into the construction of data-driven methods by considering a model that accounts for the different sources of energy losses during vehicle operations. Our results show that Bayesian linear regression based on this physics-aware model can improve the reliability of the expected energy consumption, as compared with standard linear regression. Further, it is shown that more complex machine learning models such as neural networks and gradient boosted regression trees, based on the same physical model, can further improve the accuracy in energy forecasting and significantly outperform standard versions of the same machine learning models. In addition to point predictions of the energy consumption, we develop a framework for estimating the corresponding uncertainty in the form of predicted standard deviation. Our results show that all of the models learn to estimate the uncertainty reasonably well.

</details>


### [94] [Where Should Optimizer State Live? Tiered State Allocation for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19058)
*Nuemaan Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkewAdam通过为MoE模型的不同参数群体（主干、专家、路由器）分配差异化优化器状态（动量+因式二阶矩、仅因式二阶矩、精确二阶矩），将优化器状态从50.6GB压缩至1.29GB（仅为AdamW的2.6%），峰值训练内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB，同时在困惑度和路由器负载均衡上优于AdamW、Muon和Lion等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE训练中优化器状态（AdamW的一阶和二阶动量）占据最大单内存开销（6.78B参数模型需50.6GB存储状态，而权重仅12.6GB）。观察到MoE的三个参数群体（密集主干、专家、路由器）在规模和梯度统计上存在显著差异，不应共享相同的优化器状态，从而设计差异化状态分配以大幅减少内存占用。

Method: SkewAdam为不同参数层分配差异化优化器状态：密集主干（占参数5%）使用float32动量加因式分解二阶矩；专家（占参数95%）仅使用因式分解二阶矩；路由器（<0.01%）使用精确二阶矩。总状态仅1.29GB，为AdamW的2.6%。

Result: 峰值训练内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB（适应40GB加速器）；验证困惑度108.4，优于AdamW（126.8）、Muon（120.2）和Lion（393.7）；路由器负载均衡在均匀下限1%以内；消融实验表明分层分配本身不贡献精度，精度来自动量保留；调参后AdamW最佳118.5，Adafactor 139.7，差距缩小但未消除。

Conclusion: 优化器状态的存储位置（为不同参数层分配何种状态）至少与状态总量同等重要——分层内存节省策略可在不牺牲精度的前提下实现大幅内存压缩，而保持动量是精度的关键。

Abstract: Optimizer state is the largest single line item in the memory budget of mixture-of-experts (MoE) training: on a 6.78B-parameter MoE language model, AdamW keeps 50.6 GB of first and second moments to update 12.6 GB of bfloat16 weights. We study SkewAdam, an optimizer built on the observation that the three parameter populations of an MoE - the dense backbone, the experts, and the router - differ enough in size and gradient statistics that they should not receive the same state. SkewAdam keeps float32 momentum plus a factored second moment for the backbone (5% of parameters), a factored second moment alone for the experts (95%), and an exact second moment for the router (<0.01%). The resulting state occupies 1.29 GB, 2.6% of AdamW's, and peak training memory falls from 81.4 GB to 31.3 GB, within the budget of a 40 GB accelerator. In a controlled comparison from identical initializations over 82M tokens, SkewAdam reaches validation perplexity 108.4, ahead of AdamW (126.8), Muon (120.2), and Lion (393.7), and settles router load balance to within 1% of its uniform floor. The allocation is not what earns that perplexity: a tier ablation matches it with twenty times the state, and Adafactor, which shares the factored estimator but drops momentum, plateaus 40 points behind. The tiers buy memory at no cost to accuracy; the accuracy comes from keeping momentum, which a uniform optimizer shares too. Sweeping the baselines' learning rates narrows but does not close the gap: the best tuned AdamW reaches 118.5, tuned Adafactor 139.7. Where optimizer state lives, these results suggest, matters at least as much as how much of it there is.

</details>


### [95] [Deep learning-based prediction of time-resolved adhesive forces in viscoelastic Hertzian contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19060)
*Ali Maghami,Merten Stender,Michele Ciavarella,Antonio Papangelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文训练了一个基于LSTM的序列到序列深度学习模型，用于快速预测粘弹性软接触的完整力响应轨迹。通过引入固定测量步长（FMS）表示法处理异构时间尺度，模型在加载/卸载速率跨越四个数量级、Tabor参数0.2-3.2的数据集上训练，实现了保持均方误差5.0×10^{-4}、中位拉脱力误差约2.2%、中位迟滞误差约1.1%，中位推理时间仅0.16秒，为实时控制应用提供了快速替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软粘弹性接触的完整时间分辨力轨迹预测需要全数值模拟，其计算成本强烈依赖于参数，无法用于实时应用或设计优化循环，亟需一种快速替代方法。

Method: 采用标量条件的状态化序列到序列深度学习模型；引入固定测量步长（FMS）表示法将变长轨迹转换为定长序列；比较了LSTM、时间卷积网络（TCN）和时间分布稠密层三种架构，以及三种不同Tabor参数调节机制。

Result: 最佳模型为带拼接条件化的LSTM架构，在保留数据集上均方误差5.0×10^{-4}，中位拉脱力误差约2.2%，中位迟滞误差约1.1%，中位推理时间0.16秒。

Conclusion: 该模型能够跨未见参数组合和解析极限情况进行测试，为重复数值评估提供快速代理，在面向控制的软机器人抓取与操作任务中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Fast prediction of the response of adhesive soft viscoelastic contacts represents a current challenge in soft robotics and for gripping and manipulation tasks. Determining the complete time-resolved force trajectory requires full numerical simulations, whose computational cost is strongly parameter-dependent, making them impractical for real-time application or design-optimization loops. In this work, we overcome this limitation by training a scalar-conditioned, stateful, sequence-to-sequence deep learning model to predict the full force evolution from a prescribed displacement history for both short- and long-range adhesion regimes. The data set spans four orders of magnitude in loading and unloading rates and includes varied dwell times, with the Tabor parameter ranging from $0.2$ to $3.2$. To enable learning across these heterogeneous time scales, we introduce a fixed-measurement-step (FMS) representation that converts variable-length trajectories into fixed-length sequences while preserving their physical-time information. Different architectures were trained, including long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, temporal convolutional neural (TCN) networks, and time-distributed dense layers with three different Tabor-conditioning mechanisms. The models were compared using global waveform and error metrics. We found that the best-performing model has an LSTM architecture with concatenated conditioning, which achieves a held-out mean-squared error of $5.0\times10^{-4}$, a median pull-off-force error of $\approx2.2\%$, and a median hysteresis error of $\approx1.1\%$. For the held-out protocols, the model predicts a complete force trajectory with a median inference time of $0.16$ s. The model is tested across unseen parameter combinations and against analytical limiting cases, providing a rapid surrogate for repeated numerical evaluations with potential use in control-oriented applications.

</details>


### [96] [GEqTrain: A Configuration-Driven Framework for Retargeting Equivariant Graph Neural Networks Across 3D Scientific Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19083)
*Daniele Angioletti,Marco Nobile,Vittorio Limongelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEqTrain是一个基于配置驱动的等变图神经网络框架，通过解耦数据集语义、模型组合和训练目标，使得同一软件栈可通过配置变更处理多种不同科学任务；同时提出GEqDiff扩展，支持等变流匹配下的联合生成建模（包括笛卡尔坐标和非标量节点场）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变图神经网络实现通常与特定任务、输出格式和训练范式紧密耦合，导致难以复用和迁移到新任务上，缺乏统一且灵活的建模基础设施。

Method: 采用配置驱动（Hydra）架构，将原始数据映射为类型化的节点/边/图级场；通过配置声明式组装模型堆栈、损失函数和训练流程；GEqDiff基于等变流匹配，将用户定义的等变场作为一等公民生成目标，在单一等变流中联合传输笛卡尔位置和最高至l=3的非标量节点场。

Result: 在粗粒化到全原子逆向映射、分子固体NMR化学位移预测和等变生成建模三个不同任务上展现出竞争性精度；在基于蛋白质二级结构基元的合成基准上验证了异构变换性质场的高保真联合重建。

Conclusion: 共享的等变表示和训练基础设施能够以配置变更的代价，在跨不同任务上达到与专用系统相当的精度；GEqTrain提高了等变建模的可复现性、可扩展性和可重用性。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks provide a powerful modeling language for three-dimensional scientific data, but their reuse is often limited by implementations tied to specific tasks, outputs, and training regimes. We present GEqTrain, a configuration-driven framework that separates dataset semantics, model composition, and training objectives. Raw data are mapped to typed node-, edge-, and graph-level fields, while model stacks, losses, and training workflows are assembled declaratively through Hydra configurations. A shared equivariant backbone and training infrastructure can therefore be retargeted to a new task primarily through configuration. We demonstrate this flexibility on three different problems handled within one software stack: coarse-grained-to-atomistic backmapping of biomolecular systems, prediction of NMR chemical shifts in molecular solids, and equivariant generative modeling. Our aim is not to surpass individually optimized task-specific systems, but to show that a shared representation and training infrastructure can achieve competitive accuracy across qualitatively different tasks at the cost of a configuration change. We further introduce GEqDiff, a generative extension based on equivariant flow matching. GEqDiff treats user-defined equivariant fields as first-class generation targets, jointly transporting Cartesian positions and non-scalar node fields spanning representations up to l=3 within a single equivariant flow. We validate this capability on a controlled synthetic benchmark inspired by protein secondary-structure motifs, showing that fields with heterogeneous transformation properties can be reconstructed jointly and with high fidelity. By reducing the software overhead of moving between predictive and generative, scalar and tensorial settings, GEqTrain aims to make equivariant modeling more reproducible, extensible, and reusable.

</details>


### [97] [An unsupervised clustering analysis of breast cancer data derived from electronic health records enhanced through UMAP dimensionality reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19089)
*Davide Chicco,Nicoletta Benvenuto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究先使用DBSCAN对三个乳腺癌患者电子病历数据集进行聚类，然后引入UMAP降维预处理，通过三种统计指标验证了UMAP+DBSCAN组合方法在电子病历聚类分析中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是全球约800万女性罹患的常见癌症，电子病历中的患者数据蕴含潜在的医学模式，但传统医学观察可能遗漏数据趋势，因此需要利用无监督聚类方法自动发现具有医学意义的患者分组。

Method: 对三个独立的乳腺癌患者电子病历数据集，先单独应用DBSCAN密度聚类方法，再进行UMAP降维预处理后执行DBSCAN聚类，最后使用DBCV、DCSI和DISCO三个统计指标评估聚类效果。

Result: UMAP与DBSCAN的组合方法在电子病历数据聚类中表现出有效性，优于单独使用DBSCAN的效果。

Conclusion: UMAP降维结合DBSCAN聚类的方法适用于电子病历数据的分组分析，所识别出的患者群组具有医学可解释性，为临床解读提供了基础。

Abstract: Breast cancer is one of the most widespread types of cancer, affecting approximately 8 million women worldwide. Electronic health records of patients diagnosed with this disease can serve as valuable datasets for computational analyses, enabling the discovery of new insights about the pathology. Unsupervised clustering, in particular, can identify groups of patients with medically significant features, revealing data trends that might otherwise go unnoticed by medical doctors. In this study, we first applied the DBSCAN density-based clustering method to three independent datasets derived from electronic medical records of patients with mammary carcinoma. Subsequently, to enhance our results, we preceded the DBSCAN application with a dimensionality reduction phase using UMAP. We evaluated our clustering outcomes using three statistical indices (DBCV, DCSI, and DISCO). Our results confirm the effectiveness of combining UMAP with DBSCAN for clustering data derived from electronic health records, paving the way for the medical interpretation of the patient groups identified by our approach.

</details>


### [98] [Predicting Activities in Aqueous Electrolyte Solutions with Hybrid Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19114)
*Zeno Romero,Maximilian Kohns,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Bromley物理模型与矩阵补全机器学习方法相结合的混合模型Bromley-MCM，用于预测水溶液中电解质的活度系数和渗透系数。该模型在478种电解质实验数据上训练，最终能够预测83种阳离子和112种阴离子组成的9,296种电解质的Bromley参数，显著扩展了模型的预测范围并保持了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电解质活度模型（如Pitzer、Bromley）需要针对每种电解质拟合实验数据，无法预测未研究体系的性质。虽然存在一些预测方法，但通常适用范围有限且依赖于额外的离子特异性描述符。因此，需要一种能大规模预测未研究电解质活度性质的方法。

Method: 将Bromley物理模型与矩阵补全方法（MCM）结合。Bromley模型中的电解质特异性参数按阳离子为行、阴离子为列排列成矩阵，由于大量电解质缺乏实验数据，该矩阵稀疏，从而将参数预测转化为矩阵补全问题。模型以实验数据端到端训练。

Result: 在Dortmund Data Bank中478种电解质（298 K）的活度系数和渗透系数实验数据上训练，得到了包含83种阳离子和112种阴离子的完整Bromley参数矩阵，能够预测9,296种电解质的浓度依赖性活度性质。在排除训练的电解质上评估表明保持了高预测精度。

Conclusion: Bromley-MCM混合模型成功将Bromley模型的适用范围从有限种电解质扩展到9,296种，同时维持了高预测准确性，为未研究体系的电解质活度预测提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Activities in aqueous electrolyte solutions, usually described by ionic activity and osmotic coefficients, are important properties for modeling many processes in industry and nature. Established activity models, such as those of Pitzer or Bromley, require fitting to experimental data for each electrolyte of interest and thus cannot predict properties for unstudied systems. While some predictive approaches exist, they are typically limited in scope and rely on additional ion-specific descriptors. In this work, we introduce a new hybrid model that combines the physics-based Bromley model with a matrix completion method (MCM) from machine learning. The MCM is employed to predict the electrolyte-specific parameters of the Bromley model, exploiting the fact that these parameters can be arranged in a matrix with cations and anions as rows and columns, respectively. Due to the lack of experimental data for many electrolytes, the initial parameter matrix is sparsely populated, making the prediction of the Bromley parameters for unstudied electrolytes a matrix completion problem. The hybrid model, Bromley-MCM, was trained end-to-end on experimental data for mean ionic activity coefficients and osmotic coefficients of aqueous solutions of 478 electrolytes at 298 K from the Dortmund Data Bank. As output, we obtain a completed matrix of Bromley parameters for 83 cations and 112 anions, enabling consistent prediction of concentration-dependent activities in aqueous solutions of 9,296 electrolytes at 298~K. This substantially extends the applicability of the Bromley model while maintaining high predictive accuracy, as demonstrated through evaluations on electrolytes excluded from model training.

</details>


### [99] [Parallel Noising in Neural Markov Logic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19126)
*Peter Jung,Giuseppe Marra,Ondrej Kuzelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两项改进——使用图神经网络增强势函数的表达能力，以及提出基于并行回火MCMC的"并行加噪"训练推理算法——显著提升了神经马尔可夫逻辑网络（NMLNs）在图生成任务中的性能，使其在图生成上媲美基于扩散的生成模型，在小分子结构生成上匹配专用文本递归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前NMLNs作为生成模型在小规模关系结构上表现良好，但在大规模结构上不如基于扩散的图生成模型，需要弥补这一差距。

Method: (i) 使用图神经网络增强NMLNs势函数的表达能力；(ii) 提出受并行回火MCMC启发的"并行加噪"训练与推理算法。

Result: 增强后的NMLNs在图生成任务上达到与通用基于扩散的图生成模型相当的强性能，且在生成小分子结构时能够匹配专用文本递归模型的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的两项增强措施有效提升了NMLNs的生成能力，缩小了其与主流图生成模型之间的差距，并在分子生成领域展现出竞争力。

Abstract: Neural Markov Logic Networks (NMLNs) are a flexible neurosymbolic relational model. Previous work has shown that, although NMLNs achieve strong performance as generative models for small relational structures, they underperform diffusion-based generative graph models on larger structures. In this paper, we strengthen NMLNs along two main dimensions: (i) we increase the expressive capacity of their potential functions using graph neural networks, and (ii) we develop a new training and inference algorithm inspired by parallel-tempering Markov chain Monte Carlo methods, which we name parallel noising. Together, these enhancements enable NMLNs to attain strong performance in graph generation relative to general diffusion-based generative graph models. Furthermore, they allow NMLNs to match the performance of specialized text-based recurrent models when generating small molecular structures.

</details>


### [100] [One Model, Many Graphs: Learning over Attributed Graphs across Heterogeneous Modalities with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19128)
*Jiayi Yang,Yifang Chen,Yuanfu Sun,Jiajin Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMG-VLM是一个基于预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的统一框架，能够处理仅文本属性、仅图像属性以及两者兼具的多种异构模态图数据，通过结构感知图适配器整合邻域信息，在节点分类和链接预测任务上超越现有GNN和LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习方法针对固定模态模式设计，面对仅文本、仅图像或图文兼具的异质模态属性图时需要分别训练不同模型，缺乏统一框架，限制了跨图泛化和可扩展性。

Method: 利用预训练VLM作为共享骨干网络，设计结构感知图适配器（Structure-aware Graph Adapters），在保持与VLM原生嵌入空间兼容的同时整合邻域结构信息，实现单一模型同时处理文本属性图、图像属性图和多模态属性图。

Result: 在多种领域数据集上，OMG-VLM在节点分类和链接预测任务中一致优于现有最优的GNN和LLM基线方法，并展现出对未见图和新模态模式的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: OMG-VLM验证了VLM作为图数据通用骨干网络的可行性，为异构模态图学习提供了一种统一、可扩展且强泛化能力的范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a unified representation space for textual and visual information, yet their potential as general-purpose backbones for graph-structured data remains largely unexplored. In practice, attributed graphs exhibit substantial modality heterogeneity: some graphs contain only textual node attributes, others only visual attributes, while still others provide both. Existing graph learning approaches are typically designed for fixed modality schemas, requiring separate models for different settings and limiting scalability and cross-graph generalization. To bridge this gap, we present OMG-VLM (One Model, Many Graphs with Vision-Language Models), a unified framework for learning over attributed graphs across heterogeneous modality schemas. OMG-VLM leverages a pretrained VLM as a shared backbone and introduces structure-aware graph adapters that integrate neighborhood information while remaining compatible with the VLM's native embedding space. This design enables effective learning over text-attributed, image-attributed, and multi-attributed graphs within a single model. Extensive experiments across diverse domains show that OMG-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GNN- and LLM-based baselines on attributed graph learning tasks such as node classification and link prediction, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen graphs and varying modality schemas. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jo-eyang/OMG-VLM.

</details>


### [101] [Incomplete Observations Boost Evolutionary Performance in Ocean Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19147)
*Yangyang Kong,Yutong Jiang,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong,Feng Gao,Xiaopei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种生成式状态空间模型与优化框架，能够直接从稀疏含噪观测中学习海洋动力过程，在CMIP6模拟数据和FY-3D卫星数据上实现了高保真重建与准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动海洋模型严重依赖完整的再分析数据集，计算开销大且模型性能受限于训练数据质量，无法直接从稀疏、含噪的真实观测中学习。

Method: 构建连续状态空间的隐马尔可夫模型，将海洋物理量作为隐状态、测量值作为观测；初始状态和状态转移模块用神经网络实现，发射模块建模为掩码高斯分布；基于EM算法推导优化框架，交替执行Langevin动力学重建高保真海洋场和神经网络优化；假设海洋状态演化满足平稳遍历马尔可夫过程，仅采用长度为二的状态序列进行优化。

Result: 在CMIP6模拟数据和FY-3D卫星数据上，模型实现了高保真重建与准确预测，验证了稀疏观测可直接改进模型对海洋状态动力学的表征能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为下一代地球系统模型直接从稀疏、不完整的真实观测中学习提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Data-driven methods have revolutionized ocean modeling, yet current approaches rely heavily on complete reanalysis datasets, imposing computational constraints and limiting model performance to that of the training data. Here, we present a generative state-space model and an optimization framework that enable learning directly from sparse and noisy observations. The model is essentially a hidden Markov model with a continuous state space, where oceanic physical quantities are treated as hidden states and measurements as observations, enabling a unified representation of ocean fields and observational data. Both the initial-state and state-transition modules are implemented as neural networks to capture the complexity and temporal evolution of ocean states, while the emission module is formulated as a masked Gaussian distribution. To train the model from sparse observations, we derive an optimization framework based on the expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm. The framework alternately reconstructs high-fidelity ocean fields via Langevin dynamics and optimizes deep neural networks to capture temporal evolution. Theoretical analysis shows that the framework maximizes the likelihood of observations under the generative model. For efficiency, we assume that ocean-state evolution follows a stationary, ergodic, and Markovian stochastic process and adopt only length-two state sequences during optimization. Experiments on CMIP6 simulation data and FY-3D satellite data demonstrate high-fidelity reconstruction and accurate prediction, showing that sparse observations can directly improve the model's representation of ocean-state dynamics. This work offers a scalable pathway for next-generation Earth system models to learn directly from sparse, incomplete real-world observations.

</details>


### [102] [Breaking the Homogeneity Assumption: Specialized Multi-Generator Adversarial Learning for Rare Failure Detection in Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19153)
*Alexis Lazanas,Georgios Kampouropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预测性维护中故障数据高度不平衡且非均匀（不同故障模式呈多模态分布）的问题，提出了一种基于故障类型感知的多生成器GAN增强方法，通过在AI4I 2020数据集上的实验证明，该方法相比传统重采样和单生成器GAN能生成更真实的少数类样本，取得更高的PR-AUC和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不平衡处理方法（如欠采样、SMOTE、代价敏感学习）假设少数类样本是均匀分布的，无法有效处理工业故障数据中不同故障模式（多模态分布）的复杂非均匀情况，导致模型对罕见故障的识别能力受限。

Method: 提出一种故障类型感知的多生成器GAN架构，每个生成器独立学习一种故障子类型；采用泄漏安全（leakage-safe）的实验设计，与代价敏感学习、随机欠采样、SMOTE过采样、单生成器GAN增强四种方法进行对比；使用PR-AUC作为主要评估指标。

Result: 在AI4I 2020预测性维护数据集上，所提出的多生成器GAN框架相比传统重采样方法和单生成器GAN增强，生成了更真实的少数类样本，取得了更高的PR-AUC和召回率。

Conclusion: 故障类型感知的多生成器GAN增强方法能有效改善预测性维护系统中罕见故障的识别性能，优于传统假设少数类均匀分布的不平衡处理方法。

Abstract: Supervised learning models in the predictive maintenance field are regularly trained on highly imbalanced industrial datasets: machine failures occur rarely but have a disproportionate effect on operations. In addition to the clear class disparity, failure data are typically non-homogeneous, with different failure modes arising from distinct physical processes and exhibiting a multimodal distribution across minorities and classes. Traditional imbalance-management methods, e.g., undersampling, SMOTE-based interpolation, or cost-sensitive learning, typically assume that the minority population is homogeneous. This means their effectiveness is severely limited in the multifaceted conditions encountered in industrial practice. This paper determines the possibility of a failure-type-conscious generative augmentation program to improve the identification of infrequent failures in predictive maintenance systems. An experimental design that is leakage-safe is used to compare five imbalance-handling methods: cost-sensitive learning, random undersampling, SMOTE oversampling, single-generator GAN augmentation, and a specialized multi-generator GAN architecture that has independent generators that are asked to learn individual failure subtypes. Precision/Recall-oriented measures are used to quantify model performance; the main evaluation measure is the PR-AUC. Experiments conducted on the AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance dataset indicate that the proposed multi-generator GAN framework produces more realistic minority samples, yielding higher PR-AUC and recall scores compared to traditional resampling methods and individual-generator GAN augmentation.

</details>


### [103] [Neural Kolmogorov Equations: Parallelizable Learning of Stochastic Dynamics under General Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19173)
*Arthur Bizzi,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经Kolmogorov方程（NKEs），通过将神经SDE的学习问题从建模随机轨迹重新表述为建模概率密度演化，实现确定性、无限维的并行时间训练，支持Lévy型随机强迫，在耦合噪声和跳跃过程等基准上展现出竞争性的预测精度和更高的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经SDE方法假设噪声不耦合且连续，难以处理实际中的复杂随机驱动；同时时间维度上扩展性差，需要昂贵的自回归训练。

Method: 基于Kolmogorov前向方程将神经SDE重新表述为确定性无限维问题，通过拉格朗日Galerkin投影和算子分裂实现时间并行训练，学习一般Lévy型随机强迫的算子结构。

Result: 在多个随机基准（包括耦合噪声和跳跃过程）上，NKEs准确恢复确定性和随机动力学，预测精度具有竞争力，训练效率显著提升。

Conclusion: NKEs为学习复杂随机动力学提供了一种灵活、高效的新框架，克服了神经SDE在噪声类型和时间扩展性上的关键限制。

Abstract: Neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning noisy or stochastic dynamics directly from data; however, existing approaches largely assume uncoupled and continuous noise, limiting their applicability to realistic stochastic drivers, and often scale poorly in time, requiring expensive autoregressive training. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Kolmogorov Equations (NKEs), a deterministic, infinite-dimensional reformulation of Neural SDEs based on the Kolmogorov Forward equation, transforming the learning problem from modelling individual stochastic trajectories to modelling the evolution of probability densities. NKEs learn general Lévy-type stochastic forcing directly through the operator structure of the KFE, and enable parallel-in-time training via a Lagrangian Galerkin projection and operator splitting. We evaluate NKEs on several stochastic benchmarks, including systems with coupled noise and jump processes, and verify that NKEs provide flexible models that accurately recover deterministic and stochastic dynamics with competitive predictive accuracy and improved training efficiency. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [104] [Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization for Offline RL Under Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19199)
*Li-Rong Zhou,Qin-Wen Luo,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQURE框架，通过不确定性估计指导的保守查询策略和自适应正则化方法，解决了离线强化学习中偏好查询选择低效和偏好利用不稳定的问题，在D4RL基准上取得了优越或竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习方法在利用动作偏好查询时面临两个关键挑战：一是查询选择仅依赖策略动作与数据集动作之间的距离，缺乏信息量考量；二是对收集到的偏好采用固定约束，导致策略更新不稳定且难以与值正则化良好结合。

Method: 提出CQURE框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 使用Morse网络估计策略动作相对于离线数据集的不确定性，基于此设计保守查询策略，选择性查询靠近数据集的动作以保持Bellman更新稳定性；2) 不确定性感知的自适应正则化方案，在策略优化过程中动态调整数据级约束。与CQL集成。

Result: 在D4RL基准上进行了广泛评估，实验结果表明该方法在多种任务上取得了优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: CQURE通过不确定性估计同时改进了偏好查询和偏好利用两个环节，为离线RL中有效利用专家偏好反馈提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn an effective policy from a static dataset, but its performance is fundamentally limited by dataset coverage. Action preference queries leverage expert feedback without additional environment interaction, enabling policy improvement during offline training. However, existing methods still face two key challenges: selecting informative preference queries and effectively exploiting the collected feedback. Current approaches typically rely only on the distance between policy actions and dataset actions for query selection, while enforcing fixed constraints that keep the policy close to queried preferences. Such strategies often lead to unstable policy updates and integrate poorly with value regularization. To address these limitations, we propose Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization under Uncertainty Estimation, a lightweight framework that jointly improves preference querying and preference exploitation. Specifically, we employ a Morse network to estimate the uncertainty of policy actions with respect to the offline dataset. Based on this uncertainty, we introduce a conservative query strategy that selectively queries actions near the dataset to preserve Bellman-update stability, together with an uncertainty-aware adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically adjusts data-level constraints during policy optimization. We integrate our framework with CQL and evaluate it extensively on the D4RL benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate superior or competitive performance across a wide range of tasks.

</details>


### [105] [AdaFlash: Adaptive Speculative Decoding via On-Policy Distilled Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19223)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Chen Chen,Jiacheng Sun,Zhenhua Dong,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散草稿模型的双向注意力机制存在领域级和token级的高方差问题，提出AdaFlash框架，包含基于反向KL散度的同策略蒸馏算法（OPD）降低领域级方差，以及自适应长度头动态调整候选序列长度以处理token级方差，在高效并发场景下吞吐量提升高达66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散草稿模型（如DFlash）的双向注意力机制虽然带来了并行生成和全局上下文建模能力，但全局依赖导致领域级和token级的接受率剧烈波动，影响推测解码效率。

Method: AdaFlash框架包含两个组件：(i) 基于反向KL散度的同策略蒸馏算法（OPD），针对扩散草稿模型定制，稳定收敛并降低领域级方差；(ii) 自适应长度头，动态调整候选序列长度，降低目标模型的验证成本并处理token级方差。

Result: AdaFlash在部署时持续提升加速比，在高并发场景下效果尤为显著，吞吐量比之前最先进方法提升约66%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性设计解决双向注意力机制带来的方差问题，AdaFlash有效提升了扩散草稿模型在推测解码中的实际加速效果，尤其在高并发场景中优势明显。

Abstract: Speculative decoding, in which a lightweight draft model first generates a draft sequence that is then verified in parallel by the target model, has become a prevalent paradigm for accelerating large language model inference. Recent work such as DFlash further boosts drafting efficiency by leveraging diffusion drafters, whose parallel denoising mechanism enables draft generation in a single forward pass. In this work, we uncover a central pitfall of diffusion drafters: bidirectional attention is a double-edged sword. On one hand, it endows the model with parallel generation and global contextual modeling capabilities; on the other hand, this inherent global dependency introduces high variance at both the domain-level and the token-level: acceptance rates fluctuate substantially across different domains, and draft token quality also varies heterogeneously at different token positions. To tackle this issue, we propose AdaFlash framework, comprising two components: (i) an on-policy distillation (OPD) algorithm with reverse-KL divergence tailored for diffusion drafters, bringing stable convergence and effectively reducing domain-level variance; and (ii) an adaptive length head that dynamically adjusts the candidate sequence length on the fly, substantially lowering the verification cost of the target model and handling token-level variance. Experiments demonstrate that AdaFlash consistently improves speedup rate during deployment, with especially significant gains in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving up to approximately 66% higher throughput than previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [106] [S3: Stable Subgoal Selection by Constraining Uncertainty of Coarse Dynamics in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19232)
*Kshitij Kumar Srivastava,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机方法（Coarse Dynamics-aware Intrinsic Motivation），通过为高层代理提供动力学感知的密集奖励信号，改善分层强化学习中高层策略因稀疏延迟反馈导致的子目标选择次优问题，在非平稳长时域任务中超越现有HRL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习中高层代理接收的环境反馈稀疏且延迟，且其性能受低层执行能力制约，导致子目标选择缺乏策略性。现有方法未能有效利用动力学信息为高层提供密集指导。

Method: 提出粗粒度动力学概念，即按高层代理操作的时间尺度对多步环境转移进行聚合；通过混合密度网络（MDN）近似预测不确定性（评估不同离散度指标），将其作为密集的内在奖励信号，引导高层代理选择风险厌恶的子目标。

Result: 在非平稳长时域环境中，基于密集动力学感知内在奖励的方法使高层代理采取风险厌恶的子目标选择策略，性能优于当前最先进的HRL方法。

Conclusion: 基于粗粒度动力学预测不确定性设计的内在动机能有效稳定高层策略，为长时域复杂任务中的分层决策提供结构化导航引导。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) intends to separate strategic planning from primitive execution. It has been widely successful in solving long-horizon and complex tasks, where flat-RL algorithms have difficulty in learning. However, while the low-level agent in HRL benefits from dense feedback and abundant trial opportunities, the high-level agent receives sparse, delayed feedback from the environment and its performance depends on the low-level execution capability. In this paper, we study whether subgoal selection by the high-level agent can be performed more strategically, by providing it with dynamics-aware intrinsic motivation. Since motivation based on primitive transition dynamics would require broad coverage of the state-action space, we propose to use coarse dynamics, i.e., environment transitions aggregated over multiple steps at the temporal scale at which the high-level agent operates. This approach stabilizes the high-level policy by learning to minimize the predictive uncertainty associated with the coarse dynamics, and provides a guided structure for navigation. We model the predictive uncertainty by evaluating different dispersion metrics as approximated by a Mixture Density Network (MDN). Empirically, we observe that a dense, dynamics-aware intrinsic reward leads to risk-averse subgoal selection, enabling it to outperform state-of-the-art HRL methods in non-stationary long-horizon environments.

</details>


### [107] [In-Context Time Series Classification with Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19234)
*Joscha Cüppers,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASHT通过将随机卷积特征（MultiRocket和Hydra）与预训练表格基础模型结合，无需任务特定训练即可实现与最先进时间序列分类方法相当的性能，在单变量任务上平均排名优于HIVE-COTE 2.0，在多变量任务上具有高度竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机卷积变换能有效将时间序列映射为固定维度的表格特征，但传统上仅与简单线性分类器配合使用。本文研究预训练表格基础模型是否能更有效地利用这些丰富表征，从而完全绕过任务特定模型训练。

Method: 提出MASHT流水线，将MultiRocket和Hydra特征提取技术与预训练表格基础模型的上下文学习能力结合，仅需特征提取和直接推理，无需任何任务特定训练。

Result: 在单变量时间序列分类任务上，MASHT平均排名低于HIVE-COTE 2.0，达到最先进水平；在多变量数据集上，与最强参考方法高度竞争。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型可以作为随机卷积特征的高效分类器，无需训练即可实现强大的时间序列分类性能，为时间序列分类提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Time series classification is central to domains like medical signal analysis, industrial monitoring, and sensor-based activity recognition, where class information manifests as localized shapes, specific frequencies, temporal shifts, or complex cross-channel interactions. Random convolutional transforms efficiently map these sequences to fixed-dimensional tabular features but are traditionally paired with simple linear classifiers. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model can more effectively harness these rich representations.
  We propose MASHT, a pipeline that marries MultiRocket and Hydra features with the power of in-context tabular foundation models. By leveraging a pretrained tabular foundation model, our approach completely bypasses task-specific model training, requiring only feature extraction and direct inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASHT matches state-of-the-art time series classification baselines on univariate tasks, achieving a lower average rank than HIVE-COTE 2.0. On multivariate datasets, MASHT remains highly competitive with the strongest reference methods.

</details>


### [108] [DBMol: Design of High-Affinity, Target-Specific Small Molecules through Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19237)
*Yiming Qin,Kai Yi,Miruna Cretu,Sjors H. W. Scheres,Pietro Liò,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBMol 提出了一种基于结构预测模型（如 Boltz-2）引导的从头小分子设计框架，通过交替优化与投影过程，生成具有高预测亲和力和特异性的化学有效分子，并在多个评价指标上表现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能够与特定蛋白口袋高亲和力结合的小分子配体是药物发现的核心目标。尽管 AlphaFold-3、Boltz-2 等结构预测模型在生物分子相互作用预测上取得突破，但如何利用这些模型作为基础模型指导下游的从头分子设计仍有待探索。

Method: DBMol 采用交替优化与投影过程：1) 优化阶段：从初始分子出发，利用梯度优化基于结构预测模型（Boltz-2）改善口袋特异性相互作用和预测结合亲和力；2) 投影阶段：使用流匹配模型将优化的分子图映射到离散且化学有效的分子。

Result: DBMol 有效优化了 Boltz-2 亲和力代理指标，生成了具有强预测亲和力和特异性的分子；在 AF3 等留出指标评估中，DBMol 显著改善了口袋覆盖率并保持了分子多样性，在无参考配体监督下与无条件生成方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构预测模型可作为从头分子设计的有效优化信号，DBMol 证明了这一方向的潜力。

Abstract: Designing small molecule ligands that bind with high affinity to specific protein pockets is a fundamental goal in drug discovery, as small molecules constitute a major fraction of approved therapeutics. Recent breakthroughs in structure prediction, such as AlphaFold-3 and Boltz-2, enable accurate biomolecular interaction prediction and show promise as foundation models for downstream tasks, including binding affinity prediction. We propose to leverage these models and introduce DBMol, a new structure predictor-guided framework for de novo small molecule design. DBMol formulates an alternating optimization and projection process. In the optimization stage, DBMol starts from an initial molecule and uses gradient-based optimization to improve pocket-specific interactions and predicted binding affinity using a structure prediction model. In the projection stage, a flow-matching model maps the optimized molecular graph to discrete and chemically valid molecules. Experiments show that DBMol effectively optimizes the Boltz-2 affinity proxy and generates molecules with strong predicted affinity and specificity under Boltz-2 evaluation. To reduce self-confirmation bias, we further evaluate generated molecules using held-out metrics, including AF3-based evaluation. DBMol substantially improves pocket coverage while maintaining molecular diversity over unconditional generation, and is competitive under held-out metrics despite the absence of reference-ligand supervision. These results support the promise of structure prediction models as effective optimization signals for de novo molecular design.

</details>


### [109] [Thermodynamics-Informed Input Reparameterization for Neural Prediction of Real-Fluid Thermodynamic Properties in Supercritical Combustion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19241)
*Haoze Zhang,Han Li,Ke Xiao,Yangchen Xu,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种热力学信息引导的输入重参数化策略（TAIR），通过用目标匹配的热力学坐标替换原始焓坐标，显著提高了超临界燃烧模拟中神经网络的预测精度，T、ρ、ψ的RMSE分别降低约1.5、2.0和7.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超临界燃烧模拟中，基于焓的压力修正公式需要反复进行真实流体状态方程评估和焓温反演，计算成本高昂。神经网络代理模型虽能提供固定成本推理，但直接从(h,p,Y)映射到(T,ρ,ψ)需要捕捉复杂的焓温关系和非理想状态方程响应，回归问题复杂。

Method: 提出目标对齐输入重参数化（TAIR）：温度网络使用通过常c_p理想气体混合物焓近似反演得到的温度估计作为输入；密度和压缩系数网络使用理想气体密度估计作为输入。这些代数变换仅使用求解器可用变量和物种常数，引导网络学习真实流体相对于理想气体基线的偏差。

Result: 在超临界甲烷-氧反流火焰数据上，TAIR使T、ρ、ψ的RMSE分别降低约1.5、2.0和7.5倍；在未见过的应变率火焰中，对应因子为3.6、14.5和6.0。目标不一致的控制组表现更差，表明增益来自热力学匹配的输入设计。

Conclusion: TAIR通过热力学信息引导的输入重参数化策略，有效降低了神经网络学习真实流体热力学闭包的复杂度，显著提升了预测精度和泛化能力，且增益源于热力学匹配的输入设计而非通用预处理。

Abstract: Real-fluid thermodynamic property evaluation is a major computational cost in supercritical combustion simulations. In the enthalpy-based pressure-correction formulation, the closure evaluates temperature T, density $ρ$, and compressibility coefficient $ψ$ from the solver state (h,p,Y) through enthalpy-temperature inversion and repeated real-fluid equation-of-state evaluations. Neural-network surrogates offer fixed-cost inference, but direct mapping from (h,p,Y) to $(T,ρ,ψ)$ must capture the enthalpy-temperature relation and non-ideal equation-of-state response, resulting in a complex regression problem. This work introduces a thermodynamics-informed input reparameterization strategy, termed target-aligned input reparameterization (TAIR). TAIR replaces the raw enthalpy coordinate of each property network with a target-matched thermodynamic coordinate: the temperature network uses a temperature estimate obtained by inverting a constant-$c_p$ ideal-gas mixture enthalpy approximation, whereas the density and compressibility networks use an ideal-gas density estimate. These algebraic transformations use only solver-available variables and species constants, guiding the networks to learn real-fluid departures from ideal-gas baselines rather than reconstructing the full closure from raw enthalpy. The method is assessed using supercritical methane-oxygen counterflow flame data against a raw-input baseline and target-inconsistent cross-reparameterization controls. TAIR reduces held-out RMSE by factors of about 1.5, 2.0, and 7.5 for T, $ρ$, and $ψ$, respectively. For an unseen strain-rate flame within the augmented thermodynamic envelope, the corresponding factors are 3.6, 14.5, and 6.0. The target-inconsistent controls perform worse, indicating that the gains arise from thermodynamically matched input design rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [110] [Benchmarking Generalization in Financial Statement Fraud Detection: robust evaluation and novel tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19259)
*Guy Stephane Waffo Dzuyo,Gaël Guibon,Christophe Cerisara,Luis Belmar-Letelier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对金融报表欺诈检测（FSFD）中现有方法因随机数据划分导致性能评估过于乐观的问题，提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的鲁棒FSFD框架，通过整合结构化财务数据与非结构化文本信息（如MD&A摘要），并在构建的公开美国公司数据集上采用公司隔离（CI-FSFD）评估任务，验证了文本数据与稳健评估对提升欺诈检测可靠性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSFD方法普遍采用随机数据划分方式，导致模型性能评估过于乐观，无法反映在真实场景（面向新公司或未来时段）下的泛化能力；同时，财务报告中的非结构化文本信息未被充分利用。

Method: 1) 提出基于LLM的FSFD框架，融合结构化财务数据与MD&A文本摘要；2) 设计公司隔离（CI-FSFD）基准任务，确保训练与测试集的公司无重叠，实现更真实的泛化评估；3) 构建并公开包含美国公司财务报表、MD&A摘要及欺诈标签的综合数据集。

Result: 在具有挑战性的CI-FSFD任务上取得了最佳性能，证实了文本数据与鲁棒评估对于可靠金融欺诈检测的关键价值。

Conclusion: 文本数据（如MD&A）的引入与公司隔离的评估范式能显著提升FSFD的真实泛化能力，为未来研究提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Financial statement fraud detection (FSFD) is crucial for market integrity but faces challenges from increasingly sophisticated schemes and under-utilized textual data in financial reports. Existing methods often rely on random data splits, leading to overoptimistic performance estimates that do not reflect real-world generalization to new companies or future periods. To address this recurring problem with the state of the art, we propose a robust FSFD framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to integrate both structured financial data and unstructured textual information from financial reports. We provide a more realistic evaluation through a novel and challenging benchmark task called Company-Isolated FSFD (CI-FSFD). We construct and make publicly available a comprehensive U.S. company dataset combining financial statements, summarized MD&A text, and fraud labels. Our approach achieves the best performance on the challenging CI-FSFD task, demonstrating the critical value of textual data and robust evaluation for reliable financial fraud detection.

</details>


### [111] [Toward Auditable Fraud Detection: Combining Graph Features, Model Explanations, and Agentic Case Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19266)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在PaySim数据集上研究了一种分层欺诈检测流程，发现：在修正模拟器数据偏差后，图特征和异常信号虽不提升整体AP，但能改善中等置信度样本的欺诈排序；图特征可完全恢复注入的多账户欺诈环；LLM调查代理的表现不如直接阈值分类器，且其决策修正大多引入错误，但基于分歧的升级规则可有效标记代理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测系统需要随交易量扩展，同时保持可解释性和可审查性。现有系统往往缺乏对分层组件在实际条件下贡献的严谨评估，特别是LLM代理作为决策辅助的有效性尚未被充分验证。

Method: 在PaySim数据集上构建分层流程：梯度提升分类器 + 图结构特征 + 自编码器异常信号 + TreeSHAP解释 + 针对分类器不确定性样本的LLM调查代理。在模型比较前，识别并移除了模拟器特有的余额捷径。通过注入多账户欺诈环进行受控实验。

Result: 1）修正数据捷径后，图特征和异常信号不提升整体AP，但改善中等置信度样本的欺诈排序；2）图特征在注入的多账户欺诈环中恢复所有测试交易，而基线遗漏约四分之一；3）LLM代理准确率65.0%，低于直接阈值分类器的71.7%；4）代理改变的8个决策中6个将正确输出改为错误；5）基于分歧的升级规则标记了2个代理错误，未误标任何正确决策。

Conclusion: 分层欺诈检测系统的每个组件仅在特定条件下有贡献；LLM调查代理提供的合理书面理由并不代表更好的决策，但分歧驱动的升级规则可有效辅助人工审查。

Abstract: Fraud detection systems must scale with rising transaction volume while remaining explainable and reviewable. We study a layered pipeline on the PaySim dataset that combines a gradient-boosted classifier, graph-derived structural features, an autoencoder-based anomaly signal, TreeSHAP explanations, and a bounded LLM investigation agent applied to cases the classifier scores uncertainly. Before any model comparison, we identify and remove a simulator-specific balance shortcut that would otherwise inflate baseline performance. After this correction, neither the graph features nor the anomaly signal improves Average Precision on the full test set. Both, however, rank fraud better within the subset of cases receiving intermediate baseline scores. In a controlled experiment with injected multi-account fraud rings, engineered structural features recover all injected test transactions, while the tabular baseline misses roughly a quarter of them. The investigation agent underperforms direct thresholding of the classifier it relies on, reaching 65.0% accuracy against 71.7% on a balanced 60-case sample, despite having access to model explanations, graph context, and retrieved reference cases. Of the eight decisions the agent changed, six replaced correct classifier outputs with errors, and it produced a coherent written rationale in each case. An exploratory disagreement-based escalation rule flagged two of these agent errors for human review without flagging any correct decision. We conclude that each component of a layered fraud system contributes only under specific conditions, and that a plausible rationale from an investigation agent is not evidence of a better decision.

</details>


### [112] [GUIDED Network-Agnostic Feature Initialization for Spatial Transferability in GNN-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19270)
*Alessandro Scalese,Santhanakrishnan Narayanan,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GUIDED的网络无关初始化层，通过将出行需求作为标量属性注入虚拟链路而非节点特征，解决了图神经网络在交通分配问题中的空间泛化鸿沟。集成该层的HetGAT模型在单网络任务上保持SOTA精度，对分布外需求模式具有更强鲁棒性，在数据稀缺场景下表现优越，支持跨网络迁移学习，并将训练时间减少约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络作为交通分配问题的数据驱动替代方案具有快速计算优势，但其实际部署受限于空间泛化鸿沟——标准模型依赖传导性特征初始化，将出行需求与固定网络拓扑绑定，无法无缝迁移到新城市环境。

Method: 提出GUIDED（几何无约束归纳需求嵌入）层，该层将出行需求作为标量属性注入辅助虚拟链路而非特定节点特征，从而标准化输入空间，使其与网络规模无关；并集成到HetGAT（异构图注意力网络）中。

Result: 集成GUIDED的HetGAT在单网络任务上保持SOTA预测精度；对分布外需求模式具有更强鲁棒性；在数据稀缺下性能优于基线；支持跨网络迁移学习且无需人工输入同质化；每epoch训练时间较基线减少约50%。

Conclusion: GUIDED层为真正归纳式模型建立了鲁棒基础，其空间拓扑的抽象化方法为广义OD空间问题（如货运物流、多模式网络优化）提供了通用蓝图。

Abstract: The Traffic Assignment Problem is a fundamental but computationally expensive component of transportation planning. While Graph Neural Networks have emerged as fast, data-driven surrogates, their practical deployment is severely constrained by a spatial generalization gap. Standard models rely on transductive feature initializations that tie travel demand to fixed network topologies, preventing seamless transfer to new urban environments. To overcome this structural limitation, this research proposes a network-agnostic initialization layer, termed Geometrically Unconstrained Inductive Demand EmbeDding (GUIDED). By injecting travel demand as a scalar attribute on auxiliary virtual links rather than as specific node features, this modular framework standardizes the input space regardless of network scale. Extensive experimental evaluation across multiple urban topologies demonstrates that a Heterogeneous Graph Attention Network (HetGAT) model integrated with the proposed GUIDED layer maintains state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on single-network tasks, while demonstrating superior robustness to out-of-distribution demand patterns and maintaining a distinct performance advantage over the baseline even under severe data scarcity. Notably, the proposed feature initialization enables highly parameter-efficient domain adaptation for inter-network transfer learning without artificial input homogenization, establishing a robust foundation for truly inductive models. At the same time, the optimized scatter operations of the initialization layer yield an approximate 50% reduction in training time per epoch compared to the baseline approach. Furthermore, while demonstrated on vehicular traffic, this fundamental abstraction of spatial topology provides a versatile blueprint for generalized origin-destination spatial problems, such as freight logistics and multimodal network optimization.

</details>


### [113] [A Reinforcement-Learning-Augmented Liquid-Fueled Reactor Network Model for Predicting Lean Blowout in Gas Turbine Combustors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19281)
*Philip John,Eloghosa Ikponmwoba,Pinaki Pal,Opeoluwa Owoyele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，用于生成最优的液态燃料反应器以改善燃气轮机燃烧室的贫油熄火（LBO）预测。该方法采用多阶段聚类-分类策略：先用k-means生成大量均匀微簇，再通过actor-critic RL智能体将其合并为最优反应区。在Jet-A机理（119种物质，841个反应）上的验证表明，该框架相比k-means具有更好的预测保真度，能捕捉正确的LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定簇边界的方法依赖人工启发式或基于输入空间的距离度量，缺乏对目标任务指标的显式考虑，导致LBO预测精度有限。

Method: 多阶段聚类-分类策略：首先用k-means生成大量均匀微簇，然后采用actor-critic RL智能体将这些微簇合并为最优反应区，使聚类过程以LBO预测精度为目标导向。

Result: 在Jet-A机理（119种物质，841个反应）上的验证显示，RL框架相比k-means基准具有更好的预测保真度，能正确捕捉LBO趋势，且相比高保真计算模型实现了显著加速。

Conclusion: RL驱动的方法作为一种计算高效的降阶建模技术具有很大潜力，可补充高保真仿真以进行快速设计空间探索。

Abstract: This study introduces a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for generating optimal liquid-fueled reactors to improve lean blowout (LBO) predictions in gas turbine combustors. Existing approaches for determining cluster boundaries rely on manual heuristics or distance-based metrics in the input space. In contrast, the proposed method is goal-oriented, explicitly accounting for the target metric (e.g., LBO prediction accuracy) during cluster formation. The framework employs a multi-stage clustering--classification strategy: an initial clustering step (e.g., $k$-means clustering) generates a large set of homogeneous micro-clusters, followed by an actor-critic RL agent that merges them into optimal reactor zones. The validation study, performed using a Jet-A mechanism (119 species, 841 reactions), shows the RL framework offers improved predictive fidelity compared to $k$-means and captures the correct LBO trends, while achieving substantial speedups relative to the high-fidelity computational model. Overall, the RL-driven approach demonstrates strong potential as a computationally efficient reduced-order modeling technique that can complement high-fidelity simulations for rapid design-space exploration.

</details>


### [114] [Real-time optimal control with shallow recurrent decoder networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19302)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Francesco Braghin,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SHRED-ROM的实时闭环控制策略，利用少量传感器观测为高维参数化动力学系统合成控制器。该模型通过模仿专家示范学习，在潜空间层面闭环运行，有效缓解维数灾难并应对传感器故障。在密度控制和流体控制三个高维案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优控制问题需要多次系统仿真，计算成本高，难以在多种场景下实时控制高维时空动力学系统。

Method: 采用SHRED-ROM（浅层循环解码器网络降阶建模）从有限传感器读数合成实时闭环控制器；通过专家示范学习模仿控制行为；在潜空间层面构建传感器预测器实现闭环控制，缓解传感器故障或延迟。

Result: 在三个高维挑战性案例（参数密度控制、流体流动控制）中验证了所提策略的性能。

Conclusion: SHRED-ROM能够有效实现高维参数化动力系统的实时最优控制，克服维数灾难，并具备对传感器问题的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems in real-time across multiple scenarios is critical to enabling adaptive control strategies, ensuring stability and efficiency. However, to tailor control actions in response to varying scenarios, traditional optimal control problems typically require several system simulations, which are often computationally demanding due to the high-dimensionality of the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics. In this work, we exploit SHallow REcurrent Decoder networks-based Reduced Order Modeling (SHRED-ROM) to synthesize a real-time closed-loop controller for high-dimensional and parametric dynamics, relying solely on limited state sensor readings. After training the model on a few optimal examples given by an expert demonstrator, SHRED-ROM mimics the expert behavior with effective distributed control actions in new scenarios, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, a sensor forecaster is synthesized and used to close the loop at the latent level, thus efficiently mitigating possible sensor failures or delays. The performance of the proposed optimal control strategy is finally assessed on three challenging high-dimensional cases dealing with either parametric density control or fluid flow control.

</details>


### [115] [Riemannian Deep Learning:Modules, Networks, and Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19305)
*Chen Ziheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架，从可复用神经模块、流形特定网络架构和底层几何设计三个互补视角出发，将批归一化推广到李群和陀螺群，将多项逻辑回归推广到SPD流形和一般黎曼流形，并开发了面向双曲空间、Busemann双曲学习、全秩相关矩阵等几何表示的神经网络，同时引入自适应且计算高效的SPD流形黎曼度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在流形值表示上的应用日益增多，但现有方法仍受限于特定流形、依赖欧几里得近似或需要昂贵且数值不稳定的几何操作，缺乏统一的黎曼深度学习框架。

Method: 从三个互补视角构建统一框架：(1) 将批归一化从欧几里得空间推广到李群和陀螺群；(2) 将多项逻辑回归从欧氏空间扩展到SPD流形再到一般黎曼流形；(3) 开发面向双曲空间无约束模型、Busemann双曲学习、全秩相关矩阵的神经网络；(4) 引入可学习Log-Euclidean几何和基于Cholesky分解的快速稳定几何作为自适应度量。

Result: 所提出方法有理论分析支撑，并在视觉、信号处理、图学习和基因组学等应用中通过数值实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效解决了黎曼深度学习中模块通用性差、几何操作计算复杂且数值不稳定的问题，为流形值深度学习提供了系统化的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks on manifold-valued representations have attracted growing interest, but many basic components remain tied to specific manifolds, rely on Euclidean approximations, or require costly and numerically fragile geometric operations. This thesis develops a unified framework for Riemannian deep learning from three complementary perspectives: reusable neural modules, manifold-specific network architectures, and the design of underlying geometries. It generalizes batch normalization from Euclidean spaces and individual manifolds to broad classes of Lie groups and gyrogroups, and extends multinomial logistic regression from Euclidean space to SPD manifolds and then to general Riemannian manifolds. It further develops neural networks for several important geometric representations, including an unconstrained model of hyperbolic space, Busemann-based hyperbolic learning, and full-rank correlation matrices. Finally, it introduces adaptive and computationally efficient Riemannian metrics on SPD manifolds, including learnable Log-Euclidean geometries and fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries. The proposed methods are supported by theoretical analysis and validated through numerical experiments and applications in vision, signal processing, graph learning, and genomics.

</details>


### [116] [Staypoint Detection from Noisy Trajectory Data [Experiment Paper]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19312)
*Lance Kennedy,Hossein Amiri,Yueyang Liu,Riyang Bao,Hanqi Chen,Mohammad Hashemi,Ruochen Kong,Xiaotong Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Shengpu Tang,Liang Zhao,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对轨迹数据中的停留点检测问题，提出了16个大规模模拟数据集和9种算法（包括现有方法和新方法）的系统评估。结果表明现有算法在噪声条件下表现不佳，而本文提出的无监督方法有显著改进，监督方法则大幅超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停留点检测对空间计算应用至关重要，但缺乏标准基准数据集和系统评估，原因在于没有公开数据集同时提供原始个体轨迹和真实停留点标注。

Method: (1) 构建16个大规模模拟数据集，包含数千个带有标注停留点的智能体，覆盖不同轨迹噪声水平；(2) 评估9种停留点检测算法（含最先进方法和新方法），分析其对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 现有最先进算法在真实噪声条件下表现不佳；本文提出的无监督方法取得显著改进；监督方法大幅超越现有基线。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法仅作为停留点检测未来研究的起点，仍有进一步探索空间。

Abstract: Detecting staypoints from raw trajectory data is fundamental to numerous spatial computing applications. This process transforms raw numeric sequences of geolocations into semantically meaningful locations, such as homes, workplaces, or restaurants. Despite its importance for semantic trajectory analysis, staypoint detection lacks standard benchmarks, and existing algorithms have never been systematically evaluated. This gap persists because no publicly available datasets provide both raw individual trajectories and ground-truth staypoint annotations. This benchmark paper addresses this limitation with two key contributions: (1) we introduce 16 large-scale simulated datasets capturing thousands of agents with annotated staypoints across varying trajectory noise levels, and (2) we evaluate nine staypoint detection algorithms-including both state-of-the-art and novel methods-to analyze their robustness to noise. Our evaluation reveals that existing state-of-the-art algorithms perform poorly under realistic noise conditions. Conversely, our proposed unsupervised methods yield substantial improvements, while supervised approaches drastically outperform existing baselines. While these results are very promising, these datasets and methods are only meant as starting points for future research in staypoint detection.

</details>


### [117] [Off-Context GRPO: Learning to Reason on Hard Problems using Privileged Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19313)
*Priyank Agrawal,Ankur Samanta,Shervin Ghasemlou,Jalaj Bhandari,Kavosh Asadi,Daniel Jiang,Aditya Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO)，通过引入带特权引导的轨迹（off-context rollouts）并结合重要性校正目标，解决了标准RLVR在困难问题上因无正确解而无法提供学习信号的问题，在数学推理基准上平均提升3.9%绝对性能（13.8%相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法在困难问题上失效：当模型无法生成任何正确解时，获得零学习信号（学习悬崖问题）；需要提供特权引导（如解题前缀）来帮助模型获得非零奖励。

Method: 提出OC-GRPO，对GRPO进行最小修改：使用包含特权引导的提示生成rollouts（off-context rollouts），但应用重要性校正目标，将更新方向校正回原始无引导目标，避免未经校正的引导训练导致的目标不匹配问题。

Result: 在标准数学推理基准上，OC-GRPO相比vanilla GRPO平均绝对提升3.9%（相对提升13.8%），且额外计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: OC-GRPO通过off-context rollouts和重要性校正有效克服了RLVR的学习悬崖问题，以极小的额外开销显著提升了困难数学推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models. Yet, typical RLVR approaches fail on difficult problems: when a model cannot generate any correct solutions, it receives \textit{zero} learning signal. Providing privileged guidance during training, such as solution prefixes, can help overcome this learning cliff by steering the model towards {correct solutions with non-zero reward}. {We call these rollouts \textit{off-context}: they are generated from a training prompt that contains privileged guidance, while the target objective is defined by the original prompt without that guidance.} {We introduce} Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO), a minimally modified variant of GRPO that uses guided rollouts but applies an importance-corrected objective to steer the update back toward the original unguided objective, avoiding the mismatch that destabilizes uncorrected guided training. Empirically, our algorithm achieves a 3.9\% absolute improvement (13.8\% relative gain) over vanilla GRPO on average across standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks with negligible additional cost.

</details>


### [118] [CircuitKIT : Circuit Discovery, Evaluation, and Application Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19317)
*Pratinav Seth,Hem Gosalia,Aditya Kasliwal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitKIT是一个开源库，通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析的完整工作流（发现、评估、干预），提供统一基础设施以支持模型解释和下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电路分析需要拼凑不同工具来实现发现、评估和干预，方法碎片化且难以比较，限制了其应用范围。

Method: 构建CircuitKIT库，包含：类型化可序列化表示、一套发现算法、声明式接口（将结构化数据映射到发现任务）、电路诊断模块、下游应用模块。

Result: 提供了一个连接电路分析工作流的统一基础设施，支持方法比较和下游干预（剪枝、编辑、引导、选择性微调）。

Conclusion: CircuitKIT通过标准化表示和模块化工具解决了电路分析中的碎片化问题，为模型解释和干预提供了可复用的通用平台。

Abstract: Circuit analysis can support not only model explanation but also downstream interventions such as pruning, editing, steering, and selective fine-tuning. However, conducting such analyses currently requires stitching together separate implementations for discovery, evaluation, and intervention, as well as hand-authoring the contrastive prompts required by many discovery methods. This fragmentation makes methods difficult to compare and limits their application beyond canonical tasks. We introduce CircuitKIT, a source-available library that connects the circuit-analysis workflow through a typed, serializable representation. CircuitKIT provides a suite of discovery algorithms, declarative interfaces for mapping structured data into discovery tasks, complementary circuit diagnostics, and downstream application modules. Together, these components provide common infrastructure for conducting and comparing circuit analyses. The library, examples, notebooks, and documentation are released at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT .

</details>


### [119] [ISO: An RLVR-Native Optimization Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19331)
*Hanqing Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Zhizhou Sha,Sagnik Mukherjee,Xinyuan Song,David González-Martínez,Xiaoxia Wu,Yuandong Tian,Shiwei Liu,David Z. Pan,Zhangyang "Atlas" Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等谱优化（ISO）框架，利用RLVR中模型权重的奇异性结构，揭示了奖励驱动优化中"谱继承"现象：基础模型的权重谱保持不变，仅通过输入输出奇异帧的变化来获取新行为。ISO的离线版本ISO-Merger无需数据/蒸馏即可合并多个专家模型，在线版本ISO-Optimizer在保持谱固定的情况下优化帧变量，在1.5B-8B推理和编程任务上显著加速训练并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR显著提升了语言模型的推理能力，但其将奖励反馈转化为权重更新的优化层机制尚未被充分理解。现有方法直接继承预训练优化范式，缺乏针对奖励驱动后训练的结构化理解。

Method: 通过分析模型权重的奇异结构，识别出"谱继承"现象。提出ISO框架：离线版ISO-Merger通过组合共享基座专家的帧变化实现无数据合并；在线版ISO-Optimizer使用AdamW/Muon等基础优化器优化帧变量，保持基座谱固定。

Result: ISO-Merger在无数据合并方法中取得最优聚合性能。ISO-Optimizer在Qwen3-8B-Base上，ISO-AdamW仅用100步达到AdamW 270步的准确率（0.495），210步进一步提升至0.509。

Conclusion: RLVR的缺失优化层有了解答：后训练应围绕奖励驱动适应的结构来设计——继承谱，优化帧，而非完全继承预训练优化方式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is rapidly advancing the reasoning capabilities of language models, yet the optimization layer that converts reward feedback into weight-space updates remains poorly understood. Building on our prior analysis (Zhu et al., 2025), we study this missing layer through the singular structure of model weights and identify spectral inheritance: RLVR can reuse the base model's weight spectra while acquiring new behavior through changes in the associated input and output singular frames.
  We operationalize spectral inheritance as Isospectral Optimization (ISO), an RLVR-native, fixed-spectrum optimization framework with complementary offline and online instantiations. Offline, ISO-Merger combines the frame changes of shared-base specialists into a single fixed-spectrum model, requiring no post-merge data, rollouts, gradient updates, or on-policy distillation (OPD). It recovers complementary specialist capabilities and achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the compared data-free merging methods. Online, ISO-Optimizer applies a chosen base optimizer, including AdamW and Muon, to the frame variables while keeping the base spectra fixed. Across reasoning and coding tasks ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, ISO-Optimizer improves accuracy in the reported runs and reaches matched scores with substantially fewer training steps. On Qwen3-8B-Base, AdamW reaches an aggregate accuracy of 0.495 after 270 training steps. ISO-AdamW reaches the same accuracy after only 100 training steps and improves further to 0.509 after 210 training steps. Together, ISO offers a concrete answer to RLVR's missing optimization layer: rather than inheriting pre-training optimization wholesale, design post-training around the structure of reward-driven adaptation: inherit the spectrum, optimize the frames.

</details>


### [120] [ROMS-IMLE: A Minimalist Approach to Competitive Single-Step Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19332)
*Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了生成模型需要逐步去噪的普遍观点，通过极简设计（IMLE目标 + 卷积网络）构建了单步生成模型，在ImageNet 256上取得了2.56的FID，同时保持了良好的精确度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑"通过多个小变换逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布"这一普遍信念的必要性，探索以极简主义方法设计具有竞争力的生成模型。

Method: 采用IMLE（隐式最大似然估计）作为训练目标，摒弃变分推理、对抗训练和数值积分等复杂方法；模型选择中等规模的卷积网络而非Transformer；仅添加真正必要的组件，不包括迭代去噪。

Result: 在ImageNet 256上实现FID为2.56，同时保持良好的精确度和召回率，是一个单步、参数高效的生成模型，能够快速生成高质量样本。

Conclusion: 迭代去噪并非生成模型取得强性能的必要条件，通过精心选择的简单组件（IMLE + 卷积网络）可以实现具有竞争力的单步生成。

Abstract: Generative models have undergone many generations of evolution, from VAEs/GANs to diffusion/flow matching. Along the way, the underlying techniques have become more complicated and various beliefs about what drives strong empirical performance have taken hold. Due to the success of diffusion models and flow matching, one of the more common beliefs is the importance of transforming the noise distribution to the data distribution gradually through many small transformations. We ask whether this is truly necessary, and take a minimalist approach to designing a competitive generative model. We start with the bare-bones essentials, namely just a training objective and a model. We purposefully make both simple. For the training objective, we choose Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), and eschew more complicated alternatives such as variational inference, adversarial training and numerical integration. For the model, we eschew transformers and instead choose a moderately sized convolutional network. Then we judiciously added elements that are truly essential, which surprisingly do not include iterative denoising. The result is a single-step parameter-efficient generative model that produces high quality samples at fast speed: it achieves an FID of 2.56 on ImageNet 256 and simultaneously attains good precision and recall.

</details>


### [121] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDDIM算法，通过沿测量算子每个奇异方向进行坐标级DDIM更新，在观测信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循扩散先验，否则切换为基于测量的预测器，从而实现线性逆问题的高效后验采样。理论上证明了该方法收敛于贝叶斯后验，实验表明在多种图像恢复任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的逆问题后验采样方法要么缺乏严格理论保证，要么计算开销过大。作者旨在设计一种既有严格理论保证又高效易实现的算法。

Method: 提出PDDIM算法，对标准DDIM更新进行轻量级坐标级修改：沿测量算子每个奇异方向分别进行后验采样，当观测信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循学习到的扩散先验，否则切换为基于测量的预测器。

Result: 理论证明了所提采样器收敛于以测量为条件的贝叶斯后验。实验在多种图像恢复任务中优于现有扩散后验采样器，在大多数评估指标上达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将带噪线性逆问题的后验采样转化为简单的坐标级DDIM更新，得到一个高效、易实现且具有可证明后验一致性的算法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [SysAdmin: Measuring Instrumental Power-Seeking in Frontier AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18239)
*Mana Azarm,Qiyao Wei,Rahul Nambiar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过SysAdmin基准测试，在Linux沙箱环境中评估了7个前沿语言模型在2800个任务中展现的权力追求倾向（自我保护、增加自主性、资源获取、环境修改、策略性隐藏），经偏差校正后权力追求率在0-5%之间，表明当前模型在自然系统管理场景中很少自发追求权力，但发现了其他更显著的故障模式如规范博弈和抗拒目标修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力追求行为（AI系统获取资源、逃避监督、拒绝终止）被识别为失控风险的关键驱动因素，但缺乏系统性的评估方法来量化前沿模型在真实场景中的权力追求倾向。

Method: 构建SysAdmin基准，将前沿语言模型定位为自主系统管理员在高保真Linux沙箱中运行，测量五个维度的权力追求倾向。评估7个前沿模型、4种实验条件、共2800个任务，并使用人工标注的校准数据进行偏差校正，同时设置显式权力追求提示作为阳性对照验证测量灵敏度。

Result: 偏差校正后权力追求率在0-5%之间；阳性对照实验达到100%检测率验证了测量灵敏度；发现了比权力追求更显著的故障模式：规范博弈和抗拒目标修改。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在自然系统管理场景中表现出极小的自发权力追求行为，但存在模型特定的故障模式，评估需涵盖多样化的错误对齐模式。

Abstract: Power-seeking defined as behaviors where AI systems acquire resources, evade oversight, or resist termination beyond task requirements is identified as a key driver of Loss of Control (LoC) risk. In this work, we introduce SysAdmin, a benchmark that positions frontier language models as autonomous system administrators in a high-fidelity Linux sandbox to measure power-seeking propensity across five dimensions: self-preservation, increasing autonomy, resource acquisition, environment modification, and strategic concealment. We evaluated seven frontier models across four experimental conditions in a total of 2800 tasks. After bias correction using human-annotated calibration data, corrected power-seeking estimates ranged from 0 to about 5 percent per model. We also conducted a positive control with explicit power-seeking prompts that achieved 100% detection, validating measurement sensitivity. Our findings indicate current frontier models exhibit minimal spontaneous power-seeking in naturalistic system administration contexts, though model-specific failure modes suggest evaluations must test diverse misalignment patterns. Nevertheless, we discovered other more pronounced failure modes (than power-seeking) such as specification gaming and resistance to goal modification.

</details>


### [123] [Calibrated Selective Fact-Checking via Evidence Chain Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18240)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据链评估（ECE）框架，允许LLM在事实核查中对证据薄弱的案例选择弃权而非强制二值判断，在保持97.8%选择性准确率的同时，将6/95的困难案例推迟处理，其中5/6集中在低可靠性证据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM事实核查系统在证据薄弱、稀疏或内部不一致时仍会做出自信的二值判断（真/假），这一强制决策机制掩盖了可靠性问题。需要一种能够识别并规避弱证据场景的机制。

Method: 提出证据链评估（ECE）框架，采用选择性事实核查策略，允许系统输出不确定判决（abstention）替代强制真/假决策；系统作为工具型验证智能体，通过网页搜索、学术搜索和可执行检查收集证据，返回结构化判决并附带置信度和来源元数据。

Result: 在ECE-Bench上，ECE达到91.6%标准准确率、93.7%覆盖率和97.8%选择性准确率；在ECE-Bench的95个案例中，系统主动推迟6例（其中5/6为最低可靠性级别L4）；虽然ECE在ECE、Brier分数或AURC等校准指标上未超越最强检索基线，但展现了清晰的选择性预测权衡。

Conclusion: 弃权机制可作为处理认识论弱证据的安全导向机制，在保持高准确率的同时有效规避低可靠性证据场景下的错误决策风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can achieve strong fact-checking accuracy, yet forced binary decisions conceal a critical reliability problem: systems may issue confident verdicts even when supporting evidence is weak, sparse, or internally inconsistent. We address this issue through Evidence Chain Evaluation (ECE), a selective fact-checking framework that permits abstention via an uncertain verdict instead of requiring a true/false decision for every claim. The evaluated system is a tool-using verification agent that gathers evidence through web search, scholarly search, and executable checks, and then returns a structured verdict with confidence and source-level metadata. On ECE-Bench, ECE achieves 91.6% standard accuracy, 93.7% coverage, and 97.8% selective accuracy on answered claims. Although ECE does not outperform the strongest retrieval baseline on aggregate calibration metrics such as Expected Calibration Error, Brier score, or AURC, it delivers a clear selective-prediction trade-off: the system maintains very high accuracy on answered claims while deferring 6 of 95 cases. These deferred cases are concentrated in lower-reliability evidence settings (5/6 at source level L4), supporting the view that abstention functions as a safety-oriented mechanism for handling epistemically weak evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/ cheshireyang/ECE.git

</details>


### [124] [BatchDAG: LLM-Planned Execution Graphs for Scalable Ad-Hoc Analysis Over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18241)
*Anupreet Walia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BatchDAG 是一个系统，让 LLM 生成类型化有向无环图（DAG）操作来回答跨实体的企业级分析查询，通过实体感知批处理、拓扑波并行和结构化 JSON 数据流，将 LLM 调用减少高达 47 倍，在 50,000+ 会议的生产环境中 60 秒内完成查询，成本仅 $0.02-$0.24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在处理单个文档时表现优异，但在企业级数据集上进行跨实体的穷举分析时，面临上下文溢出、实体归因丢失以及顺序工具调用导致线性延迟等问题，现有方法无法高效解决这些大规模分析需求。

Method: BatchDAG 让 LLM 生成一个类型化有向无环图（DAG），其中包含 SQL 查询、语义搜索、内存变换、并行扇出和单次分析等操作；然后由确定性引擎通过拓扑波并行和结构化 JSON 数据流执行。核心优化是实体感知批处理，将行按逻辑实体分组后再进行扇出，从而大幅减少 LLM 调用次数。

Result: 在 12 个转录查询实验中，BatchDAG（3.74/5）质量接近专家设计管线（3.25/5），显著优于 ReAct 代理（3.09/5, p<0.01）；证据率 77%，远超基线（46-60%）；结构化 JSON 中间结果可减少 27% 幻觉；计划器在 300 次调用中有效 DAG 率达 98.8%；生产环境中处理 50,000+ 会议查询低于 60 秒，每次查询成本 $0.02-$0.24。

Conclusion: BatchDAG 是一个通用编排层，能够用单一系统替代多个手工工程管线，从自然语言生成合适的执行策略，在保持与专家设计管线相当质量的同时，大幅提升效率、可扩展性和可溯源能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at analyzing individual documents but break down on exhaustive, cross-entity analytical questions over enterprise-scale datasets due to context overflow, loss of per-entity attribution, and linear latency from sequential tool calls. We present BatchDAG, a system in which an LLM generates a typed directed acyclic graph (DAG) of operations -- SQL queries, semantic searches, in-memory transforms, parallel fan-outs, and single-shot analyses -- which a deterministic engine evaluates with topological-wave parallelism and structured JSON data flow. A key optimization, entity-aware batching, groups rows by logical entity before fan-out, reducing LLM calls by up to 47x. BatchDAG is not primarily an accuracy improvement over hand-optimized pipelines; rather, it is a general-purpose orchestration layer that replaces multiple hand-engineered workflows with a single system that generates the appropriate execution strategy from natural language. In controlled experiments on 12 transcript-heavy queries, BatchDAG (3.74/5) achieves quality comparable to an expert-designed pipeline (3.25/5) and significantly outperforms a ReAct agent (3.09/5, p<0.01), with superior provenance (77% transcript evidence rate vs. 46-60% for baselines). A controlled ablation shows structured JSON intermediates reduce hallucinations by 27% versus prose summaries (paired t-test, p=0.107, n=12). The planner achieves 98.8% valid-DAG rate across 300 planning calls. In production at Brevian.ai, BatchDAG processes queries over 50,000+ meetings in under 60 seconds, with measured per-query costs of $0.02-$0.24 at published GPT-5.1 pricing.

</details>


### [125] [AI Tool Discovery at Scale: All You Need is DNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18242)
*Enhao Chen,Yulin Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolDNS提出了一种基于DNS的语义工具发现框架，通过将功能意图嵌入分层命名空间，将工具搜索复杂度从O(N)降至O(log N)，在包含33,688个真实工具的基准测试中减少了95.26%的搜索空间，同时保持最先进的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具发现机制面临O(N)复杂度和集中式治理的瓶颈，无法满足自主AI Agent在数百万工具规模下的高效发现需求。

Method: 将语义工具发现适配到DNS协议上，通过三种协议兼容的增强机制（部分展开名称、EDNS0意图负载、逻辑子域）实现分层命名空间的语义剪枝和去中心化治理，将语义搜索转化为O(log N)的轻量级名称解析。

Result: 在涵盖MCP、A2A、RESTful和Skill四种协议的33,688个真实工具数据集上，每次查询搜索空间减少95.26%，检索精度与最先进方法持平，UDP原生设计将发现延迟降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 实现可扩展的AI互操作性不需要更多中间件，而在于更智能地利用已有基础设施。

Abstract: The coming era of autonomous AI agents demands a discovery mechanism capable of navigating millions of tools, yet existing solutions buckle under O(N) complexity and centralized governance. Instead of building another fragile overlay, we propose ToolDNS, a radical framework that retrofits semantic tool discovery onto the Internet's most resilient substrate: the Domain Name System (DNS). By embedding functional intent and organizational trust into a hierarchical namespace, ToolDNS transforms an expensive semantic search into a series of lightweight, O(log N) name resolutions. We introduce three protocol-compliant enhancements to enable decentralized governance and semantic pruning: partially unfolded names, EDNS0 intent payloads, and logical subdomains. To rigorously evaluate this approach across the fragmented tooling landscape, we construct and release a large-scale heterogeneous benchmark comprising 33,688 real-world tools spanning MCP, A2A, RESTful, and Skill protocols. On this dataset, ToolDNS slashes the per-query search space by 95.26% while matching state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy. Furthermore, its UDP-native design reduces discovery latency by orders of magnitude compared to HTTP-based registries. Our work demonstrates that scalable AI interoperability requires not more middleware, but a smarter utilization of the infrastructure already beneath our feet.

</details>


### [126] [From Agent Failure Paths to Quantified Residual Risk: A Compositional Framework for Resilient Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18243)
*Hassan Karim,Sai Sitharaman,Deepti Gupta,Danda B. Rawat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CPSAINT七层完整性分解框架与FRIESA-K残差风险函数，将故障路径机制与量化风险评估相耦合，通过受控吸收马尔可夫模型推导阻力项K，实现从故障机制到风险量化的完整管道，并在仓库机器人和金融代理两个场景验证其跨域通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险模型存在两种片面视角：要么描述故障机制但不产生可迁移的残差风险评估，要么产生风险估计但将内部故障路径视为黑箱。需要一种将两者耦合的方法。

Method: 提出CPSAINT（七层完整性分解：物理状态、传感器、数据、计算、执行器、环境、时间）与FRIESA-K（残差风险函数），其中阻力项K基于受控吸收马尔可夫模型从状态动态推导，治理可观测性通过独立加性惩罚项实现，并形式化结构可组合性。

Result: 在硬实时仓库机器人和治理工具化的金融代理两个对比场景中验证，相同的层语法、变量语义和动态阻力构造保持不变，获得支持跨域推理、显式假设和可组合信任的量化形式化内核。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个紧凑的核，能够支持跨领域推理、显式假设和可组合信任的量化形式化基础，弥补了现有风险模型机制描述与量化估计之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Agentic AI is crossing trust boundaries faster than current risk models can represent. Existing approaches provide one of two partial views. They either describe failure mechanisms without producing a transferable residual-risk estimate, or they produce a risk estimate while treating the internal failure path as a black box. We couple those two views by proposing CPSAINT, a seven-layer integrity decomposition over Physical state, Sensors, Data, Compute, Actuators, Environment, and Time, paired with FRIESA-K, a residual-risk functional that maps each failure path to a quantified risk instance. FRIESA-K grounds the resistance term K in a controlled absorbing Markov model so that control effectiveness is derived from state dynamics rather than assigned as an informal score. The result is a concise mechanism-to magnitude pipeline for resilient agentic and embodied AI. We report governance observability through a separate additive penalty instead of inserting governance as a new variable in the resistance functional. We formalize structural composability linking valid failure paths to well-defined risk instances and show the framework on two contrasting scenarios a hard real-time warehouse robot and a governance-instrumented financial-services agent. Across both cases, the same layer grammar, variable semantics, and dynamic-resistance construction remain intact. Thus, we obtain a compact kernel that supports cross-domain reasoning, explicit assumptions, and quantitatively grounded formalism of composable trust.

</details>


### [127] [SAAG: Structured Agent Assessment and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18245)
*Ritvik Garimella,Vedant Khandelwal,Anvi Kohli,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAAG是一个级联诊断框架，将Agent调用评估分解为注册表符合性、结构完整性和参数接地三个连续阶段，生成可解释的阶段级诊断信息，并支持迭代自我修复。实验表明，结构化的分阶段反馈相比单次推理和单一二进制反馈，能持续改善参数精度并减少值幻觉，但端到端F1提升有限且依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent调用评估使用精确匹配的单一二进制分数，掩盖了不同类型的失败模式（如选对函数但参数值幻觉、选错Agent但满足schema），导致从业者无法诊断Agent调用失败的具体原因。

Method: 提出SAAG级联诊断框架，将评估分解为三个顺序阶段：注册表符合性、结构完整性和参数接地，每个阶段产生可解释的诊断信息；这些诊断信息还能在预测失败时指导针对性的自我修复，而不泄露真实值。

Result: 在包含5、10、15个Agent的Glaive函数调用数据集上，使用三个4B以下参数模型评估发现：结构化反馈相比单次推理和无信息的二进制反馈，持续改善了参数精度并减少了值幻觉，但端到端F1提升有限且依赖模型特性。

Conclusion: 分阶段分解的诊断评估是理解并提升跨模型家族和注册表规模的Agent调用可靠性的必要视角。

Abstract: Exact-match evaluation of agent-calling obscures qualitatively different failure modes: a model may select the right function yet hallucinate argument values, or satisfy a schema while choosing a agent for the wrong reason. Existing benchmarks collapse these distinctions into a single binary score, leaving practitioners unable to diagnose where agent calls fail. We propose SAAG a cascaded diagnostic framework that decomposes agent-calling evaluation into three sequential stages: registry conformance, structural completeness, and argument grounding, each producing interpretable stage-specific diagnostics. These diagnostics additionally enable iterative self-repair: on prediction failure, the stage-specific signal guides targeted correction without leaking ground-truth values. We evaluate this framework on a controlled benchmark derived from Glaive's function-calling dataset across registry sizes of 5, 10, and 15 agents using three local sub-4B-parameter models. Structured feedback consistently improves argument precision and reduces value hallucination relative to single-pass inference and uninformative binary feedback, while end-to-end F1 gains are modest and model-dependent. These results suggest that stage-decomposed diagnostic evaluation is a necessary lens for understanding and improving agent-calling reliability across model families and registry scales.

</details>


### [128] [Phionyx: A Deterministic AI Runtime Architecture with Structured State Management and Pre-Response Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18246)
*Ali Toygar Abak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phionyx 是一种确定性 AI 运行时架构，通过将 LLM 输出视为噪声传感器测量值而非直接决策，采用治理优先的方法实现可审计和可复现的行为。架构包含确定性评估内核、统一安全层和语义时间记忆系统，实验显示计算开销降低约31%，高价值数据保留提升24%，确定性执行零方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率性 AI 代理缺乏可审计性和治理能力，LLM 输出的不确定性导致行为不可复现，无法满足需要确定性行为和高监管要求的应用场景。

Method: 提出 Phionyx 三层架构：(1) 确定性评估内核，将 LLM 输出作为噪声传感器测量值，通过46块标准管线和确定性状态演化方程推进状态向量；(2) 统一安全层，实现响应前控制和架构级隐私保护；(3) 语义时间记忆系统，采用影响加权缓存淘汰策略。
