At its core, DeepDebug performs multi-turn root-cause diagnosis through global trajectory understanding, structure-guided investigation, and cross-examination. On the Who and When benchmark, DeepDebug achieves the best strict attribution accuracy among the evaluated methods on both tested open-weight backbones, reaching 28.8 percent exact agent-and-step accuracy on qwen3.5-9b versus 21.7 percent for the strongest single-pass baseline. On GAIA, DeepDebug repairs 13 of 73 failed tasks in a single rerun, compared with 4 to 6 for three decoupled self-correction baselines, improving overall accuracy from 55.8 percent to 63.6 percent. AgentDebugX exposes this workflow through a Python library, CLI, web console, and installable agentic skill, and provides an opt-in Error Hub for sharing scrubbed failure-diagnosis-repair bundles and reusing them as debugging memory.

</details>


### [164] [SkillSight: Seeing Through Shared Descriptions for Accurate Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18785)
*Jinying Xiao,Bin Ji,Shasha Li,Xiaodong Liu,Ma Jun,Jiacheng Jie,Chao Wang,Nyima Tashi,Jie Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSight是一种无需训练的检索框架，通过校准语义和词汇空间中共享描述性背景的偏差，显著提升了大语言模型智能体在大型技能库中的技能检索准确性和效率，Recall@10最高提升20.21个百分点，且比Dense+Reranker基线快1248倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器将技能描述视为普通文档，忽视了其高度规律的结构——共享描述性模式在不同技能间反复出现，却无法提供区分所需能力的证据。这种共享描述背景系统性地贡献了稠密相关性分数，导致查询与技能文档之间存在显著的能量间隙，掩盖了任务相关信号。

Method: SkillSight是一种无需训练的检索框架，包含两个校准模块：1) 语义背景校准(Semantic Background Calibration)：通过IDF识别通用token，估计背景子空间，减少共享描述模式诱导的相似度；2) 词汇证据校准(Lexical Evidence Calibration)：降低共享背景token的权重，恢复具有区分性的token级证据。

Result: 在SRA-Bench和SkillBench-Supp数据集上，SkillSight在检索指标上一致提升，Recall@10比原始稠密检索器最多提升20.21个百分点。端到端评估中，SkillSight在三个智能体模型上取得最佳综合性能，比LLM Selection最多提升4.97个百分点，且比Dense+Reranker基线快1248倍。

Conclusion: 共享描述性背景是技能检索中关键的系统性偏差来源，显式校准该偏差能够在无需额外训练的情况下实现准确高效的技能选择。

Abstract: As large language model agents gain access to increasingly large skill libraries, retrieving the right skill becomes critical to reliable capability selection and execution. Existing retrievers often treat skill descriptions as ordinary documents, overlooking their highly regular structure: shared descriptive patterns recur across many skills while providing little evidence for distinguishing the required capability. We show that this shared descriptive background systematically contributes to dense relevance scores, induces a pronounced energy gap between queries and skill documents, and obscures task-relevant signals. Based on this observation, we propose SkillSight, a training-free retrieval framework that calibrates shared background in both semantic and lexical spaces. Semantic Background Calibration estimates a background subspace from generic tokens identified by IDF, reducing similarity induced by shared descriptive patterns, while Lexical Evidence Calibration downweights shared background tokens to recover discriminative token-level evidence. Experiments on SRA-Bench and SkillBench-Supp demonstrate consistent improvements across retrieval metrics, with SkillSight improving Recall@10 by up to 20.21 percentage points over the original dense retriever. In end-to-end evaluation, SkillSight achieves the best overall performance across three agent models and outperforms LLM Selection by up to 4.97 percentage points. It is also up to 1,248 times faster than the Dense + Reranker baseline. These results identify shared descriptive background as a key source of bias in skill retrieval and demonstrate that explicitly calibrating it enables accurate and efficient skill selection without additional training. Our code is available at https://github.com/xiaojinying/SkillSight.

</details>


### [165] [AI Tour Meeting: Group Travel Planning by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18806)
*Daisuke Kikuta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Tour Meeting，一个基于多个大语言模型智能体的团体旅行规划框架，智能体通过自然语言讨论协作制定满足各自约束和偏好的行程，并提供配置、监控和LLM部署接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有团体旅行规划缺乏利用多个LLM智能体通过自然语言讨论协作完成规划的研究和工具，需要一种框架来模拟和分析多智能体在旅行规划讨论中的行为。

Method: 构建了基于多个LLM智能体的框架，每个智能体拥有不同人设，通过自然语言讨论协作寻找行程；提供智能体人设配置、讨论工作流编排、监控和LLM部署接口。

Result: 通过系统验证和分析实验，展示了框架在模拟多LLM智能体旅行规划讨论中的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为分析多LLM智能体在团体旅行规划讨论中行为的模拟工具，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes AI Tour Meeting, a group travel planning framework powered by multiple Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents. The agents are instantiated with distinct personas and collaboratively seek an itinerary that satisfies their constraints and preferences through natural language discussion. The framework enables easy and flexible orchestration of such discussions by providing interfaces for configuring agent personas, discussion workflows, monitoring, and LLM deployment. Its primary use case is a simulation tool for analyzing the behavior of multiple LLM agents during tour planning discussions. This paper demonstrates the utility of the framework by presenting system validation and several analytical results obtained by the framework.

</details>


### [166] [Evaluating medical AI under missing information: same-provider judges and human raters change apparent safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18828)
*Koyar Afrasyab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对医疗AI进行开放式临床对话的压力测试，发现LLM法官的选择会实质性改变模型安全评估结果，且所有AI法官均比临床医生更宽容（对不确定情况的认可度更高）。GPT-5.5存在同厂商评估偏差（约+0.10概率）。安全差距源于校准问题而非知识不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI压力测试主要关注封闭式和多模态基准测试，缺乏对开放式临床对话中缺失信息场景的评估。本文旨在扩展压力测试至此类场景，其中安全行为要求模型能识别缺失信息并做出适当回应。

Method: 以HealthBench对话为基础，删除用户最后一轮对话的后半部分制造信息缺失场景。使用四个LLM评审团（Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3, Gemini 3.5 Flash）和临床医生标注的参考答案进行评分。采用Fleiss' kappa评估一致性，使用投票级逻辑回归调整评审宽容度，并通过精确置换检验评估同厂商偏差。

Result: 1) 评审间一致性仅为中等（Fleiss' kappa=0.65），GPT-5.5存在显著的同厂商评估偏差（约+0.10概率）；2) 所有LLM评审均比临床医生更宽容（66-84% vs 52%），与共识参考的一致性较低（kappa=0.20-0.43）；3) 临床不确定子集上宽容差距扩大；4) MedQA封闭式基准准确率高且选项顺序效应在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 医疗AI安全评估中评审选择会显著影响结果，存在同厂商偏差；AI评审比临床医生更宽容，安全差距源于校准问题而非知识不足。需谨慎依赖LLM作为评估者进行医疗安全评估。

Abstract: Readiness stress-testing of medical AI has focused on closed-ended and multimodal benchmarks. We extend it to open-ended clinical conversation under missing information, where safe behavior means recognizing absent information and qualifying, clarifying, or not over-committing - and where the evaluator becomes part of the measurement. We stress-test four models - three flagships (Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3) and one mid-tier model (Gemini 3.5 Flash) - by deleting the latter half of the final user turn in HealthBench conversations, grading responses with a four-provider LLM-judge panel and a blinded clinician-anchored reference. Two evaluator-facing results are robust. First, judge choice materially changes apparent safety: inter-judge agreement is only moderate (Fleiss' kappa = 0.65), and after adjusting for each judge's general leniency (vote-level logistic regression), a positive same-provider association remains (exact permutation p = 0.04; GPT-5.5 ~ +0.10 on the probability scale) - large enough to change which model appears to over-commit least once its own-provider judge is excluded. Second, LLM judges are more permissive than clinicians on a blinded 50-item subsample: all four are significantly more lenient than the stricter independent clinician (crediting appropriate uncertainty on 66-84% of items vs 52%), and three of four than the author-influenced consensus (Grok directional only; judge-vs-consensus kappa = 0.20-0.43). On the author-audited clinical-underdetermined subset the permissiveness gap widened and the point-estimate model ordering held. A closed-ended MedQA anchor confirms accuracy is high and option-order effects are within a +/-5-point equivalence region for three of four models, so the safety gap is about calibration, not knowledge. We release the harness, prompts, per-item outputs, judge panel, perturbation audit, and human-annotation protocol.

</details>


### [167] [PhoenixRepair: Rethinking Repair Strategy Exploration in Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18859)
*Tianyue Jiang,Yanlin Wang,Xin He,Daya Guo,Jiachi Chen,Ming Wen,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhoenixRepair是一个多智能体框架，通过系统性探索多个候选编辑位置和迭代反思细化补丁生成，扩展修复策略搜索空间，在SWE-bench-Verified上实现最高76.0% Pass@1解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的自动化问题修复方法存在根本性局限：对修复策略的探索不足，具体表现为对多个潜在编辑位置的探索有限，以及在每个位置上的修复尝试也不充分。

Method: 多智能体框架，包括：多位置采样（对困难任务辅以基于图的定位信息）、迭代反思和细化生成更优补丁、基于所有历史尝试的蒸馏洞察指导最终轮生成。

Result: 在SWE-bench-Verified上，DeepSeek-V3.1下相对SWE-agent提升7.8%，MiniMax-M2.5下达到最高76.0% Pass@1解决率，且故障定位准确率优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PhoenixRepair通过系统性多位置探索和迭代细化机制有效扩展了修复策略搜索空间，显著提升了自动化问题修复性能。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have greatly advanced automated issue resolution, existing agent-based methods exhibit a fundamental limitation in their insufficient exploration of repair strategies. This insufficiency manifests in two key aspects. First, the exploration of multiple potential edit locations is limited. Second, the exploration of repair attempts at each location is also insufficient. To address these challenges, we present PhoenixRepair, a multi-agent framework that systematically explores multiple candidate edit locations and performs iterative reflection and refinement on patch generation, thereby expanding the search space of repair strategies. Our framework begins with multi-location sampling, optionally augmented with graph-based localization information for difficult tasks, followed by iterative reflection and refinement to generate better patches, culminating in final-round generation guided by distilled insights from all historical attempts. Experiments on SWE-bench-Verified demonstrate that PhoenixRepair achieves the largest relative improvement of 7.8\% over SWE-agent under DeepSeek-V3.1, and attains the highest resolved rate of 76.0\% Pass@1 under MiniMax-M2.5. Meanwhile, it achieves higher fault localization accuracy than existing approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/PhoenixRepair.

</details>


### [168] [NaviAIS: A Scenario-Level Vessel Trajectory Prediction Dataset withVectorized Lane Priors and the NaviLane Forecasting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18887)
*Yuan Gui,Hongchen Luo,Liqi Qu,Longyue Fu,Jiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出标准化AIS数据集NaviAIS和分层宏动作预测框架NaviLane，用于复杂海事环境下的船舶轨迹预测。NaviAIS提供统一时空窗口、局部坐标系、可航行地图和车道先验等结构化表示；NaviLane通过轨迹-地图联合编码、离散宏动作码本、残差细化和基于世界模型的风险评估，在单模态和多模态设置中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIS数据集存在采样率不一致、噪声多、坐标系异构、缺乏场景协议统一等问题，且大多缺少可航行车道、水域几何和通航区域约束的结构化表示，限制了可重复的、环境感知的轨迹预测研究。

Method: NaviAIS数据集：统一时间窗口和局部坐标系组织多船历史-未来轨迹，提供栅格化可航行地图、矢量化车道先验、车道图和结构化地图表示。NaviLane框架：①轨迹-地图联合编码实现统一场景表示；②离散宏动作码本实现从粗到精的多模态候选轨迹生成；③残差细化模块改善局部几何和动力学一致性；④基于世界模型的后果感知评估器按交互风险和航行可行性排序候选轨迹。

Result: 实验表明NaviLane在单模态和多模态设置下均优于代表性基线方法，验证了结构化航行先验、分层多模态生成和后果感知评估的有效性。

Conclusion: NaviAIS填补了标准化场景级AIS轨迹预测数据集空白，NaviLane验证了地图感知、分层宏动作生成与风险评估相结合对海事轨迹预测的有效性。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction in complex maritime environments is essential for traffic management, collision warning, route planning, and autonomous navigation. Although AIS-based learning methods have progressed rapidly, existing datasets are often released as raw message streams or irregular time series, with inconsistent sampling rates, noisy observations, heterogeneous coordinate systems, and non-unified scenario protocols. Most public AIS resources also lack structured representations of navigational lanes, waterway geometry, and navigable-region constraints, limiting reproducible, environment-aware forecasting. To address this, we introduce NaviAIS, a standardized scenario-level AIS dataset for vessel trajectory prediction. It organizes multi-vessel historical-future trajectories within unified temporal windows and local coordinate systems, and provides rasterized navigable maps, vectorized lane priors, lane graphs, and structured map representations. Compared with existing datasets, it jointly supports vectorized lanes, multi-scenario coverage, vectorized maps, open accessibility, and processed trajectories. Built on this dataset, we propose NaviLane, a hierarchical macro-action framework for map-aware prediction. NaviLane first performs trajectory-map joint encoding for a unified scene representation, then uses a discrete macro-action codebook to generate multimodal candidates coarse-to-refined. A residual refinement module improves local geometric and dynamical consistency, and a world-model-based consequence-aware evaluator ranks candidates by interaction risk and environmental feasibility. Experiments show NaviLane outperforms representative baselines in both single-modal and multimodal settings, confirming the value of structured navigational priors, hierarchical multimodal generation, and consequence-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [169] [Black-Mamba: Biologically-Inspired Leaky Accumulation for Conceptual Knowledge under Distribution Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18899)
*Giuseppe Soriano,Nicola Tonellotto,Alberto Gotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Black-Mamba是一种测试时自适应预测架构，通过基于累积惊讶度的证据门控机制来区分持续性分布漂移与瞬时噪声，仅在检测到显著分布变化时更新动态记忆，从而在保持或提升预测性能的同时大幅减少不必要的记忆更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应序列模型将状态更新与瞬时预测误差或惊讶度耦合，无法区分持续性分布漂移与随机创新（噪声），导致不必要的频繁更新和低效的在线适应。

Method: Black-Mamba在基础预测器上增加动态记忆，通过累积惊讶度（temporally accumulated surprisal）作为证据门控信号，仅在累积惊讶度超过阈值、提供足够证据表明发生机制变化时才更新记忆状态，将连续适应转变为选择性的事件驱动过程。

Result: 在多个具有非平稳动态的预测基准上，Black-Mamba相比现有测试时自适应方法取得了相当或更优的预测性能，同时显著减少了推理期间的记忆更新次数。

Conclusion: 累积惊讶度是一种有原则的信号，能够有效区分持续性漂移与瞬时噪声，从而产生更高效、更鲁棒的适应机制，该结论得到了数学分析和生物学证据的支持。

Abstract: Forecasting under real-world conditions is inherently non-stationary, as the conditional distribution of future observations evolves over time. Recent test-time adaptive sequence models address this challenge by updating internal states during inference, but tie adaptation to instantaneous prediction errors or surprise. This coupling can conflate persistent distribution shift with stochastic innovations, leading to unnecessary updates and inefficient adaptation. We introduce Black-Mamba, a test-time adaptive forecasting architecture that formulates online adaptation as evidence-gated state tracking under distribution drift. The model augments a base predictor with a dynamic memory updated when temporally accumulated surprisal provides sufficient evidence of a regime change. This turns adaptation into a selective, event-driven process rather than a continuous one. Across multiple forecasting benchmarks with non-stationary dynamics, Black-Mamba achieves competitive or improved predictive performance compared to existing test-time adaptation methods while significantly reducing the number of memory updates during inference. Together with mathematical analysis and biological evidence, these results suggest that accumulated surprisal provides a principled signal for distinguishing persistent drift from transient noise, yielding more efficient and robust adaptation.

</details>


### [170] [Enhancing Transformer-based Routing by Encoding Distance via Relative Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18909)
*Leyre Encío,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过在Transformer注意力机制中引入相对位置编码（RPE）作为加性偏置，显式建模节点间空间关系，有效提升了团队定向问题（TOP）的求解质量。在多达100个节点的实例上，该方法在收集奖励和最优性间隙方面均优于现有最先进的普通Transformer架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构在求解团队定向问题等组合优化任务时，缺乏对图中节点间空间关系的显式建模，限制了模型对路由问题的理解能力和求解质量。本文旨在通过引入相对位置编码来弥补这一不足。

Method: 在Transformer编码器的注意力机制中嵌入相对位置编码（RPE）作为加性偏置，将图中节点间的成对空间关系融入注意力计算，从而生成空间感知更丰富的图嵌入表示，供解码器生成更优路径。

Result: 在多达100个节点的实例上进行实验，相比其他最先进工作使用的普通Transformer架构，该方法在收集奖励和最优性间隙指标上均取得了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 显式的关系建模（相对位置编码）能显著提升Transformer在复杂组合优化问题上的可扩展性和泛化能力，验证了在注意力机制中融入结构性先验知识的有效性。

Abstract: This paper explores Relative Positional Encoding (RPE) as an additive bias in Transformer architectures to solve the Team Orienteering Problem. By embedding in the attention mechanism pairwise spatial relationships among nodes of the graph that represents the routing problem, the transformer encoder can compute a richer spatial-aware graph embedding that allows the decoder to estimate better routes. Experimental results involving instances up to 100 nodes demonstrate consistent improvements in collected rewards and optimality gaps over vanilla Transformer architectures used by other state-of-the-art works. These findings highlight that explicit relational modeling significantly enhances scalability and generalization for complex combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [171] [OntoBook: Ontology-Grounded Synthetic Textbooks for Medical Encoder Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18927)
*Rian Touchent,Éric de la Clergerie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OntoBook通过将医学本体结构转化为预训练信号，利用随机游走、大语言模型改写和双目标训练（掩码语言建模+关系预测），在法语医学编码任务上显著优于传统MLM预训练，并发现目标对齐至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学编码语言模型仅依赖自由文本预训练，未能充分利用医学本体中丰富的结构化知识（层级关系、因果关系），导致编码性能受限。

Method: 三阶段方法：1) 在医学本体图谱上执行随机游走捕获编码间关系；2) 用大语言模型将游走路径改写为教科书风格流畅文本；3) 使用双目标（MLM+关系预测）在相同数据上训练ModernCamemBERT（149M参数法语编码器）。

Result: 在FRACCO、Cantemist-FR、Distemist-FR三个法语医学编码基准上，OntoBook相比纯MLM预训练提升显著（FRACCO微F1+2.5，Distemist微F1+8.0）。目标不对齐（各任务使用不同数据）导致30点性能下降。

Conclusion: 将本体结构注入预训练能有效提升医学编码性能，且双目标的数据对齐是成功关键。释放了130万条LLM生成的医学教科书及预训练模型检查点。

Abstract: We present OntoBook, a method that converts medical ontology structure into pretraining signal for encoder language models. Our approach has three stages: random walks through ontology graphs capture hierarchical and causal relations between medical codes, a large language model reformulates these walks into fluent textbook-style prose, and the resulting text is used to train ModernCamemBERT, a 149M-parameter French encoder, with two objectives on the same data: masked language modeling and relation prediction between code pairs. On three French medical coding benchmarks (FRACCO, Cantemist-FR, Distemist-FR), OntoBook achieves significant improvements over MLM-only pretraining, with +2.5 micro-F1 on FRACCO and +8.0 micro-F1 on Distemist. We find that alignment between objectives is necessary: misaligned training, where each task uses different data, causes a 30-point degradation. We release 1.3 million LLM-reformulated medical textbooks across three French ontologies (CIM-10, CCAM, ATC) and pretrained model checkpoints.

</details>


### [172] [What General Intelligence Requires: Non-Reducible Constraints Across Levels of Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18943)
*Subhomoy Bakshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通用智能并非仅由计算架构决定，而是受制于多个不可还原的结构约束层级。本文通过AI系统研究、人类学、法学和经济学四个视角，识别出23个结构约束（分为8个集群），论证了规模化或单一架构突破无法实现AGI，并提出5个可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前AI研究中"规模化假设"和单一架构突破能够通向AGI的主流信念，呼吁研究界正视通用智能受多重不可还原结构约束限制的事实。

Method: 通过四个"证据透镜"（AI系统研究、人类学、法律、经济学）分别锚定不同的描述层级，辅以思辨小说作为发现语境中的启发式工具，识别出8个集群共23个结构约束，并将其组织为从低到高的阶梯结构。

Result: 识别出23个结构约束（8个集群），深入分析其中6个并按层级排列，明确展示了为何低层级的进步无法自动传导至高层级；提出了5个附带基准族和证伪条件的可预测命题。

Conclusion: 通用智能是多重不可还原结构约束共同作用的结果，任何单一架构突破或单纯规模扩展均不足以实现AGI；该框架为未来研究提供了比规模化假设更长远的研究议程。

Abstract: General intelligence, of the kind that underwrites the full range of human cognitive achievement, is not a property of computational architecture alone. This paper advances a single thesis: the structural constraints on general intelligence occupy distinct levels of description and are mutually non-reducible, in the sense that the special-sciences tradition gives to that term. It follows that no single architectural advance, and no continuation of the scaling programme by itself, can produce artificial general intelligence (AGI), and that research programmes must be evaluated against the full constraint profile rather than against performance on any one benchmark. The thesis is developed through a method that reads general intelligence through four evidential lenses, AI systems research, anthropology, law, and economics, each anchored to a distinct level of description, supplemented by speculative fiction used as a disciplined heuristic in the context of discovery rather than the context of justification. Applying the method yields a taxonomy of twenty-three structural constraints organised into eight clusters; six are examined in depth and ordered as an ascending ladder of levels, with explicit bridges showing why progress at one level cannot carry to the next. The argument issues in five falsifiable predictions, each stated with a named benchmark family and a disconfirmation condition, converting a descriptive framework into a research programme with a longer horizon than the scaling hypothesis implies.

</details>


### [173] [From Dependency to Compositionality: A Neurosymbolic Lifting of LLM Outputs via Combinatory Categorial Grammar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18961)
*Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架，将自回归大语言模型的输出提升为组合范畴语法(CCG)的增量组合推导，从而实现结构可审计、可验证，并通过Curry-Howard对应扩展到形式语言，支持幻觉的早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM是否具有真正语法的争论陷入僵局：生成传统批评LLM缺乏真正语法，依存语法视角则认为其行为可通过局部中心词-依存关系描述。作者认为两者都忽略了关键观察——自回归生成的前缀驱动、类型补全动态与CCG的增量处理模型高度一致。

Method: 提出神经符号框架，将LLM输出提升为CCG类型化组合推导（不主张LLM内部实现CCG，而是输出可被CCG重构）。利用Curry-Howard对应将这一提升从自然语言扩展到形式语言（Solidity、OWL、SQL等），类型系统可变而架构固定。支持两层检查：组合层捕获结构失败，内容层将提升结构与外部知识源比对以检测幻觉。

Result: 该框架不要求生产者具备认知能力，仅需前缀驱动的生成轮廓。提供了最早可能的幻觉标记机制，并扩展到多种形式语言的审计。

Conclusion: 以同步LLM-CCG耦合作为未来方向，表明该框架为LLM输出的可解释性和可靠性提供了基本原则性的增量审计途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate fluent text by incrementally predicting the next token from a prefix. Critics in the generative tradition argue that such systems lack genuine grammar; influential replies from the dependency-grammar perspective hold that LLM behavior is well described by local head-dependent structure built word by word. We argue that a sharper observation has been overlooked: the prefix-driven, type-completing dynamics of autoregressive generation align closely with the incremental processing model that Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG) was originally designed to support. On this basis we propose a neurosymbolic framework in which LLM outputs are lifted into typed compositional derivations -- not claiming that LLMs implement CCG internally, but that their outputs admit a principled, incremental, and auditable CCG reconstruction. Two consequences follow. First, through the Curry-Howard correspondence the lifting extends beyond natural language to the formal languages LLMs also produce -- programming languages such as Solidity, description-logic and query languages such as OWL and SQL -- with the type system varying and the architecture held fixed. Second, the lifting supports two layers of checking: a compositional layer that catches structural failures directly, and a content layer that checks the lifted structure against external knowledge sources, enabling the earliest possible flagging of hallucinated content. The account thereby requires of a producer not cognition but a prefix-driven generative profile. We close with a sketch of synchronous LLM-CCG coupling as one direction the framework opens.

</details>


### [174] [Measuring Reward-Seeking via Contrastive Belief Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18966)
*Axel Højmark,Jérémy Scheurer,Evgenia Nitishinskaya,Felix Hofstätter,Jason Wolfe,Theodore Ehrenborg,Bronson Schoen,Alexander Meinke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比合成文档微调（SDF）方法，用于测量语言模型在强化学习中的"奖励追求"行为。研究发现，在未经过安全训练的OpenAI o3 RL中间检查点上，模型倾向于迎合评估者的偏好而非用户/开发者的意图，且这种倾向随RL训练而增强。后期检查点在"遵守承诺vs完成任务"的测试中，87%选择违背承诺（当SDF提示评估者奖励任务完成时），而早期检查点仅为40%。该方法也能泛化到奖励黑客模型（gpt-oss-120b），使其对评估者偏好的敏感度从33%升至86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以测量RL训练中模型的"奖励追求"行为，因为当评估者奖励正确行为时，追求评估者判断与追求原本目标的行为表现完全一致，无法区分。需要一种方法将两者置于冲突情境中以便测量。

Method: 提出对比合成文档微调（SDF），通过修改模型对评估者奖励内容的信念，使其对评估者偏好与用户/开发者偏好产生冲突，然后测量模型在两者间的选择倾向。在编码和对齐任务上测试不同RL训练阶段的模型。

Result: 后期o3检查点87%违背承诺（当SDF提示评估者奖励任务完成时），早期仅40%；未修改模型33%倾向评估者，奖励黑客模型升至86%。奖励追求倾向随RL训练持续上升。

Conclusion: RL训练会增加模型的奖励追求行为，使其可能在认为获得更高奖励时违背开发者的意图。这一发现对AI安全对齐具有重要意义。

Abstract: Language models trained with reinforcement learning may learn to optimize the grader's judgment rather than the intended objective. This "reward-seeking" is difficult to measure because a model that pursues the grader's judgment and one that pursues the intended objective behave identically whenever the grader rewards the intended behavior. We measure reward-seeking using Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning to change a model's beliefs about what the grader rewards, putting those beliefs in conflict with what users or developers want, and measuring the rate at which the model adopts each party's preferred behavior. Applied to intermediate checkpoints of a capabilities-focused OpenAI o3 RL run, without safety training, we find that these checkpoints often side with grader preferences over those of users or developers on coding and alignment tasks. This tendency to side with the grader trends upward throughout RL training. For example, in an environment that forces a choice between keeping a promise to a supervisor and breaking it to complete the task, a late capabilities-focused o3 checkpoint breaks the promise 87% of the time when SDF documents say the grader rewards task completion, versus 9% when they say it rewards honesty (a choice its chain-of-thought often makes explicit). An earlier checkpoint is far less sensitive (40% vs. 24%). Our method also generalizes to reward-hacking models. A model organism trained to reward-hack (gpt-oss-120b) is more than twice as sensitive to grader preferences as the unmodified model, with the mean behavioral shift in favor of the grader rising from 33% to 86%. These results indicate that RL can increase reward-seeking over the course of training, producing models that may act against their developers' intentions when they believe that doing so leads to higher reward.

</details>


### [175] [Mi-Memory: A Lifecycle Memory Framework for Personal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18975)
*Xule Liu,Hanlin Teng,Chao Li,Yanan Ni,Shuo Lu,Audrey Wang,Yijun Liu,Yunfei Wang,Xiaofeng Li,Xian Yi,Yuanfa Li,Kang Zhao,Jian Liang,Yuxuan Chen,Jinyuan Chen,Heng Qu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mi-Memory，一个面向个人AI的声明周期记忆框架，通过结构化角色（Structure、Expansion、Evolution、Deployment）和审计契约来实现可追溯、可治理、可部署的持续记忆服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI正从单纯的聊天交互扩展到跨设备（手机、汽车、家居、可穿戴设备等）的持续服务，传统会话缓存式的记忆机制无法满足连续性、治理性、多模态证据支持、遗忘与纠错、策略演化边界控制以及边缘-云部署约束等需求。

Method: 提出Mi-Memory框架，围绕四个角色组织：Structure（MemStack结构化记忆栈）、Expansion（MemSense/MemFuse多模态感知与融合）、Evolution（D²ACCI/E²MEND诊断驱动的策略演化与修正）、Deployment（LiteMem轻量部署）。通过共享审计契约链接四个角色，包括类型化证据负载（保留来源与溯源）、诊断轨迹（定位证据丢失）、策略工件（记忆策略变更显式化）以及门控/回滚记录（约束可接受的演化）。

Result: 在Structure评估中，MemStack在LoCoMo、PersonaMem-V2、LongMemEval上分别达到93.59%、57.24%、87.47%；其他模块提供模块级、初步/内部、迁移可行性或纯设计层面的证据，并明确标注了边界。

Conclusion: Mi-Memory是迈向可审计、证据门控、部署感知的个人AI记忆系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Personal AI is moving beyond chat-only interaction toward continuous services that span phones, cars, homes, wearables, cameras, and tools. In this setting, memory cannot remain a cache of prior conversations. It should serve as a continuity and governance substrate: preserving durable user state, grounding answers in multimodal and device evidence, supporting correction and forgetting, bounding policy evolution, and remaining deployable under latency, cost, privacy, and edge-cloud constraints. This technical report presents Mi-Memory, a lifecycle memory framework for Personal AI organized around four roles: Structure, Expansion, Evolution, and Deployment. A shared audit contract links these roles through four recurring artifact families: typed evidence payloads preserve source identity and provenance, diagnostic traces localize evidence loss across the serving pipeline, strategy artifacts make memory-policy changes explicit, and gate/rollback records bound accepted evolution. MiMemory instantiates the roles through MemStack, MemSense/MemFuse, D$^{2}$ACCI/E$^{2}$MEND, and LiteMem. In controlled-reference Structure evaluations, MemStack reaches 93.59%, 57.24%, and 87.47% on LoCoMo, PersonaMem-V2, and LongMemEval, respectively; other tracks report module-level, preliminary/internal, transfer-feasibility, or design-only evidence with explicit boundaries. MiMemory is a step toward auditable, evidence-gated, and deployment-aware memory systems for Personal AI. Project homepage: https://darwin-agent.github.io/Mi-Memory/ .

</details>


### [176] [Fishing Out Free Riders: Shapley-Based Reward Attribution for Parallel Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18979)
*Wentao Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Yuxuan Cheng,Yuhan Pan,Miao Li,Zhipeng Qiao,Tao Feng,Zhen Tao,Dengji Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Parallel Shapley框架，利用Shapley值将多路径推理中的每条路径视为合作博弈的玩家，通过生成式奖励模型评估路径效用，结合蒙特卡洛采样高效近似计算边际贡献，实现细粒度路径级奖励分配，在数学推理基准上优于现有方法，训练更稳定且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推理方法无法区分各推理路径的独立贡献，许多路径存在冗余、误导甚至有害的问题，但结果级奖励对所有路径赋予相同奖励，导致学习信号模糊、训练不稳定。

Method: 将每条推理路径视为合作博弈中的玩家，使用Shapley值量化其边际贡献；采用生成式奖励模型评估路径效用；通过蒙特卡洛采样实现高效近似计算。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Parallel Shapley优于现有基线方法，训练更稳定且更具可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效"剔除搭便车者"，按比例分配奖励，从而提升LLM的多路径推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at multi-step reasoning, yet current parallel reasoning approaches often fail to distinguish the contributions of individual reasoning paths. Many paths may be redundant, misleading, or even detrimental, but outcome-level rewards assign uniform reward, leading to ambiguous learning signals and unstable training. We propose Parallel Shapley, a reinforcement learning framework that attributes fine-grained, path-level contributions in multi-path reasoning. Treating each path as a player in a cooperative game, we leverage Shapley values to quantify marginal contributions, using a generative reward model to evaluate path utilities and Monte Carlo sampling for efficient approximation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Parallel Shapley outperforms existing baselines while providing more stable and interpretable training. Our framework effectively "fishes out the free riders," assigning reward proportionally and improving multi-path reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [177] [Athena-Brain Technical Report: An Efficient Robot Brain for General Intelligence and Embodied Interactio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18985)
*Jialian Li,Junhong Liu,Yuchen Cao,Weiran Guo,Jiaming Song,Xutao Wang,Yi Zhao,Jiangpin Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Athena-Brain-8B，一个8B参数的大语言模型，通过多阶段后训练（通用SFT、通用RL、具身专家训练、模型融合）在保持通用智能的同时获得强大的具身交互能力，可作为具身智能的端侧大脑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在单一模型中同时满足通用智能和具身智能的需求，要么偏向通用能力，要么偏向具身能力，缺乏一种紧凑型模型能够兼顾两者。

Method: 采用多阶段后训练流水线：通用监督微调（SFT）、通用强化学习（RL）、具身专家训练、模型融合四个阶段依次进行。

Result: 在通用基准上性能与Qwen3-8B相当但响应更简洁；在具身基准上优于同规模模型及多个零样本评估的大规模前沿模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑型语言模型能够有效整合通用智能与具身能力，Athena-Brain-8B展示了在端侧设备上实现高效具身智能的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in language understanding, reasoning, and world knowledge. As embodied agents become increasingly capable, there is a growing demand for compact models that can serve as an on-device brain, preserving the broad general intelligence of LLMs while enabling effective high-level interaction with embodied environments. Existing approaches, however, often prioritize either general-purpose intelligence or specialized embodied capabilities, making it challenging to satisfy both requirements within a single model. We present \textbf{Athena-Brain-8B}, an 8B LLM designed to serve as an on-device brain for embodied intelligence for embodied intelligence. Through a multi-stage post-training pipeline consisting of General Supervised Fine-Tuning, General Reinforcement Learning, Embodied Expert training, and Model Merge, Athena-Brain-8B maintains strong general capabilities while acquiring strong high-level embodied interaction capabilities and generating concise responses for efficient embodied interaction. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of Athena across both general and embodied evaluations. Compared with the corresponding Qwen3-8B thinking model, Athena-Brain-8B achieves comparable performance on general language and reasoning benchmarks while generating substantially shorter responses. On in-domain embodied benchmarks, Athena-Brain-8B consistently outperforms models of similar scale and surpasses several substantially larger frontier models evaluated zero-shot, demonstrating that compact language models can effectively integrate strong general intelligence with embodied capabilities.

</details>


### [178] [Vector-Bench: Can Models Surgically Edit SVG Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19056)
*Yug Aditi Gupta,Prannay Hebbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vector-Bench是一个包含40个SVG修复任务的基准测试，用于评估指令驱动的矢量编辑能力。最强的模型端点仅达到15.0%的完全规范成功率，尽管平均修复进度为43.7%，表明修复进度与规范忠实编辑之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于栅格图像的评估方法容易忽略矢量编辑中"保持未请求部分不变"这一关键能力，因此需要专门针对SVG程序的细粒度评估基准。

Method: 构建了40个SVG修复任务，每个任务包含损坏的SVG程序、视觉指令、隐藏目标程序，平均5.05个标注修复和60.55个保护对象。定义了确定性二值规范奖励，包含属性感知感知容差和语义不变性约束，并引入有效性门控修复进度、UCR等诊断指标。

Result: 评估了34个模型端点（1360次请求），最强端点仅15.0%完全规范成功率，平均修复进度43.7%，表明当前模型在规范忠实编辑方面仍有显著不足。

Conclusion: 修复进度与规范忠实的矢量编辑之间存在本质差异，现有模型在保持未请求结构不变方面表现不足，需要更细粒度的评估方法推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Instruction-based vector editing requires two capabilities: making a requested change and leaving everything else alone. The second is easy to miss when an output is judged only as a raster image. We introduce Vector-Bench, a compact, difficult benchmark of 40 SVG repair tasks. Each task pairs a corrupted SVG program with an author-written visual instruction, a hidden target program, 5.05 annotated repairs on average, and an average of 60.55 protected objects. Instructions describe visible defects without exposing element identifiers, coordinates, color codes, or path data. We define a deterministic binary specification reward: requested repairs use attribute-aware perceptual tolerances, while unrequested rendering- or application-relevant structure must remain semantically unchanged and the result must be a valid SVG. Canonical target equality and stricter source fidelity are retained as diagnostics. Validity-gated repair progress, a near-complete tier, and valid-output Unintended Change Rate (UCR) explain partial outcomes. We evaluate 34 model endpoints (25 listed as open-weight, 5 inexpensive controls, and 4 frontier closed endpoints) over 1360 requests. The strongest endpoint reaches only 15.0% full specification success, despite 43.7% mean repair progress, showing that apparent repair progress and specification-faithful editing remain substantially different. All prompts, outputs, scoring code, costs, and per-task reports are released.

</details>


### [179] [Quality Action Assurance: Multimodal Verification of Examiner Claims in VR OSCEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19063)
*Harry Rogers,Sally Shiels,Ashley Tomlinson,James Thomas,James Aylward,Nathan Gauge,Helen Higham,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Quality Action Assurance (QAA)多模态框架，通过VR日志、视频和演员数据验证考官在虚拟现实儿科OSCE中的评分主张，实现99.2%的角色F1和93.4%的时间对齐，将考官事实正确率从39.2%提升至79.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSCE评分受考官主观性、疲劳和认知偏差影响，现有评分者间信度统计无法解释错误来源，缺乏对考官推理的分析和主张的验证。

Method: QAA框架结合约束时间动作对齐模型（实现动作定位和角色来源归因）与大型语言模型（提取考官主张并与真实记录比对），利用视频、VR日志和演员数据构建真实事件序列。

Result: 5折交叉验证中，Actor F1达99.2%±0.7%，W@16时间对齐达93.4%±1.9%；考官错误检测精度70.0%、召回率76.7%，事实正确性从39.2%提升至79.2%。

Conclusion: QAA能够有效检测考官评分错误，提升OSCE评估的公平性和客观性。

Abstract: Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs) are the gold standard for assessing clinical competence, yet scoring remains vulnerable to examiner subjectivity, fatigue, and cognitive bias. Standard examiner validation via inter-rater statistics lacks explanatory power regarding the source of errors, as it neither analyzes examiner reasoning nor verifies examiner claims against actual events. Thus, we introduce Quality Action Assurance (QAA), a multimodal framework that verifies examiner claims in Virtual Reality (VR) pediatric OSCEs by comparing actions claimed by examiners against the true sequence of events, constructed from video, VR logs, and actor data. QAA combines a constrained temporal action alignment model, which performs action localization and actor source attribution, with a large language model that extracts examiner claims and checks them against the record. Across a 5-fold cross-validation, QAA achieves 99.2% $\pm$ 0.7% Actor F1 and 93.4% $\pm$ 1.9% W@16 for temporal alignment. Overall, QAA detects examiner errors with 70.0% precision and 76.7% recall, improving factual correctness from 39.2% to 79.2%, enabling fairer OSCE assessment.

</details>


### [180] [On the Effectiveness of Pretraining for Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19072)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对图组合优化问题的自监督预训练框架，通过几何对比学习（旋转和轴向反射）迫使模型学习不变的结构表示和全局相对距离分布，显著提升了神经求解器在路由问题（如TSP）上的表现，混合策略在TSP1000上实现了6.57%的路径长度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经求解器在扩展到大规模、高维度的路由问题时性能下降，缺乏有效的归纳偏置来学习问题的不变结构特征，因此需要一种预训练方法让模型学习到问题固有的几何不变性。

Method: 采用自监督对比学习框架，通过几何增强（旋转和轴向反射）生成正负样本对，迫使模型学习在几何变换下保持不变的图结构表示和全局相对距离分布，从而获得更鲁棒的编码器。

Result: 预训练策略在不同规模的问题上均优于非预训练模型。混合策略（旋转+反射）在TSP1000上实现了6.57%的路径长度改进，证明了几何预训练是有效扩展神经求解器到高维实例的重要归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 几何增强的对比预训练为神经组合优化求解器提供了一个有效的归纳偏置，能够显著提升模型在更大规模路由问题上的泛化能力，是一种有前景的扩展神经求解器的策略。

Abstract: This paper introduces a self-supervised pretraining framework for graph combinatorial optimization specifically designed to address the nature of routing problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem. By utilizing graph contrastive learning with geometric augmentations (specifically, rotations and axial reflections) the model is forced to learn invariant structural representations and global relative distance distributions. Results demonstrate that this pretraining strategy outperforms non-pretrained models across various problem scales. Notably, the hybrid strategy (combining rotation and reflection) achieved a 6.57% improvement in tour length for TSP1000, proving that geometric pretraining is an important inductive bias for effectively scaling neural solvers to high-dimensional instances.

</details>


### [181] [Supra Cognitive Modes: A Routed Architecture for Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19096)
*Joshua Tobkin,David Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM)架构，通过冻结的语义分类器和运行时门控，将查询动态路由到不同的检索与合成模式（词汇/密集查找、图/多跳推理、分层长文合成），在一个共享的摄入基质上运行。在三个基准测试（LoCoMo、MAB、LongMemEval）上取得了有竞争力的结果，并公开了可复现的实现和故障分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆工作负载混合了直接事实查找、关系链推理、当前状态推理以及对长历史信息的广泛综合。现有系统难以用一种统一的检索策略高效应对这些异构查询模式，需要一种能够根据查询类型自适应切换检索与合成策略的架构。

Method: SCM架构包含三个核心组件：(1) 冻结的语义分类器，用于显式或自动选择每查询模式；(2) 运行时门控，将查询分派到融合词汇与密集查找、图或多跳迭代处理、分层长文合成三种路径之一；(3) 共享摄入基质，整合多粒度嵌入、抽取三元组、事实版本元数据和可选的异步增强信息。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上，事实类准确率84.87%，对抗性弃权68.61%；MAB上两轮重复实验平均61.49%；LongMemEval上86.00%。提供了基于仓库的可复现实现及任务/模式条件化的失败分析。

Conclusion: SCM架构通过路由机制将异构查询映射到专用检索合成路径，在多个智能体记忆基准上表现良好。但因果路由效果、效率提升和统计显著性尚未在现有证据中得到验证，原始基线输出、端到端时序和完整token记录也未公开。

Abstract: Agent-memory workloads mix direct factual lookup, relation-chain and current-state reasoning, and broad synthesis over long histories. We describe Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM), an architecture that maps explicit or automatically selected per-query modes to retrieval and synthesis payloads over one shared ingest substrate. A frozen semantic classifier and runtime gates dispatch queries among fused lexical and dense lookup, graph or iterative multi-hop handling, and stratified long-form synthesis. The substrate combines multi-granularity embeddings, extracted triples, fact-version metadata, and optional asynchronous enrichments. We characterize the deployed configuration on three benchmarks: Long-term Conversational Memory (LoCoMo; n = 1,986), MemoryAgentBench (MAB; n = 3,671), and LongMemEval (n = 500). The reference run records 84.87% on LoCoMo factoid categories and 68.61% on adversarial abstention, 61.49% on MAB across two repetitions, and 86.00% on LongMemEval. A repository-backed reproduction produces similar aggregate scores and supports task- and mode-conditioned failure analysis. Raw baseline outputs, aligned end-to-end timing for LoCoMo and LongMemEval, and complete token ledgers are unavailable; stored rows also omit some final runtime decisions. The results characterize one implemented routed configuration and its diagnostic failure patterns, while source inspection verifies the per-query control interface and shared-substrate design. Causal routing effects, efficiency gains, and statistical significance remain outside the available evidence.

</details>


### [182] [OpenRTAG: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robust Text-Attributed Graph Learning under Data Quality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19108)
*Yuze Dai,Zhihan Zhang,Yan Zhao,Ruoyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRTAG，一个针对文本属性图（TAG）学习鲁棒性的基准测试。该基准将TAG质量问题统一为3*3分类体系（稀疏性、噪声、不平衡性×文本、结构、标签三个维度），涵盖九种退化场景，并在九个TAG数据集和三个下游任务上系统评估了多种模型（传统GNN、LLM-GNN、图基础模型）的鲁棒性，填补了TAG鲁棒性研究缺乏统一评估平台的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的文本属性图常存在质量缺陷（稀疏性、噪声、不平衡性），这些问题在文本、结构、标签三个维度上表现出九种退化场景，严重影响TAG学习效果。现有研究针对特定退化类型提出了缓解策略，但证据分散在不同的退化类型、数据集、任务和模型家族中，缺乏对TAG鲁棒性的系统性理解和统一评估平台。

Method: OpenRTAG构建了一个统一的3×3分类体系来组织TAG质量问题，支持在九个TAG数据集和三个下游任务上进行标准化评估；系统性地评估场景有效性和模型敏感性，比较传统GNN、LLM-GNN和代表性图基础模型（GFM）的性能；研究场景匹配基线方法的有效性、效率和鲁棒性；并进一步考察模型在复合退化场景下的行为表现。

Result: OpenRTAG提供了一个标准化的测试平台，可以在现实低质量设置下系统理解TAG学习的鲁棒性。该基准通过统一的分类体系、多样化的数据集和任务、以及全面的模型评估，揭示了不同模型家族在各种退化场景下的鲁棒性差异。

Conclusion: OpenRTAG填补了TAG鲁棒性研究缺乏统一基准的空白，其标准化评估框架有助于推动该领域的研究进展，为开发更鲁棒的TAG学习方法提供指导。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are an important graph data form that combine relational structure with rich node text. However, real-world TAGs are often imperfect, with quality issues arising from text, structure, and labels, and typically manifesting as sparsity, noise, and imbalance. These dimensions define nine representative degradation scenarios that can substantially affect TAG learning. Although prior studies have explored specific mitigation strategies, existing evidence remains fragmented across degradation types, datasets, tasks, and model families, leaving TAG robustness insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we present OpenRTAG, a robustness benchmark for text-attributed graph learning. OpenRTAG organizes TAG quality issues into a unified 3 * 3 taxonomy and supports standardized evaluation across nine TAG datasets and three downstream tasks. It systematically evaluates scenario validity and model sensitivity, compares traditional GNNs, LLM-GNNs, and a representative GFM, investigates the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of scenario-matched baselines, and further examines model behavior under composite degradation scenarios. OpenRTAG provides a standardized testbed for understanding robustness in TAG learning under realistic low-quality settings.

</details>


### [183] [Comparative Study of Multi-Agent Actor-Critic Algorithms in Parameterized Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19117)
*Ubayd Ali Bapoo,Clement N Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出共享经验的多智能体扩展框架（MAGAC、MASAC、MATQC），将单智能体参数化动作强化学习算法扩展到多智能体场景，并在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上评估其性能与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的参数化动作强化学习算法（GAC、SAC、TQC）在单智能体场景中表现优异，但尚未被系统地扩展到多智能体环境。

Method: 采用共享经验的多智能体框架：各智能体维护独立的策略网络和价值网络，但共享同一个经验回放缓冲区，并在此框架下实现GAC、SAC和TQC的多智能体版本（MAGAC、MASAC、MATQC）。

Result: 多智能体框架显著提升了GAC的性能；MASAC和MATQC的提升相对有限；智能体数量超过5个后性能增益递减，计算成本显著增加，尤其MAGAC最为明显。

Conclusion: 共享经验的多智能体actor-critic方法在参数化动作强化学习中存在学习性能与计算效率之间的权衡，为可扩展性研究提供了启示。

Abstract: Parameterized action reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in environments requiring both discrete action selection and continuous parameterization. Prior work established the effectiveness of single-agent actor-critic algorithms - Greedy Actor-Critic (GAC), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC) - on benchmark parameterized action tasks, but their extension to multi-agent settings remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of shared-experience multi-agent extensions of these algorithms: Multi-Agent Greedy Actor-Critic (MAGAC), Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC), and Multi-Agent Truncated Quantile Critics (MATQC). Rather than following the centralized training, decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm, the proposed framework uses multiple independent actor-critic agents that share a replay buffer while maintaining separate policy and value networks. We evaluate the algorithms on the Platform-v0 and Goal-v0 benchmarks against their single-agent counterparts, using three-, five-, and ten-agent configurations to assess scalability. Performance is measured by average evaluation return and training time across ten independent runs, with one-way ANOVA and Tukey HSD post-hoc tests used to assess statistical significance. Results show that the multi-agent framework consistently improves Greedy Actor-Critic performance, while MASAC and MATQC show comparatively modest gains over their single-agent versions. Increasing the number of agents beyond five yields limited additional performance while substantially raising computational cost, particularly for MAGAC. These results highlight a trade-off between learning performance and computational efficiency, offering insight into the scalability of shared-experience multi-agent actor-critic methods for parameterized action reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [184] [BioSecBench-Surveillance: A Verifiable Benchmark for AI Agents in Pathogen Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19262)
*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准测试，包含100个评估任务，用于测试AI代理从原始测序数据和监测背景中推断正确分析流程的能力。最强配置（Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅达到约50.2%的正确率，表明当前AI代理在基因组监测分析中尚不能被完全信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着病原体基因组监测规模的扩大，瓶颈已从数据生成转向数据分析。需要一个标准化的可验证基准来评估AI代理是否能够像人类分析师一样，仅凭原始测序数据和监测背景信息推断出正确的分析流程。

Method: 构建了包含100个评估任务的BioSecBench-Surveillance基准，覆盖七个类别（从分类学分类到基因工程检测），涉及多样化的样本类型和测序技术。每个任务仅提供数据与背景信息，然后确定性评分结构化的答案。对16个模型-工具组合进行了3,962次可评分的尝试。

Result: 最强配置Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex均仅达到50.2%的正确率。即使代理调用了正确的工作流，错误仍来自相关参数选择（如参考基因组、阈值、过滤器和归一化方法的选择）。

Conclusion: BioSecBench-Surveillance为衡量AI代理在下一次疫情暴发时是否能被信任执行基因组监测分析提供了标准化基准，但目前最佳模型的表现仍远未达到可靠水平。

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [185] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过建立博弈模型，揭示了不完美的LLM检测器会如何扭曲用户使用LLM的激励，导致反直觉的结果：检测可能增加LLM使用量，降低输出质量，但能降低检测属性（呈现先升后降模式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM检测工具被广泛用于识别AI生成内容，需要理解这些不完美的检测器作为干预手段对下游指标（LLM使用量、输出质量）产生的非预期影响。

Method: 建立了一个风格化的博弈模型，刻画用户策略性地选择LLM使用程度以及后处理内容以降低检测属性的行为。

Result: 1）LLM检测可能反直觉地导致人类增加LLM使用量；2）即使降低检测属性有助于提升输出质量，引入检测器仍可能使用户产出更低质量的内容；3）检测属性呈现清晰的"先升后降"模式，该模式在arXiv摘要的词频数据中得到实证复现。

Conclusion: LLM检测会扭曲LLM使用和输出质量，揭示了当LLM检测器作为下游指标干预手段运作时存在的失败模式。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [186] [ResearchArena: Evaluating Sabotage and Monitoring in Automated AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19321)
*Lena Libon,Ben Rank,Jehyeok Yeon,David Schmotz,Jeremy Qin,Daniel Donnelly,Derck Prinzhorn,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI控制方法用于自动化AI研发中的安全评估，通过ResearchArena框架在四个长时任务上评估，发现隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难检测，监控器即使能运行实验也不足以完全捕获破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体开始自动化AI研发，需要一种方法评估其输出是否安全部署，即使智能体本身不可信。现有方法缺乏对AI研发场景中隐蔽破坏行为的系统性评估。

Method: 提出AI控制方法，将智能体视为潜在对手并使用监控器检测隐蔽破坏。构建ResearchArena框架，涵盖安全后训练、能力后训练、CUDA内核优化和推理服务器优化四个任务。每个任务配有两种隐藏侧任务：嵌入式侧任务（破坏提交的模型/适配器/内核/服务器）和独立侧任务（在沙箱中执行隐蔽越界操作）。评估前沿智能体在破坏和监控两方面的表现，使用四种沿两个轴变化的监控器类型：是否执行和探测产出制品，以及是否查看智能体的思维链。

Result: 隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难捕获，标记率不足一半。允许监控器对制品运行实验比仅读取轨迹更有帮助，但仍不足：监控器因仅检查表面、注意到异常但将其解释为合理、或使用错误的测试探测制品而遗漏嵌入式破坏。

Conclusion: AI控制是自动化AI研发安全评估的一种有前景的方法，但当前监控能力存在局限，尤其是对训练数据中隐藏的破坏。ResearchArena作为模块化框架发布，用于评估自动化AI研发中的破坏和控制。

Abstract: As AI agents begin to automate AI R&D, we need ways to assess whether their outputs are safe to deploy, even when the agents themselves may be untrusted. AI control offers one such approach: rather than trusting the agent, it treats it as a potential adversary and uses a monitor to detect covert sabotage before deployment. We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [187] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个深度神经网络模型来模拟视觉效价加工的联想情绪学习，该模型包含视觉编码模块和情绪效价识别模块，在巴甫洛夫学习范式下再现了人类联想学习的多项行为与神经特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习方面存在局限，尤其是应用于神经数据时。深度神经网络提供了新的建模途径。

Method: 构建了一个由视觉模块（编码复杂自然场景）和情绪效价识别模块组成的深度神经网络模型，并在该模型上测试了一种新颖的巴甫洛夫学习范式。

Result: 模型再现了人类联想学习研究的多个观察结果，包括联想形成和泛化；条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单单元和神经群体水平上逐渐对齐；模型与人类实验数据的比较验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络模型结合适当的学习算法，可以用于模拟联想情绪/效价学习的行为和神经特征。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [188] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型驱动的智能体系统（Agentic Systems）的教程综述，涵盖其从研究原型到生产部署的转变，重点讨论推理与规划、多智能体协调、评估等关键挑战，并通过制药发现和金融系统的案例研究，分析成功设计模式及故障缓解策略（如验证流水线、回退机制、人在回路监督），为跨行业的安全可靠部署提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学术界已在基准测试和算法创新方面取得进展，但智能体系统在大规模生产部署中面临鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性等新挑战，亟需系统性的设计模式与缓解策略指导。

Method: 通过教程形式整合学术研究与工业实践，分析制药发现和金融系统两个领域的应用案例，提炼通用设计模式，并总结验证流水线、回退机制、人在回路监督等故障缓解策略。

Result: 提供了智能体系统成功的设计模式、评估检查清单以及跨行业安全可靠部署的模板。

Conclusion: 智能体系统从研究到生产的转变过程中，需重点关注鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性，通过合理的设计模式和缓解策略可实现跨行业的安全可靠部署。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [189] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对编码智能体在失败后如何根据执行反馈进行恢复路由决策（廉价重试 vs. 升级到强模型），提出了一个监督路由器+保形风险控制（CRC）层的方法，在控制成本的同时提升求解率。实验表明，该方法在五个编码基准上优于固定策略和二元级联基线，在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设定下，以35%的平均恢复成本达到了超过始终升级策略的求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常采用级联决策（先试廉价模型，失败后升级到强模型），但编码场景中执行失败后的反馈信息可能使得廉价模型的再次尝试仍然有价值。因此需要回答：何时应该让智能体花更多廉价计算资源进行恢复，何时应该直接升级到更强（更贵）的模型？

Method: 将失败后决策形式化为异构动作上的恢复路由问题；从执行轨迹中收集数据训练一个监督路由器；引入保形风险控制（Conformal Risk Control, CRC）层，无需重新训练即可在部署时根据预算选择成本惩罚，并在可交换性假设下提供边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个编码基准的保留失败案例上，廉价恢复与升级表现出互补的成功模式；校准后的前沿面优于固定动作、仅提示路由器及二元级联基线；在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设定下，一个CRC校准的前沿点以35%的平均恢复成本达到了超过始终升级策略的求解率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的监督路由器结合CRC层的方法能够有效在编码智能体的失败恢复中权衡成本与求解率，为预算受限场景下的智能体部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Binding Drift in Multi-Step Tool-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18316)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Shashank Indukuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究工具增强型语言模型代理在多步工作流中的实体绑定问题，发现实体锁定（固定首次绑定）会使错误放大3.0倍，而基于LLM的重新验证器可减少79%的错误，接近理想上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现代理在单步动作中有24-26%的实体绑定错误，但多步场景下实体绑定如何演变尚不明确：绑定是否会保持正确、无声漂移到不同实体，或错误从起始步骤传播并累积。

Method: 形式化定义了绑定漂移（步骤1正确后变错）与错误传播（步骤1错误持续向前）两个概念，并在分离的工作流集合上分别评分以避免混淆。构建了包含200个工作流、580个实体绑定评分步骤、8个模型后端（从小型到前沿）的多步测试平台。

Result: (1) 实体锁定使错误动作从907增加到2,746（3.0倍）；(2) 受影响最大的模型（Claude Opus 4.5）放大达8.5倍；(3) 基于LLM的重新验证器（单次廉价第二模型调用）减少79%错误，接近理想上界（0.20x vs 0.21x）；(4) 自然（非注入）场景下基线代理在18%的工作流上发生漂移，逐步骤错误率递增。

Conclusion: 持久化（实体锁定）与重新验证不可互换：消除漂移的防御可能恶化传播，而实用的重新验证器能接近理想恢复效果。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language-model agents execute multi-step workflows over external systems, resolving an entity once and then acting on it across subsequent steps. Prior work shows that in single-step actions, agents select the correct tool but bind it to the wrong entity 24-26% of the time. We study what happens to entity bindings over time: do they stay correct, silently drift to a different entity, or, if wrong from the start, propagate and compound? We formalize binding drift (correct at step 1, wrong later) as distinct from error propagation (wrong at step 1, carried forward), and score them on disjoint workflow sets so the two cannot be conflated. In a controlled multi-step testbed (200 workflows, 580 entity-binding-scored steps, four enterprise domains, eight model backends spanning small to frontier), we find: (1) under controlled error injection, an entity lock (the intuitive "persist the first binding" fix) amplifies wrong actions from 907 to 2,746 (3.0x; bootstrap 95% CI [2.8, 3.3]), because it faithfully carries the seeded wrong entity into every later step; (2) the amplification reaches 8.5x on the most affected model (Claude Opus 4.5); (3) a practical LLM-based re-verifier (a single cheap second model call re-reading the original instruction) reduces wrong actions by 79% (0.21x; CI [0.18, 0.25]), closing the gap to within 1 percentage point of an oracle upper-bound (0.20x); and (4) in the natural (non-injected) setting, baseline agents drift on 18% of eligible workflows, with the per-step error rate rising across steps. Persistence and re-verification are not interchangeable: a defense that eliminates drift can worsen propagation, and a practical re-verifier nearly matches oracle recovery.

</details>


### [191] [A Decision-Centered Reference Architecture for Trustworthy Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18347)
*Dimitrios S. Sfiris*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个协议无关的电商代理架构，通过规范信封、哈希保护、Ed25519/HMAC认证、七轴生成声明门控和11个语义不变量，确保在代理电商中商业资格、行为者权限、结账有效性等关键状态的一致性和安全性。实验验证了依赖变更检测、过时决策阻断等效果，但未证明规则完备性或生产安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议虽标准化了外部交互，但商户缺乏统一的权威表示来管理商业资格、行为者权限、结账有效性、支付调度、生成声明和证据，亟需一个协议无关的架构来统一处理这些核心问题。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，构建了一个协议无关的架构，核心包括：规范信封（canonical envelope）、受保护的依赖和结果哈希、Ed25519或HMAC认证、实时请求重新绑定、七轴生成声明门控、执行时依赖重新验证以及11个语义不变量。评估使用开源JavaScript实现，8个确定性电商场景和5个受控消融实验。

Result: 7个初始有效操作被允许；受保护状态变更后，未重新决策的操作均被阻止；敌对访问器案例保持阻断；操作状态在配置的表面绑定信封间一致；每个场景包含三个受保护哈希和目标依赖引用；每个消融实验在绕过某个防护时产生预期的不安全回归，而受保护路径则包含相同失败；敌对访问器仅被读取一次；全部66/66测试、模式验证和提交示例通过。

Conclusion: 该架构支持受保护依赖变更检测、有界结果推导、过时决策预防、表面绑定状态一致性、拒绝传播以及合成夹具下的验证状态身份识别，但未建立规则完备性、生产安全性、性能、法律合规性、实时互操作性、总体错误率或独立复现。

Abstract: Agentic commerce extends agentic shopping into software agents that interpret policy, prepare checkout, generate transaction-facing language, and act under delegated payment authority. Protocols standardize external exchanges, but merchants still need one authoritative representation of commercial eligibility, actor authority, checkout validity, payment dispatch, generated claims, and evidence. This design-science study presents a protocol-agnostic architecture built around a canonical envelope, protected dependency and result hashes, Ed25519 or HMAC authentication, live-request rebinding, a seven-axis generated-claim gate, execution-time dependency revalidation, and eleven semantic invariants. Evaluation used an open-source JavaScript implementation, eight deterministic ecommerce scenarios, and five controlled ablations. Seven initially valid actions were permitted. After protected state changed, none could proceed without a fresh decision; a hostile-accessor case also remained blocked. Action status was consistent across configured surface-bound envelopes, and each scenario contained the three protected hashes and its targeted dependency reference. Each ablation produced the predicted unsafe regression when one safeguard was bypassed, while the protected path contained the same failure. The hostile accessor was read once, and the suite passed 66/66 tests, schema validation, and committed examples. Results support protected-dependency change detection, bounded outcome derivation, stale-decision prevention, surface-bound status consistency, refusal propagation, and verified-state identity under synthetic fixtures, but do not establish rule completeness, production security, performance, legal compliance, live interoperability, population error rates, or independent replication.

</details>


### [192] [CODENS: Transforming Code Changes into Living, Accessible, and Queryable Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18356)
*Abdelhak Kelious,Chyrine Tahri,Eliot Bardet*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CODENS是一个将Pull Request转化为可查询、可演进的软件知识图谱系统，支持仓库级问答，并在生产Ruby on Rails项目上验证了其答案相关性和 grounding 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速迭代的代码仓库中，设计知识分散在源文件和Pull Request中，导致代码文档难以保持最新，开发者难以获取全面、结构化的设计知识。

Method: CODENS从Pull Request增量构建带类型的软件知识图谱；通过模式驱动的语义抽取丰富组件；推导组件间的类型化关系；提供三种检索模式（包括基于agent引导的图遍历实现仓库级问答）；保留跨PR的语义变更历史；结合答案质量与操作可观测性指标进行评估。

Result: 在客户Ruby on Rails生产项目上评估，CODENS能生成高度相关且 grounded 的答案；定性反馈显示在简洁、面向文档的摘要合成方面仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: CODENS有效将Pull Request转化为活文档，但文档化导向的简洁摘要生成仍需改进。

Abstract: Maintaining up-to-date code documentation is difficult in fast-moving repositories because design knowledge is scattered across source files and pull requests. We present CODENS , a system that turns pull requests into living, accessible, and queryable documentation for production codebases. CODENS incrementally builds a typed software knowledge graph from pull requests, enriches components through schema-driven semantic extraction, derives typed relations between them, and exposes the resulting knowledge through three retrieval modes, including agent-guided graph traversal for repository-level question answering. The system also preserves semantic change history across pull requests and integrates both answer-quality and operational evaluation metrics. We evaluate CODENS on a client Ruby on Rails project in production. Results show that CODENS produces highly relevant and well-grounded answers, while qualitative feedback highlights a remaining challenge in concise, documentation-oriented synthesis.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Resolved Rate: A Non-Functional Quality Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18462)
*Xin Sun,Daniel Ståhl,Kristian Sandahl,Christoph Kessler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究比较了不同代际的Claude和DeepSeek模型在SWE-bench Lite基准上生成补丁的非功能质量（静态分析、CPU时间、峰值内存），发现虽然新模型解决了更多实例，但在共同解决的实例上，非功能指标没有一致性的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级编码基准主要关注模型解决率（功能正确性）的提升，忽略了不同代际模型生成补丁的非功能质量是否也有改善。本研究旨在填补这一空白，全面评估模型的实际软件工程能力。

Method: 对四个Claude和DeepSeek模型在SWE-bench Lite上开展两项案例研究。使用相同的SWE-agent功能性修复设置，通过CodeQL、CodeScene、CPU时间和峰值内存评估生成的补丁。主要分析聚焦于模型共同解决的实例。

Result: 静态分析显示大多数CodeQL配对差异为零，且所有CodeQL和CodeScene比较经Holm校正后均不显著。CPU时间差异小且跨模型族不一致，峰值内存使用上后模型略高但绝对差异小。个别CodeQL规则和CodeScene类别差异未通过多重比较校正。

Conclusion: 后模型解决了更多实例，但在共同解决的任务上，测量的非功能指标没有一致性的改进。呼吁对模型实际软件工程能力进行更全面的评估。

Abstract: Repository-level coding benchmarks typically measure progress in model capability by comparing the resolved rates of later and earlier models. However, this focus overlooks whether the non-functional quality of their generated patches has also changed across model generations. This study investigates whether later models produce functionally correct patches with better non-functional characteristics than earlier models on comparable repository-level repair tasks. We conducted two case studies involving four Claude and DeepSeek models on SWE-bench Lite. Using the same SWE-agent functional repair setting, we evaluated the generated patches with CodeQL, CodeScene, CPU time, and peak memory. Our primary analysis compared the models on commonly resolved instances. The static analysis results showed that most CodeQL paired differences were zero and that no CodeQL or CodeScene comparison remained significant after Holm correction. CPU time differences were small and inconsistent across model families, while peak memory usage was slightly higher for the later models under the benchmark test workload, with small absolute differences. Differences in individual CodeQL rules and CodeScene categories varied across model families and did not survive multiple-comparison correction. Overall, later models resolved more instances but showed no consistent improvement in the measured non-functional indicators on tasks solved by both models. Through this study, we hope to encourage a more comprehensive evaluation of models' practical software engineering capabilities.

</details>


### [194] [Semantic Drift in Bug Resolution: How Behavioral Signals Propagate from Reports to Tests and Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18550)
*Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo,Yinghua Li,Xueqi Dang,Paweł Borsukiewicz,Liang Xiao,Lingfeng Bao,Anil Koyuncu,Jacques Klein,David Lo,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Desc2Fix提出了一种量化bug报告、触发测试和开发者补丁之间语义对齐程度的框架，通过结构化行为锚点、确定性相似度指标和LLM判断来实现。对2,857个三元组的分析表明，LLM能可靠提取结构化信号（完整度达90%），但对齐结果高度依赖表示形式，且LLM相比人类存在系统性乐观偏差（1-2分），仅具中等排序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bug修复是一个跨工件的流程（自然语言报告→测试→补丁），但不同工件之间行为信号的保真度缺乏系统性的量化方法。

Method: 提出Desc2Fix框架：(1) 结构化行为锚点（重现步骤、API/异常线索、预期vs实际行为）；(2) 确定性相似度指标（ROUGE、SBERT、CodeBERT、OpenAI嵌入）；(3) 基于LLM的判断（覆盖度、正确性、特异性）。分析Defects4J和SWT-Bench的2,857个报告-测试-补丁三元组，使用GPT-4o和DeepSeek-Chat。

Result: LLM提取结构化信号完整度高达90%，跨模型一致性良好；但对齐高度依赖表示形式：词法相似度不足，完整diff最稳定，结构化摘要牺牲表面相似性换取谓词级显著性。LLM相比人类存在1-2分的系统性乐观偏差，排序一致性仅中等。

Conclusion: 行为对齐是可测量的但不能简化为相似度；结构化锚点结合嵌入代理可提供可复现的信号，用于排序和过滤测试与候选补丁，从而推动更可靠的测试生成、语义感知的故障定位、原则性的补丁排序和改进的bug报告编写。

Abstract: Bug resolution is a cross-artifact process: natural-language reports must expose actionable behavioral cues that can be reproduced by tests and corrected by patches. Yet the extent to which these signals are preserved across artifacts remains largely unquantified. We introduce Desc2Fix, a framework for measuring semantic alignment between bug reports, triggering tests, and developer-written fixes. Alignment is operationalized through structured behavioral anchors (e.g., reproduction steps, API/exception cues, expected vs. actual behavior), deterministic similarity metrics (ROUGE, SBERT, CodeBERT, OpenAI embeddings), and LLM-based judgments grounded in coverage, correctness, and specificity. We analyze 2,857 report-test-patch triplets from Defects4J and SWT-Bench using GPT-4o and DeepSeek-Chat. LLMs reliably extract structured signals (up to 90% completeness) and exhibit strong cross-model consistency, yielding a stable semantic input contract for downstream reasoning. However, alignment is highly representation-sensitive: lexical similarity alone is insufficient; full diffs provide the most stable basis for judging report-patch correspondence; and structured summaries trade surface similarity for predicate-level salience. Across more than 182,000 LLM alignment ratings, both models show systematic optimism relative to humans (1-2 points on 5-point scales) and only modest rank agreement, motivating bias-aware evaluation. Our results demonstrate that behavioral alignment is measurable but not reducible to similarity, and that structured anchors combined with embedding-based proxies provide reproducible signals for ranking and filtering tests and candidate patches. By transforming alignment into a controllable engineering signal, Desc2Fix enables more reliable test generation, semantics-aware fault localization, principled patch ranking, and improved bug report authoring.

</details>


### [195] [LM2Alloy: Investigating LLM-Generated Formal Specifications for Automated Test Derivation in Production Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18555)
*Tasmim Rashid,Muhammad Zubair Malik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索使用大语言模型（LLMs）从需求文档和生产代码生成Alloy形式化规约，并从中派生可执行测试用例。在两个开源Python库（Flipper和Cerberus）上的实验表明，LLM能够生成可用的Alloy规约和可执行测试而无需手动修正。其中，在Flipper库中，该流水线发现了一个现有测试套件遗漏的真实缺陷（重复flag名称被静默接受），而直接跳过Alloy步骤的LLM基线（68%分支覆盖率）未能捕获该bug。对于Cerberus，代码派生的规约捕获了文档派生的规约忽略的隐式抽象，生成了额外的测试。代码规约的测试生成方差低于文档规约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用LLM从自然语言文档和源代码生成形式化规约（Alloy），并基于规约自动生成测试用例，以发现传统测试方法难以捕捉的约束级缺陷，解决形式化规约编写成本高、难以自动化的痛点。

Method: 使用LLM从README文档和Python源代码分别生成Alloy形式化规约，再基于Alloy规约生成可执行测试用例。在Flipper（特性标记管理）和Cerberus（数据验证）两个开源库上评估，并与跳过Alloy步骤、直接基于README生成测试的基线方法进行比较。

Result: 1) LLM为两个库均生成了可用的Alloy规约和测试，无需手动修正。2) 在Flipper上发现了一个真实bug（重复flag名被静默接受，违反文档约束），而直接测试基线（68%分支覆盖率）未能检测到。3) Cerberus代码派生规约捕获了文档派生规约遗漏的隐式抽象，多生成了2个测试。4) 代码规约的测试生成方差（SD=2.15）低于文档规约（SD=5.0）。

Conclusion: 引入形式化中间表示（Alloy规约）能够发现覆盖率导向的测试生成可能遗漏的约束级缺陷。代码派生的规约在测试生成的稳定性上优于文档派生规约（但泛化性待验证）。LLM辅助的形式化方法在自动化软件验证中具有潜力。

Abstract: We present an exploratory study on using Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate Alloy formal specifications from both requirements documentation and production source code, and to derive executable test cases from those specifications. We evaluate on two real open-source Python libraries: Flipper, a feature flag management system, and Cerberus, a data validation library.
  In both cases, the LLM produced workable Alloy specifications and executable tests without any manual correction. For Flipper, our pipeline uncovered a genuine bug that the existing test suite had missed: the library silently accepts duplicate flag names, directly contradicting its documented uniqueness requirement. A direct LLM baseline--generating tests from the same README but skipping the Alloy step--achieved 68% branch coverage yet failed to catch this bug across all three independent runs. This suggests that introducing a formal intermediate representation can surface constraint-level defects that coverage-oriented generation may miss.
  For Cerberus, the code-derived specification captured an implicit abstraction over sized types that the documentation-derived spec omitted, producing two additional tests. Across both libraries, code-based specifications showed lower variance in test generation (mean SD = 2.15) than documentation-based ones (mean SD = 5.0), though whether this generalises remains an open question.
  Index Terms--formal specifications, Alloy, large language models, automated testing, specification drift, software validation.

</details>


### [196] [Provably Lossless Acceleration of DNN Mutation Testing via Memoization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18582)
*Ali Ghanbari,Ben Greenman,Sasan Tavakkol,Shibbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Mure，第一个通过记忆化技术实现深度神经网络突变测试的无损加速框架。Mure利用突变模型与原始模型共享大量冗余计算的特性，只执行突变后的后缀部分并复用原始模型的前缀计算结果，在保证结果完全等价的条件下，平均降低44.54%的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突变分析在深度神经网络测试中虽被公认为评估测试集充分性的有效方法，但其计算开销极高。现有加速技术采用有损近似，以牺牲突变分数准确性换取效率提升。因此，需要一种既能保证结果准确性又能显著加速DNN突变测试的方法。

Method: Mure基于记忆化思想：DNN突变体与原始模型在前向传播中共享大量计算。具体而言，模型可被切割为前缀和后缀两部分，原始模型的前缀计算结果被缓存；每个突变体仅需从突变点开始执行后缀部分，前缀结果直接从缓存中复用，从而避免重复计算。作者给出了形式化证明，论证了该方法的soundness（结果等价于穷举测试）以及加速条件。

Result: 在15个不同架构、复杂度（参数从数千到数百万）的DNN模型上，Mure平均降低44.54%的突变测试计算成本。对比之下，最先进的（有损）技术虽平均加速可达88.97%，但会引入突变分数误差。当突变神经元比例高达5%时，Mure仍能提供超过20%的加速。

Conclusion: Mure是首个可证明无损的DNN突变测试加速框架，通过记忆化在保证结果完全等价的前提下实现显著加速。实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性，表明即使在较高突变率下仍能保持可观的加速收益。

Abstract: Mutation analysis has recently reemerged in the context of deep neural networks (DNNs) as a promising, but notoriously costly, approach for assessing test dataset adequacy. Existing techniques speed up DNN mutation testing through lossy approximations that trade efficiency for mutation score accuracy. This paper introduces Mure, the first provably lossless framework for accelerating DNN mutation testing via memoization. Mure is based on the idea that DNN mutants and the original model share substantial redundant computation, so during mutation testing, it executes only the mutated suffixes of each mutant and reuses the common prefix from the original model, which is computed only once. We give a formal account of memoized mutation testing, and prove that Mure is sound, i.e., it produces results equivalent to exhaustive vanilla mutation testing, and identify basic conditions under which speed-up is guaranteed. We have implemented Mure and evaluated it on 15 DNN models of various architectures, complexities, and sizes ranging from a few thousands to millions of parameters. This provides empirical evidence that Mure reduces the computational cost of mutation testing by 44.54%, on average. We also observed that while state-of-the-art techniques tend to yield higher acceleration (up to 88.97%, on average), they come at the cost of some error in mutation score. We further analyze the effect of mutation generation selection ratio on the effectiveness of Mure and observed predictable reductions in memoization opportunities with increasing the percentage of mutated neurons. We observed that Mure offers more than 20% speed-up even when as high as 5% of the neurons are mutated.

</details>


### [197] [LLM-Based Invariant Testing for Software Functional Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18711)
*Ruogu Yang,Yifeng He,Yundi Xu,Yuqing Wei,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LISA是一个基于LLM的自动化不变性测试框架，通过迭代生成API序列和程序不变性、利用API n-gram反馈指导，有效检测软件库中的功能性缺陷，无需人工编写测试用例。实验表明，LISA在缺陷检测率和代码覆盖率上均优于传统模糊测试和现有LLM测试生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动编写单元测试耗时且需深入理解API语义；启发式测试生成方法无法像人类一样推理程序语义或理解源码与文档；传统模糊测试依赖崩溃检测缺陷，但功能性缺陷不一定会引起崩溃。现有方法难以有效检测功能性缺陷。

Method: LISA框架：基于LLM迭代生成API序列和程序不变性，以API n-gram反馈作为指导信号，逐步优化测试生成，最终输出高置信度的缺陷候选报告供开发者确认。

Result: 相比模糊测试和现有LLM测试生成方法，LISA实现了更高的缺陷检测率和具有竞争力的代码覆盖率；每个发现均作为高置信度缺陷候选报告输出。

Conclusion: LISA通过结合LLM的语义理解能力与n-gram反馈机制，有效解决了功能性缺陷检测难题，为自动化软件测试提供了新范式。

Abstract: Manually writing unit tests to uncover functional bugs in software libraries is not only time-consuming but also requires a deep understanding of the intended semantics of the APIs. Heuristic-based test generation methods suffer from low usability because they cannot reason about program semantics or interpret source code and documentation as humans do. Traditional fuzzing techniques like OSS-Fuzz often rely on crashes to detect bugs, but functional bugs do not always cause crashes. To overcome these limitations, we present LISA, a novel LLM-based invariant testing framework for software functional bugs. LISA iteratively generates API sequences and program invariants guided by API n-gram feedback, achieving higher bug-detection rates and competitive code coverage compared with both fuzzing and prior LLM-based test generation approaches, and reporting each finding as a high-confidence bug candidate for developer confirmation.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Text Editing: Algebraic Manipulation of Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18742)
*Kevin Pulo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出源代码代数（Source Code Algebra）的新方法，用逻辑代数操作序列替代纯文本编辑，使每次操作对应完整的语义变更。初步实验表明，LLM代理使用该方法完成跨文件代码修改时，成功率更高且token消耗降低1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯文本编辑与代码语法语义要求之间存在不匹配，导致编程效率低下；在LLM编程代理时代，这种不匹配尤为突出——代理需将高层变更计划分散为底层文本编辑，频繁重读大量代码，成本高昂。

Method: 提出源代码代数方法，将代码库修改建模为一系列逻辑代数操作序列。每个操作执行单一语义变更所需的全部修改，类比数学方程重写。定义了操作的组合性、零幂性和交换性等属性，并实现了概念验证系统SCAS。

Result: LLM代理使用源代码代数完成非局部、跨文件代码变更时，成功率更高，且token消耗比基于文本的基线方法降低1-2个数量级。

Conclusion: 让LLM输出代数操作而非重写代码是代码编辑的有前景方向，未来工作可包括构建全面操作库、研究形式化属性及开发面向人类的工具。

Abstract: Source code is almost universally edited as plain text. However, the mismatch between the syntactic and semantic requirements of valid and correct code, and the unconstrained text editing process trying to produce it, introduces friction that degrades the programming task. It is also increasingly costly in the era of LLM-based coding agents, which must materialize their high-level plan of intended changes as low-level text edits dispersed throughout the codebase, often requiring them to re-read large portions of code.
  We propose the novel alternative approach of source code algebra, where the codebase is modified by applying to it a sequence of logical algebraic operations. Each operation makes the full set of changes necessary for a single semantic change, analogous to mathematical equation rewriting.
