We sketch initial properties of such operations - including composition, nullipotency, and commutativity - that distinguish this approach from text editing, and make it well-suited as a substrate for agentic code editing.
  A feasibility probe with our proof-of-concept implementation (SCAS) suggests that LLM agents can use source code algebra to complete a non-local, cross-file code change with both higher success rates and one to two orders of magnitude fewer tokens, relative to text-based baselines. While preliminary, this is consistent with the hypothesis that having LLMs emit algebraic operations, rather than rewritten code, is a promising direction for code editing - and motivates broader future research into source code algebra, such as comprehensive operator libraries, formal properties, and human-facing tooling.

</details>


### [199] [TrapHunter: Exposing Covert Pathways in Trap Token Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18753)
*Yin Wu,Yixuan Liu,Yi Li,Chenyang Peng,Hao Wu,Ming Fan,Ting Liu,Haijun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统化分类了代币合约中的"陷阱代币"问题，提出了TrapHunter框架，通过意图偏差分析（结合抽象行为树ABT、增强路径图APG和大语言模型）检测严格遵循标准协议但嵌入欺诈逻辑的恶意合约，在269个真实合约上平均精确率81.8%、召回率85.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者利用标准化代币合约（如ERC-20）的合规外表隐藏恶意逻辑，这种"欺骗性合规"策略使得传统检测工具难以区分合规合约与陷阱合约，亟需一种能够揭示隐藏欺诈路径的检测方法。

Method: 提出TrapHunter框架：(1) 提出基于代币功能生命周期（生成、流通、持久化、观察）的陷阱分类体系；(2) 构建统一语义表示，结合抽象行为树（ABTs）规范化过程内语法，结合增强路径图（APGs）揭示过程间状态依赖驱动的隐藏执行路径；(3) 利用LLM推理偏离参考实现的语义意图，并通过基于分叉的动态验证确认可利用性。

Result: 在269个真实合约上使用三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini）进行评估，TrapHunter能够检测全部六类陷阱，平均精确率81.8%、召回率85.4%，显著优于现有工具。

Conclusion: TrapHunter通过结合静态分析（ABT+APG）与LLM语义推理及动态验证，有效弥合了语法合规与语义欺诈之间的鸿沟，为检测隐藏恶意逻辑的标准化代币合约提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Standardized token contracts (e.g., ERC-20) form the foundation of digital assets. However, attackers increasingly abuse this standardization to disguise malicious trap tokens. Unlike obvious violations, these contracts employ a strategy of "deceptive adherence": they strictly adhere to standard protocols to evade detection while embedding covert logic to defraud users. To address this, we first systematize the trap landscape by proposing a novel taxonomy derived from the intrinsic functional lifecycle of tokens (Generation, Circulation, Persistence, and Observation). We then propose TrapHunter, a framework designed to identify these traps and expose covert pathways within these deceptive contracts via intent deviation analysis. Specifically, TrapHunter introduces a unified semantic representation combining Abstract Behavior Trees (ABTs) and Augmented Path Graphs (APGs) to normalize intra-procedural syntax and reveal hidden execution paths driven by inter-procedural state dependencies. Crucially, it bridges the semantic gap by leveraging LLMs to reason about the behavioral intent of deviations from reference implementations, followed by fork-based dynamic validation to confirm exploitability. Experimental evaluation on 269 real-world contracts with three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini) demonstrates that TrapHunter effectively detects all six categories of traps, achieving an average precision of 81.8% and recall of 85.4%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art tools.

</details>


### [200] [TraceDev: A Traceability-Driven Multi-agent Framework for Requirement-to-Code Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18886)
*Mingyu Chen,Yakun Zhang,Zihao Xie,Yixing Luo,Jinrui Xu,Cuiyun Gao,Kaiqi Zhao,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceDev是一个基于用例的多智能体框架，通过五个角色智能体和异构可追溯性图实现从自然语言需求到仓库级代码的端到端自动生成，在ETOUR和SMOS数据集上分别达到53.63%和56.82%的成功率，超越基线方法最高达340.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖简化指令（如单句描述），无法反映复杂软件开发场景，且缺乏明确的需求可追溯性机制，难以精确对齐和验证生成代码与原始需求。

Method: 提出TraceDev多智能体框架，包含需求精炼者、设计者、开发者、测试者和验证者五个角色智能体。验证者智能体构建并维护一个异构可追溯性图，链接需求、设计模型和代码工件，用于与其他四个智能体交互，保持各工件间的一致性，并作为结构化上下文实现高效内存管理，支持可靠的仓库级代码生成。

Result: 在ETOUR数据集上成功率达53.63%，超越基线方法最高186.63%；在SMOS数据集上成功率达56.82%，超越基线方法最高340.80%。

Conclusion: TraceDev在从需求到仓库级代码生成任务中展现出有效性，通过可追溯性图机制显著提升了代码生成的成功率。

Abstract: In modern software development, the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made the end-to-end transformation of Natural Language Requirements (NLRs) into executable repository-level code increasingly feasible. However, existing approaches typically rely on simplified instructions (e.g., single-sentence descriptions), failing to reflect complex software development scenarios. Moreover, they lack explicit requirement traceability mechanisms, making it difficult to precisely align and validate generated code against original requirements. To address these limitations, we propose TraceDev, a multi-agent framework for automated software development grounded in use cases that contain multiple functional points and complex semantics. TraceDev employs five role-specific agents, including a Requirement Refiner, Designer, Developer, Tester, and Validator. Notably, the Validator Agent constructs and maintains a heterogeneous traceability graph that links requirements, design models, and code artifacts for interacting with the preceding four agents. The traceability graph maintains consistency across various artifacts and serves as a structured context for efficient memory management, supporting reliable repository-level code generation. We evaluate TraceDev on two widely used datasets (including 125 use cases) compared with two state-of-the-art approaches. On the ETOUR dataset, TraceDev achieves a success rate of 53.63\%, outperforming baseline approaches by up to 186.63\%. A similar trend is observed on the SMOS dataset, where TraceDev attains a success rate of 56.82\%, exceeding baseline approaches by up to 340.80\%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of TraceDev in repository-level code generation from requirements.

</details>


### [201] [Skillware: A Software Ontology and Engineering Lifecycle for Persistent Behavioral Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18970)
*Haodi Fan,Zucong Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Skillware软件抽象概念，将AI Agent系统中的持久化行为工件（Skills）定义为独立的软件对象，通过独立身份、生命周期和Agent Host执行关系来管理这些工件，并基于大量实证数据验证了该ontology的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究描述了Agent Skills的规范、执行、维护和演化，但缺乏将这些工件定义为独立软件对象的本体论，无法支持其作为可识别、可组合、可维护、可演化的软件工件进行工程化管理。

Method: 定义Skillware抽象层：Skill Artifact（指定可复用任务行为）→ Skillware Unit（管理工件的独立身份与生命周期）→ Agent Host（运行时解释执行）。提出三个必要条件（行为首要性、独立软件身份、Agent Host执行关系）和Lifecycle Continuity属性。结合Agent Skills规范、138,133条SKILL.md记录、20,556个仓库ID、15个边界案例和13个工程实现进行实证验证。

Result: 验证了以下发现：存在可重复出现的工件信封、可分离的软件身份、兼容的执行路径以及生命周期工程压力。这些证据支持Skillware作为Agent能力的软件本体论和工程生命周期的有效性。

Conclusion: Skillware提供了软件本体论和工程生命周期，使Agent能力能够成为可识别、可组合、可维护和可演化的软件工件，填补了Agent Skills领域缺少独立软件对象定义的空白。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become persistent behavioral artifacts across independent AI agent systems. They combine natural-language task specifications with metadata and optional references, scripts, assets, hooks, package manifests, tests, and companion interfaces. Existing studies explain how Skills are specified, executed, maintained, and evolved, but lack an ontology that defines these artifacts as independent software objects. This paper introduces Skillware as the software abstraction that extends software engineering to persistent Behavioral Artifacts in agent systems. A Skill Artifact specifies reusable task behavior; a Skillware Unit manages that artifact as software through an independent identity and lifecycle. A compatible Agent Host activates the unit for runtime interpretation. Three necessary conditions operationalize category membership: behavioral primacy, independent software identity, and an Agent Host execution relationship. Lifecycle Continuity records whether the same unit identity persists through update, maintenance, rollback, and removal as a separate software-grade property. Evidence combines the Agent Skills specification, a frozen corpus of 138,133 content-deduplicated SKILL.md records associated with 20,556 repository identifiers, independent empirical studies, 15 category-boundary cases, and 13 fixed-revision engineering implementations. The evidence establishes a recurring artifact envelope, separable software identities, compatible execution paths, and lifecycle engineering pressure. Skillware provides the software ontology and engineering lifecycle through which agent capabilities can become identifiable, composable, maintainable, and evolvable software artifacts.

</details>


### [202] [From Collaboration to Regulation: Characterizing Governance Practice in Three Deep Learning Open Source Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19022)
*Ruiqiao Qiu,Wenhao Yang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对PyTorch、TensorFlow和Paddle三个成熟深度学习框架的混合方法实证研究，分析了109份治理文档中的17个规则主题，追踪了1700多次提交，识别出四种治理功能，并提炼出33条可操作的治理实践，为大型开源软件项目的规则设计提供系统指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件项目面临协作和质量控制的挑战，但维护者缺乏系统性的指导来制定、引入、修订和组织治理规则。

Method: 混合方法实证研究。基于制度分析与发展（IAD）框架，分析三个成熟深度学习框架（PyTorch、TensorFlow、Paddle）的109份治理文档，识别17个规则主题；追踪1700多次提交分析规则演变模式。

Result: 识别出17个规则主题（分属7种规则类型）；操作层规则（如工作流）出现更早、修订更频繁，结构层规则（如角色层级）出现较晚、变化较少；规则内容逐渐向任务/角色专属文件专业化；识别出四种治理功能：规范对齐、工作流细化、协调结构化、社区与治理发展；提炼出33条可操作治理实践。

Conclusion: 为大型成熟开源项目提供了基于实证的规则设计指南，揭示了不同规则类型的时间演变规律和专业化趋势，帮助维护者更系统地管理治理文档。

Abstract: Collaboration in Open Source Software (OSS) projects creates substantial coordination and quality-control challenges across diverse contributor bases. Projects address these challenges through documented governance rules, yet maintainers have limited systematic guidance on what rules to codify, when to introduce or revise them, and how to organize them across documents. We conducted a mixed-methods empirical study of three mature deep learning frameworks: PyTorch, TensorFlow, and Paddle. Using the Institutional Analysis and Development framework, we analyzed 109 documents and identified 17 rule themes across seven rule types. Operational rules, such as workflows, appeared across all three projects, whereas structural rules, such as role hierarchies, varied more substantially. Tracing more than 1,700 commits, we found that operational rule themes generally appeared earlier and were revised more frequently, while many structural rule themes emerged later and changed less often. Rule-bearing content also became increasingly specialized across task- and role-specific files. We further identified four governance functions reflected in substantive rule changes: Norm Alignment, Workflow Refinement, Coordination Structuring, and Community and Governance Development. Synthesizing these findings, we derive 33 actionable governance practices for mature, large-scale OSS projects with substantial coordination demands and organizational involvement.

</details>


### [203] [SciCodePile: A 128GB Corpus and Executable Benchmark for Challenging Scientific Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19104)
*Weifeng Sun,Ye Fan,Yuchen Chen,Gou Tan,Jieke Shi,Yuan Yidi,Swee Liang Wong,Jonathan Pan,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciCodePile——迄今最大的科学代码语料库（128GB，来自37,737个公开仓库），并从中构建了包含200个可执行任务的基准测试。评估15个LLM后发现，科学代码生成极具挑战性：最强的模型在可执行基准上的Pass@1仅12.30%，而基于该语料库的继续预训练和指令微调分别将CodeBLEU提升2.84倍、Pass@1提升4.79倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在通用代码生成上表现出色，但科学代码生成能力尚不明确。现有数据集和基准在规模、领域覆盖或可执行验证上存在局限，无法充分评估当前LLM与可靠科学代码生成器之间的真实差距。

Method: 从37,737个公开仓库构建128GB的科学代码语料库SciCodePile，覆盖多学科；进一步从中筛选200个任务构建可执行基准，每个任务配备沙箱执行环境和自动化测试框架；评估15个开源和闭源LLM在三种任务（前缀补全、中间填充、可执行代码生成）上的表现。

Result: 最佳CodeBLEU在两种补全任务上分别仅为38.13和38.37；最强模型在可执行基准上的Pass@1仅12.30%。基于SciCodePile的继续预训练使科学代码补全CodeBLEU提升2.84倍，指令微调使可执行基准Pass@1提升4.79倍。

Conclusion: 科学代码生成对当前LLM仍极具挑战性，现有模型远未达到可靠水平；同时，大规模科学代码语料库对提升LLM科学代码生成能力具有显著训练价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at general-purpose code generation, yet how well they handle scientific code remains an open question. Existing datasets and benchmarks are limited in scale, domain coverage, or executable verification, leaving the true gap between current LLMs and reliable scientific code generators inadequately assessed. To address these limitations, we present SciCodePile, the largest scientific code corpus to date, constructed from 37,737 public repositories and collectively comprising 128GB of code that spans multiple computational science disciplines. From this corpus, we further curate an executable benchmark of 200 tasks, each equipped with a sandboxed execution environment and an automated test harness for functional verification. We evaluate 15 LLMs from both open-source and closed-source families on three tasks: prefix-to-suffix completion, fill-in-the-middle infilling, and executable code generation. Results show that scientific code generation remains highly challenging: The best CodeBLEU reaches only 38.13 and 38.37 on the two completion tasks, while the strongest model achieves just 12.30\% Pass@1 on the executable benchmark, underscoring how far current models remain from reliable scientific code generation. To demonstrate the training utility of SciCodePile, we further show that continued pretraining on our corpus improves CodeBLEU by $\times$2.84 on scientific code completion, and instruction tuning on our data improves Pass@1 by $\times$4.79 on the executable benchmark. All code and data are available at https://huggingface.co/SciCodePile.

</details>


### [204] [EmbeddedKittens: An Evaluation of Code Embeddings for Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19291)
*Benedikt Fein,Gordon Fraser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索了四种大型语言模型和五种代码嵌入方法在Scratch程序学习分析中的应用，通过标记预测和分类任务实验，证明代码嵌入可以迁移到教育环境中，无需额外微调即可预测学生程序的功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然代码嵌入在机器学习应用中越来越普遍，但哪种嵌入方法最适合编程教育中的学习分析仍是开放问题。特别是视觉化块编程语言（如Scratch）无法直接应用基于自然语言处理的大语言模型方法，需要探索专用嵌入模型是否能通过捕获额外结构信息来提升性能。

Method: 实例化了四种LLM和五种不同的Scratch程序嵌入方法，创建了一个标记预测任务和两个不同的分类任务及对应数据集，并在这些任务上对模型进行了实证评估。

Result: 实验表明，代码嵌入可以成功迁移到Scratch教育环境中。在大规模公开Scratch数据集上训练的嵌入模型能够捕获相关的结构和语义信息，使得在典型的小规模课堂场景中无需任务特定的模型微调，即可实现学习分析（如预测学生程序的功能正确性）。

Conclusion: 代码嵌入方法对块编程语言的教育环境（Scratch）是可行的，且无需额外微调即可支持学习分析任务。

Abstract: The trend of embedding source code for machine learning applications also enables new opportunities in learning analytics in programming education, but which code embedding approach is most suitable for learning analytics remains an open question. A common approach to embedding source code lies in treating the code as a token sequence similar to natural language when training large language models~(LLMs). However, in case of visual block-based programming languages like Scratch, this approach cannot be applied directly. While text-based representations of block-based code can be created to apply LLMs to this problem, other dedicated embedding models could potentially exhibit improved performance by capturing additional structural information. In this paper, we therefore instantiate four LLMs and five different popular embedding approaches for Scratch programs, create a token-prediction and two different classification tasks with corresponding datasets, and empirically evaluate the models on them. Our experiments demonstrate that a transfer of code embeddings to the educational environment of Scratch is feasible. The embedding models trained on large open Scratch datasets capture relevant structural and semantic information about the code to enable learning analytics like predicting functional correctness of student programs, in the typically small classroom setting without requiring further task-specific model fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Topology-Aware Tokenization for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18600)
*Yaokun Liu,Yifan Liu,Zhenrui Yue,Gyuseok Lee,Zelin Li,Ruichen Yao,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式推荐中物品分词导致的拓扑失真问题，提出了一种拓扑感知分词框架TopoTok，通过多层级蒸馏方案（组间、组内、物品间）在不同粒度上逐步恢复物品关系结构，实验表明该方法有效缓解了拓扑失真，在Recall@5上最高提升9.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐范式在物品分词过程中，预训练语义嵌入空间中物品的邻接关系在量化后遭到显著破坏，这种拓扑失真误导了模型对物品相似性的感知，成为生成式推荐准确性的瓶颈。

Method: 提出TopoTok框架，引入多层级蒸馏方案：1) 组间蒸馏（Inter-Group Distillation）捕获全局聚类间关系；2) 组内蒸馏（Intra-Group Distillation）细化语义聚类内的局部结构；3) 物品间蒸馏（Inter-Item Distillation）在单个物品层面进行细粒度对齐，从而从粗到细逐步恢复拓扑结构。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，TopoTok有效缓解了拓扑失真，持续优于现有最优分词器，在Recall@5上取得高达9.42%的性能提升。

Conclusion: TopoTok通过多层级蒸馏机制在不同粒度上恢复物品关系结构，有效解决了生成式推荐中物品分词导致的拓扑失真问题，显著提升了推荐准确性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates sequential recommendation as an autoregressive generation task, yet a critical issue in this paradigm remains overlooked: topology distortion in item tokenization. In particular, we observe that the intrinsic adjacency relationships of items in the pretrained semantic embedding space are significantly disrupted after quantization. This topology distortion misleads the model's perception of item similarity, ultimately bottlenecking the accuracy of generative recommendations. To address this issue, we propose Topology-Aware Tokenization (TopoTok), an item tokenization framework that preserves item relational structure throughout the quantization hierarchy. Different from the prior monolithic supervision in tokenization, TopoTok introduces a multi-level distillation scheme to progressively recover the topology from coarse to fine granularity: 1) Inter-Group Distillation to capture global cluster-wise relations; 2) Intra-Group Distillation to refine local structures within semantic clusters; and 3) Inter-Item Distillation to enforce fine-grained alignment at the individual item level. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that TopoTok effectively alleviates topology distortion and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art tokenizers, achieving significant performance gains of up to 9.42% in Recall@5.

</details>


### [206] [AutoIndex: Learning Representation Programs for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18603)
*Sam O'Nuallain,Nithya Rajkumar,Ramya Narayanasamy,Hanna Jiang,Shreyas Chaudhari,Andrew Drozdov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AutoIndex是一个自动学习文档表示程序的框架，通过搜索可执行的文档变换操作（切片、丰富、归一化、重加权、重组等）来优化索引构建，在BM25检索器固定不变的情况下，在8个异构检索任务上平均提升Recall@100 +8.4%和nDCG@10 +8.3%，最高分别提升+30.5%和+43.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档表示不应被视为检索前固定的预处理选择，而应作为显式的优化目标。传统方法仅调优检索器、重排序器或少量超参数，忽略了文档表示本身的优化空间。

Method: 提出AutoIndex框架，通过验证引导的程序搜索（validation-guided program search），智能体诊断当前程序的失败案例并综合候选更新，仅保留能提升检索质量的更新。搜索空间包括切片、丰富、归一化、重加权、重组等可执行变换操作。

Result: 在CRUMB基准测试的8个异构检索任务上，固定BM25不变，AutoIndex相比静态全文BM25基线在全部任务上提升Recall@100和nDCG@10，平均提升+8.4%和+8.3%，最大提升+30.5%和+43.6%。

Conclusion: 文档表示应作为检索系统的显式优化目标而非固定预处理步骤，AutoIndex通过程序搜索能有效自动发现最优的文档表示策略。

Abstract: We present AutoIndex, a framework for learning representation programs: executable transformations that map raw documents into the representations exposed to a retrieval system. Rather than tuning retrievers, rerankers, or a small set of preprocessing hyperparameters, AutoIndex searches over programs that slice, enrich, normalize, reweight, or reorganize documents before indexing. At each iteration, AutoIndex performs validation-guided program search, in which agents diagnose failures of the current program and synthesize candidate updates, retaining only updates that improve retrieval quality under the resulting index. We evaluate AutoIndex on CRUMB, a benchmark of heterogeneous retrieval tasks, with BM25 held fixed across all experiments. The learned programs improve recall over a static full-document BM25 baseline on all 8 tasks, with average gains of +8.4% in Recall@100 and +8.3% in nDCG@10, and largest gains of +30.5% in Recall@100 and +43.6% in nDCG@10. These results suggest that document representation should not be treated as a fixed preprocessing choice made before retrieval begins, but as an explicit optimization target. Code to reproduce our results is available at https://github.com/auto-index/autoindex.

</details>


### [207] [Mitigating Matthew Effect: Multi-Hypergraph Boosted Multi-Interest Self-Supervised Learning for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18609)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ruilin Xu,Guohua Wang,Liang Lin,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HiCore框架，通过多超图增强的多兴趣自监督学习来缓解对话推荐系统中的马太效应，在四个数据集上达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多在静态或准静态场景下缓解马太效应，但在动态用户-系统反馈循环的对话推荐系统中，马太效应更为显著，亟需有效解决方案。

Method: 构建多级超图（物品、实体、词导向的多通道超图）来学习多级别用户兴趣，结合自监督学习，在动态对话推荐场景中缓解马太效应。

Result: 在四个基于CRS的数据集上达到新的最先进性能，有效缓解了马太效应。

Conclusion: HiCore通过多超图和多兴趣自监督学习，成功在动态对话推荐场景中缓解马太效应，为对话推荐系统的公平性提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Matthew effect is a big challenge in Recommender Systems (RSs), where popular items tend to receive increasing attention, while less popular ones are often overlooked, perpetuating existing disparities. Although many existing methods attempt to mitigate Matthew effect in the static or quasi-static recommendation scenarios, such issue will be more pronounced as users engage with the system over time. To this end, we propose a novel framework, Multi-Hypergraph Boosted Multi-Interest Self-Supervised Learning for Conversational Recommendation (HiCore), aiming to address Matthew effect in the Conversational Recommender System (CRS) involving the dynamic user-system feedback loop. It devotes to learn multi-level user interests by building a set of hypergraphs (i.e., item-, entity-, word-oriented multiple-channel hypergraphs) to alleviate the Matthew effec. Extensive experiments on four CRS-based datasets showcase that HiCore attains a new state-of-the-art performance, underscoring its superiority in mitigating the Matthew effect effectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/zysensmile/HiCore.

</details>


### [208] [PLAID-PRF: Pseudo-Relevance Feedback with Centroid-like Tokens in PLAID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18626)
*Xiao Wang,Sean MacAvaney,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PLAID-PRF，一种在PLAID（基于ColBERT的多向量密集检索模型）上执行伪相关反馈（PRF）的方法。该方法利用PLAID内部的质心向量，选择少量高效益的扩展向量附加到原始查询中，重新运行PLAID以优化候选生成和最终评分。在MSMARCO和四个BEIR基准测试上，PLAID-PRF相比PLAID在nDCG@10上提升高达4.3%，在MRR@10上提升高达7.3%，同时计算开销远低于先前的PRF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRF方法在多向量检索模型上计算成本高，且缺乏轻量级、高效的反馈感知机制来改进后期交互检索的顶部结果质量。

Method: 基于PLAID的质心向量，将PRF中的扩展向量选择类比于传统PRF中的词元选择，选择小型且多样化的高效益扩展向量，将其附加到原始查询后重新运行PLAID进行候选生成和最终评分。

Result: 在MSMARCO和四个BEIR基准上，PLAID-PRF相比PLAID在nDCG@10上提升4.3%，MRR@10上提升7.3%，计算开销低于先前PRF方法。

Conclusion: 提出的质心感知PRF方法是一种有效且轻量级的机制，能够在不进行昂贵的查询时文档-词元聚类的前提下，提升顶部检索结果的质量。

Abstract: Multi-vector dense retrieval models, such as ColBERT, achieve strong retrieval effectiveness by modelling fine-grained token-level interactions between queries and documents. Methods such as PLAID use centroid-based quantisation of each token's vector to reduce the index size and speed up retrieval while maintaining strong effectiveness. In this work, we introduce PLAID-PRF, a method that performs Pseudo-Relevance Feedback (PRF) over PLAID to reformulate ColBERT's query vectors based on the top-retrieved results. In contrast with prior methods that perform PRF on multi-vector retrieval models, PLAID-PRF keeps computational costs low by leveraging the internal PLAID centroid vectors, treating them similarly to tokens in traditional PRF methods. The method selects a small and diverse set of high-utility expansion vectors and appends them to the original query, rerunning PLAID to refine both candidate generation and final scoring. Extensive experiments on the standard in-domain MSMARCO and four out-of-domain BEIR benchmarks show that PLAID-PRF consistently improves retrieval effectiveness over various baselines. In particular, PLAID-PRF improves over PLAID by up to 4.3% nDCG@10 and 7.3% MRR@10, while introducing substantially less computation overhead than prior PRF methods. The results demonstrate that our proposed centroid-aware PRF method offers an effective and lightweight mechanism to improve the quality of top-ranked retrieved results. Overall, this work enables effective and efficient feedback-aware late-interaction retrieval without expensive query-time document-token clustering.

</details>


### [209] [An Epistemic Position-Based Click Model: From Interactions to Epistemic Distributions of Relevance and Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18712)
*Oscar Rolando Ramirez Milian,Harrie Oosterhuis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于证据深度学习的位置点击模型，能够对相关性和位置偏差参数输出Beta分布，捕获认知不确定性，并通过近似和条件化技术解决优化稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点击模型仅提供频率学派点估计，无法捕捉认知不确定性，导致无法评估预测的置信度。

Method: 采用证据深度学习框架，输入物品和位置特征，对位置点击模型中的每个相关性和位置偏差变量输出Beta分布；提出近似和条件化技术确保数值稳定性和方差缩减。

Result: 模型能够在未见数据上捕获认知不确定性，而标准策略梯度方法无法学习到有意义的分布。

Conclusion: 首次将贝叶斯不确定性引入点击建模，为基于位置的点击模型提供了认知层面的替代方案，是融入不确定性量化的重要步骤。

Abstract: User interactions with rankings are affected by both items' relevances and display positions. Accordingly, click probabilities are often modeled as a product of relevance and position factors; and for improving recommendation and search, one needs to disentangle relevance from position bias. However, existing click models only provide frequentist point-estimates that do not capture any measure of epistemic uncertainty. Consequently, there is no indication of how much confidence one should have in their predictions. In this work, we introduce the first evidential deep-learning approach to form an epistemic alternative to the important position-based click model. Our learned model takes as input item and position features and outputs a beta-distribution for every relevance and position-bias variable of the position-based model. These distributions capture epistemic uncertainty about click probabilities and the underlying effects of attraction and position bias. The main challenge of our approach is its optimization for which we propose approximation and conditioning techniques to provide numerical stability and variance reduction. Our experiments indicate that our approach captures epistemic uncertainty in predictions on previously-unseen data, whereas standard policy gradients fail to learn meaningful distributions. We believe our contribution of the first contextual epistemic click model constitutes an important step in incorporating Bayesian uncertainty into click modeling.

</details>


### [210] [RAGAL: A Frugal, Fully Local Retrieval-Augmented Assistant for Technical Support at a Government Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18756)
*Dan Musetoiu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RAGAL系统，一个在严格数据本地化约束下（零数据出口、只读权限、单台8GB消费级笔记本电脑）为罗马尼亚农村投资机构AFIR构建的检索增强助手。核心发现包括：检索工程和检索器微调是关键杠杆性能提升从62%到81%；通过微调bge-m3嵌入器，召回率@10从0.663提升至0.850；发现了单领域微调会降低另一领域检索性能的通用陷阱；PII掩码提升生成质量；结构锚点蒸馏消除SQL幻觉；以及用CPU运行744B参数模型作为云评估代理的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构持有大量敏感文件，无法使用云端语言模型，迫切需要一种在严格数据本地化约束下（零数据出口、只读权限、受限硬件）构建高效问答助手的解决方案。

Method: 采用混合稠密-稀疏检索与意图路由（HyDE风格）；微调bge-m3嵌入器（在8GB VRAM内完成，耗时72分钟）；使用GenQ本地生成查询补偿单领域微调导致的检索退化；实施PII掩码；设计结构化锚点蒸馏（anchor distillation）方案使SQL幻觉在构造层面被消除。

Result: 混合检索与意图路由将内部评估从62%提升至81%；微调后召回率@10从0.663提升至0.850，MRR从0.489提升至0.684；发现单领域微调会降低文档领域检索性能，通过GenQ修复；PII掩码反而提升了生成质量；锚点蒸馏完全消除了SQL幻觉。

Conclusion: 在极端资源约束下，检索工程和检索器微调比扩大生成器规模更具性价比；成功构建了完全本地化的RAG助手，并提供了可复现的脚本；验证了CPU运行超大规模模型作为离线评估代理的可行性。

Abstract: Public institutions hold large volumes of sensitive documents and support tickets that cannot leave the premises, ruling out cloud-hosted language models entirely. We report on RAGAL, a retrieval-augmented assistant for the technical-support team of AFIR, the Romanian Agency for Financing Rural Investments, built and operated under three hard constraints: zero data egress (no external API calls, even for synthetic data), a read-only mandate (the assistant drafts, humans execute), and a single 8 GB consumer laptop as the only development and training machine. Over a Romanian-language corpus of ~25,000 chunks -- 15,073 resolved support tickets and internal normative documents -- we show that the highest-leverage investments were retrieval engineering and retriever fine-tuning rather than a larger generator: hybrid dense-sparse retrieval with intent routing raised our internal evaluation from 62% to 81%, and fine-tuning the bge-m3 embedder on real ticket data improved recall@10 from 0.663 to 0.850 (MRR 0.489 to 0.684) after 72 minutes of training. We document a general pitfall: single-domain fine-tuning silently degraded retrieval on the untouched document domain below the stock baseline, detected only after building a per-domain evaluation set and repaired with locally generated queries (GenQ). We report two counter-intuitive findings -- PII masking improved generation quality, and a structural "anchor distillation" scheme made SQL hallucination impossible by construction -- along with a reproducible recipe for full embedder fine-tuning in 8 GB of VRAM. Finally, since zero egress also rules out a cloud judge, we describe a substitute: a 744B-parameter model run on CPU, too slow to serve interactively but affordable in overnight batch, used as a second opinion whose limits we quantify. We release the sanitized pipeline scripts for institutions facing similar data-locality constraints.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Noisy Signals: Dual-Level Denoising for Multi-modal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18786)
*Jie Luo,Qi Jin,Xinming Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DDMSR框架，通过图特征去噪（拉普拉斯平滑）和频谱序列去噪（FFT+可学习滤波器）解决多模态序列推荐中的双重噪声困境（特征冗余与序列随机性），并引入多模态对比对齐，在四个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐方法面临双重噪声困境：（1）特征级冗余——通用预训练表示与细粒度推荐意图之间存在语义鸿沟；（2）序列级随机性——意外点击等虚假交互引入噪声。现有方法未能系统性解决这两个层面的噪声问题。

Method: 提出DDMSR框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）基于图的特征去噪模块——利用拉普拉斯平滑作为结构低通滤波器，在高斯噪声条件下抑制高频语义噪声、保留显著特征；（2）频域序列去噪模块——使用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）和可学习频率滤波器自适应调制交互频谱，衰减异常信号；（3）多模态对比对齐目标——弥合异质性差距，强制跨模态语义一致性。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DDMSR在所有指标上持续优于最先进的基线方法，提供了一种高鲁棒且高效的多模态序列推荐解决方案。

Conclusion: DDMSR通过特征-拓扑和序列-频率双视角的系统性去噪，有效解决了多模态序列推荐中的双重噪声困境，验证了图低通滤波和频谱滤波在推荐去噪中的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal Sequential Recommendation (SR) incorporates rich side information (e.g., textual and visual features) to enhance dynamic user preference modeling. However, existing frameworks inevitably suffer from a \textbf{Dual-Noise Dilemma}: (1) \textit{Feature-level redundancy} stemming from the semantic gap between generic pre-trained representations and fine-grained recommendation intent; and (2) \textit{Sequence-level stochasticity} induced by spurious interactions such as accidental clicks. To break this bottleneck, we propose \textbf{DDMSR}, a novel \textbf{D}ual-level \textbf{D}enoising \textbf{M}ulti-modal \textbf{S}equential \textbf{R}ecommendation framework that systematically purifies signals from both feature-topological and sequence-frequency perspectives. Specifically, we first design a graph-based feature denoising module that leverages Laplacian smoothing on item semantic graphs as a structural low-pass filter, effectively suppressing high-frequency semantic noise while preserving salient features. For sequence purification, we introduce a frequency-domain sequence denoising module, utilizing the Fast Fourier Transform and a learnable frequency filter to adaptively modulate the interaction spectrum and attenuate anomalous signals. Furthermore, a multi-modal contrastive alignment objective is incorporated to bridge the heterogeneity gap and enforce cross-modal semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on four public benchmark datasets demonstrate that DDMSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, providing a highly robust and efficient solution for multi-modal sequential recommendation. The source code is available at: ~\href{https://github.com/jluo00/DDMSR}{\textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/jluo00/DDMSR}}.

</details>


### [212] [TSGR: Taobao Search Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18796)
*Tianyu Zhan,Gui Ling,Tong Xiong,Kunhai Lin,Yang Wang,Kaixuan Zhang,Zhihong Chen,Yuliang Yan,Dan Ou,Shengyu Zhang,Haihong Tang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TSGR提出了一种面向工业电商搜索的价值感知生成式检索框架，通过查询感知并行SID和值感知排序模块，在保持语义匹配能力的同时提升高价值商品的检索优先级，离线HR@1000提升9.16%，在线交易笔数和GMV分别增长1.12%和1.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索系统仅优化语义匹配，对商品商业价值不敏感：SID构建不考虑价值信息，候选排序无法利用物品侧信息，导致高价值商品在检索阶段被忽视或降权，制约了电商搜索中的业务目标。

Method: 1) QP-SID：基于查询-物品统计信息构建并行码本，将查询条件的价值排序编码到SID中；2) VRM：在GR模型上构建并联合优化的值感知排序模块，单一模型同时充当检索器和预排序器；3) 渐进式训练流水线：依次对齐语义相关性、用户偏好和业务目标。

Result: 离线HR@1000提升9.16%；在线A/B测试IPV+0.43%、交易笔数+1.12%、GMV+1.64%。

Conclusion: TSGR通过将价值感知嵌入到生成式检索的表示与排序环节，在不引入额外预排序阶段的前提下有效提升了高价值商品的检索优先级，实现了语义匹配与商业目标的统一优化。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) has demonstrated strong promise for industrial e-commerce search by training a single autoregressive model to directly generate the Semantic IDs (SIDs) of target items. However, existing GR systems are primarily optimized for semantic matching and remain insensitive to item business value: SID construction is value-unaware, and candidates are ranked without access to item side-info. Consequently, high-value items are often missed or deprioritized at the retrieval stage, limiting downstream business impact. This limitation is particularly critical in industrial settings such as Taobao Search, where business objectives are central to system design. To address this, we propose $\textbf{T}$aobao $\textbf{S}$earch $\textbf{G}$enerative $\textbf{R}$etrieval ($\textbf{TSGR}$), a unified generative retrieval framework that incorporates value awareness into both item representation and candidate ranking. 1) For item representation, TSGR introduces $\textbf{Query-aware Parallel SID (QP-SID)}$, which encodes query-conditioned value orderings into the SID construction by building parallel codebooks derived from query-item statistics, so that higher-value and query-relevant items are assigned better token indices. 2) For candidate ranking, we introduce a $\textbf{Value-aware Ranking Module (VRM)}$ that is built upon and jointly optimized with the GR, enabling a single model to seamlessly serve as both retriever and pre-ranker without a dedicated pre-ranking stage. A progressive training pipeline further aligns the model with semantic relevance, user preferences, and business objectives. Offline experiments show that TSGR achieves an 9.16% improvement in HR@1000, and online A/B tests further validate its effectiveness, yielding gains of +0.43% in IPV, +1.12% in Transaction Count, and +1.64% in GMV.

</details>


### [213] [Answer-Reconstruction Search Density: Measuring the Query and Source Work Compressed by Conversational Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18904)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出答案重建搜索密度（ARSD），用于量化对话系统将搜索工作压缩为单一答案的程度，通过测量在固定重建策略下支持目标比例的可检索答案单元所需的最小查询动作数，并引入页面密度指标以区分查询压缩与源压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索指标评估排序、努力或事实支持，但未能量化完成答案所代表的传统搜索工作量。

Method: 定义ARSD为在固定且带日期的重建策略下，支持目标比例的可检索原子答案单元所需的最小不同查询动作数；并行使用页面密度指标以分离查询压缩与源压缩。

Result: 提出ARSD和页面密度两个新指标，分别量化答案重建所需的最小搜索工作和区分查询与源的压缩程度。

Conclusion: ARSD和页面密度指标为评估对话系统对传统搜索工作的压缩效果提供了更全面的量化方法。

Abstract: Conversational systems can collapse a visible sequence of web queries, result inspections, and source comparisons into a single synthesized answer. Existing retrieval metrics evaluate ranking, effort, or factual support, but they do not quantify the minimum conventional search work represented by a completed answer. We define answer-reconstruction search density (\ARSD): the minimum number of distinct query actions required, under a fixed and dated reconstruction policy, to support a target share of atomic retrievable answer units. A parallel page-density measure separates query compression from source compression.

</details>


### [214] [Spectral Biclustering-Driven Scalability for Post-Hoc Explainability in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19189)
*Jose L. Salmeron,Irina Arévalo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于谱双聚类的块删除诊断框架，通过将用户和物品分组并删除整块交互，从而高效实现推荐系统的反事实可解释性分析，在SVD和神经协同过滤模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统的事后可解释性方法面临严重可扩展性问题，逐观测删除诊断虽能提供反事实分析，但随着数据集规模增长计算成本急剧上升，亟需更实用的高效诊断方法。

Method: 利用谱双聚类对用户和物品进行分组，然后删除整块交互而非单个用户或物品，从而大幅减少重训练次数；在SVD和神经协同过滤两种推荐范式上，使用MovieLens和Amazon数据集进行评估。

Result: 排名靠前的推荐对特定交互块的敏感性高于排名靠后的；部分交互块起支持作用，部分则对推荐质量有 detrimental 影响；不同用户段对块删除的敏感性存在异质性。

Conclusion: 块删除诊断提供了一种实用且模型无关的事后分析框架，能够揭示标准推荐指标无法直接反映的诊断信息，但解释结果依赖于所选的块结构。

Abstract: Explainability in recommender systems is essential for ensuring transparency, accountability, and trust, yet existing post-hoc methods often encounter severe scalability challenges. Observation-level deletion diagnostics offer a counterfactual way to analyze recommendations by retraining models after removing individual users or items, but their cost grows rapidly with dataset size. To improve the practical tractability of this analysis, this paper introduces a block-deletion diagnostic framework that uses spectral biclustering to group users and items and then removes entire blocks of interactions. This formulation reduces the number of retraining procedures relative to finer-grained deletion strategies and produces explanations at the level of user segments, item groups, and their interactions. The framework is evaluated on two representative recommender paradigms, Singular Value Decomposition and Neural Collaborative Filtering, using the MovieLens and Amazon datasets. The results show that top-ranked recommendations are often more sensitive to specific interaction blocks than lower-ranked ones, with some blocks acting as supporting evidence and others having a detrimental effect on recommendation quality. The analysis also indicates that user segments differ in their sensitivity to block removal, suggesting heterogeneous levels of reliance on localized interaction patterns. These findings provide diagnostic information that is not directly visible through standard recommendation metrics. Overall, the results suggest that block-deletion diagnostics offer a practical and model-agnostic post-hoc analysis framework for recommender systems, while also highlighting that the resulting explanations depend on the chosen block structure.

</details>


### [215] [PAGE-RAG: Evidence-Grounded Adaptive Graph Retrieval for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19301)
*Xingyu Chen,Junxiu An,Jun Guo,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAGE-RAG是一种投影感知的自适应图检索框架，将图结构视为组织文档知识的语义骨架而非独立知识源，通过任务自适应检索路由和严格知识边界控制，实现长文档问答中检索效率与知识可靠性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG通过引入结构化表示提升了长文档问答能力，但自动构建的图本身是源文档的不完整投影（即存在"投影不完备"问题），将其视为独立知识源会导致不可靠的检索与生成。现有方法忽视了这种投影特性带来的知识边界模糊问题。

Method: 1) 投影感知视角：将图结构视为组织与导航文档知识的语义骨架，而非替代原始知识源；2) 任务自适应检索路由策略：根据查询需求动态选择适当的检索行为；3) 严格知识边界控制：确保生成响应基于可用证据，对超出知识范围的信息予以拒答。

Result: 在长文档问答任务上，PAGE-RAG在保持竞争性答案质量的同时，显著提升了检索效率和知识可靠性，验证了投影感知图建模、自适应检索和显式知识边界控制对于可信GraphRAG系统的重要性。

Conclusion: PAGE-RAG通过重新定义图结构在RAG中的角色（语义骨架而非独立知识源），结合自适应路由与知识边界控制，为构建可信赖的GraphRAG系统提供了有效范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: GraphRAG improves long-document question answering by introducing structured representations beyond conventional retrieval. However, automatically constructed graphs are inherently incomplete projections of source documents, and treating them as independent knowledge sources may lead to unreliable retrieval and generation. We propose PAGE-RAG, a projection-aware adaptive graph retrieval framework for reliable long-document question answering. PAGE-RAG views graph structures as semantic skeletons that organize and navigate document knowledge, rather than replacing the original knowledge source. Based on this perspective, PAGE-RAG introduces a task-adaptive retrieval routing strategy that dynamically selects appropriate retrieval behaviors according to query requirements. Furthermore, PAGE-RAG incorporates strict knowledge boundary control, ensuring that generated responses remain grounded within available evidence and abstaining from unsupported information beyond the accessible knowledge scope. Experiments demonstrate that PAGE-RAG achieves competitive answer quality while improving retrieval efficiency and knowledge reliability, highlighting the importance of projection-aware graph modeling, adaptive retrieval, and explicit knowledge boundary control for trustworthy GraphRAG systems. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/CXY0112/PAGE-RAG.

</details>
